At the single-agent level, untrusted inputs through prompts, memory, retrieved knowledge, and tool interfaces create attack surfaces. In multi-agent settings, delegation and communication introduce challenges related to identity, trust, capability control, and decision transparency, while the underlying model routing and execution control plane remains vulnerable to manipulation and to unverified model provenance. Perhaps the most fundamental challenge is behavioral containment: sequences of individually permissible actions may collectively violate system-level constraints and safety invariants. At the broader level, supply-chain integrity, provenance, accountability, and end-to-end observability remain largely open problems. A common principle unifies these directions: security must become a verifiable property of the architectures, protocols, and runtimes that govern agent behavior, rather than an optional layer of guidance. Charting these challenges provides a roadmap toward trustworthy autonomous agent deployment.

</details>


### [120] [Does the Competitive Component of Adversarial Self-Play Improve Legal Reasoning? A Controlled Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01559)
*Miseog Shawn Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个诚实的负面结果：在法律推理任务中，对抗性自我博弈训练（学生起草论证、对手攻击、基于可验证引用的存活奖励）相对于完全相同的非竞争性训练并没有带来可靠的优势。通过四次独立测试（bootstrap比较、双种子复制、逐案例对抗鲁棒性比较、盲法头对头判断）及一个增强对手的后续实验，竞争组件均未表现出可靠收益（胜率49%和50%，p均约1.000）。作者指出该无效结果与配套编码领域研究结论一致：多教师课程的价值在于构建可验证环境而非竞争本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性自我博弈是法律推理训练中一个吸引人的训练范式——让学生模型起草论证，让对抗者攻击，当论证在攻击中幸存时给予奖励。作者设计了一个可验证的"存活"奖励信号，其中学生引用的权威和对抗者引用的反权威都通过引文验证器检查。然而，作者提出了一个窄但重要的问题：竞争组件本身（对抗者和存活奖励）是否在完全相同的非竞争性训练之上增加了任何价值？这个问题的答案是研究界普遍存在假设但很少有人系统检验的。

Method: 作者设计了一种可验证的"存活"奖励训练信号：学生的引用权威与对抗者的反权威均由引文验证器核查，存活判定基于已验证的事实而非修辞，捏造的引用会被自动中和。为检验竞争组件的边际价值，作者进行了四次独立测试：bootstrap比较、双种子复制、配对逐案例对抗鲁棒性比较、盲法头对头判断，外加一个使用故意增强对抗者的后续试点实验。共有约六项评估（包括初始+29%优势反转的案例）。

Result: 竞争组件未产生可靠的收益：盲法判断给出49%的胜率（二项检验p约1.000）；增强对抗者试点给出50%的胜率（32:32，p约1.000）。最初看似+29%的优势在小样本上反转并证明是伪影。此外发现两个具体陷阱：(1) 一个起初有希望的指标在更多数据上反转；(2) 对抗鲁棒性指标在对抗者停止引用与金答案相同权威时静默退化为纯召回率。

Conclusion: 该无效结果与配套编码领域研究（Kim, 2026）的结论一致：多教师课程的价值源于构建可验证环境而非竞争本身。本文的价值在于可复现性和分享具体陷阱，为法律推理领域以及更广泛的对抗训练研究提供了诚实、方法论严谨的负面证据。

Abstract: Adversarial self-play is an appealing recipe for legal reasoning: have a student model draft an argument, have an adversary attack it, and reward the student when its argument survives the attack. We designed exactly such a training signal -- a verifiable "survival" reward in which both the student's cited authorities and the adversary's counter-authorities are checked by a citation verifier, so that survival is decided on verified grounds rather than rhetoric, and fabricated citations are automatically neutralized. We then asked a narrow but important question: does the competitive component itself -- the adversary and the survival reward -- add anything on top of an otherwise identical non-competitive training run? Across four independent tests -- a bootstrap comparison, a two-seed replication, a paired per-case adversarial-robustness comparison, and a blinded head-to-head judgment of generated arguments, plus a follow-up pilot with a deliberately strengthened self-play adversary -- the competitive component produced no reliable benefit. The blinded judgment gave a 49% win rate (binomial p approx. 1.000); the strengthened-adversary pilot gave a 50% win rate (32:32, p approx. 1.000). An early apparent +29% advantage reversed and proved to be a small-sample artifact. We report this as an honest negative result. The value of the paper is reproducibility and the sharing of concrete pitfalls: an initially promising metric that inverted on more data, and an adversarial-robustness metric that silently collapsed to plain recall once the adversary stopped citing the same authorities as the gold answer. This null is consistent with, and reconfirms in the legal domain, the conclusion of the companion coding-domain study (Kim, 2026, arXiv:2607.08255) that the value of multi-teacher curricula arises from constructing a verifiable environment rather than from competition itself.

</details>


### [121] [Is More Privileged Information Better? From Solution Traces to Problem-Solving Structure in Self-Distilled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01589)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Zhihu Wang,Xu Caiyue,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出问题空间引导的在线策略自蒸馏（PS-OPSD），用轨迹锚定的问题空间指导（初始状态、目标条件、约束和状态转移路径）替代完整参考解作为教师信号，在不修改学生rollout和OPSD目标的情况下，在三个数学推理基准和1.7B-8B模型规模上取得最优的仅问题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）通过特权视角（基于参考解的教师信号）监督仅观测问题的学生视角，但教师提供的token级目标可能依赖推理时不可用的参考特定信息，导致训练与推理之间的信息错配。

Method: PS-OPSD将完整参考解替换为轨迹锚定的指导信息，具体包含：初始状态（initial state）、目标条件（goal conditions）、约束（constraints）以及选定的状态转移路径（state-transition path）。学生的rollout过程与OPSD训练目标保持不变，仅改变教师侧提供信息的表示形式。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准上、模型规模从1.7B到8B的实验中，PS-OPSD取得了所比较方法中最高的聚合仅问题准确率。消融/受控实验表明，指导相关性和路径连贯性对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 特权信息的表示形式是OPSD中的一个重要设计选择，通过将特权信息组织为问题空间的结构化描述（而非完整解），可以更有效地将教师的知识迁移给学生模型。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by using a privileged view of a model conditioned on reference solutions to supervise a student view that observes only the question. However, the teacher-provided token-level targets may depend on reference-specific information unavailable at inference time. We propose Problem-Space-Guided OPSD (PS-OPSD), which replaces the complete solution with trajectory-grounded guidance describing the initial state, goal conditions, constraints, and a selected state-transition path. The student rollout and OPSD objective remain unchanged. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales ranging from 1.7B to 8B, PS-OPSD achieves the highest aggregate question-only accuracy among the compared methods. Controlled experiments further indicate that guidance relevance and path coherence contribute to these gains, highlighting the representation of privileged information as an important design choice in OPSD.

</details>


### [122] [Latent Thought Credit: Multi-Answer Credit Assignment for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01593)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Yong Chen,Wenjia Zeng,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Thought Credit (LTC)，一个用于潜在推理的分层信用分配框架。LTC对每个提示采样多个潜在思维，固定每个思维后的上下文，并通过平均多个答案的奖励来估计思维级别的期望奖励。LTC使用思维级别优势优化潜在思维阶段，答案级别优势优化答案阶段，并采用优势加权思维匹配目标帮助策略复现高信用潜在思维。在数学推理和STEM多选题任务上，LTC取得了最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理允许语言模型在连续潜在表示中进行中间推理，而非完全外化为离散思维链。然而，从仅含答案的奖励中为潜在思维分配信用是困难的，因为单个最终答案混合了思维质量和答案采样噪声，难以区分二者的贡献。

Method: LTC采用分层信用分配框架：(1) 对每个提示采样多个潜在思维；(2) 固定每个思维后的上下文；(3) 通过在该固定上下文中生成多个答案并平均奖励，估计思维级别的期望奖励；(4) 使用思维级别优势优化潜在思维阶段；(5) 使用答案级别优势优化答案阶段；(6) 引入优势加权思维匹配目标，帮助策略复现高信用的潜在思维。该框架以GRPO风格的在线训练方式实现。

Result: 在数学推理和STEM多选题任务上，LTC在对比方法中取得了最佳平均准确率。消融实验和固定上下文诊断表明，多答案估计降低了奖励估计误差，并缓解了模糊或不正确的思维级别信用分配问题。

Conclusion: LTC通过将信用分配拆分为思维层面和答案层面的分层框架，有效解决了潜在推理中从答案奖励反向传播到潜在思维时的信用分配难题。多答案估计策略显著降低了奖励估计噪声，提升了训练的有效性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning allows language models to carry out intermediate reasoning in continuous latent representations rather than fully externalizing it as discrete chains of thought. However, assigning credit to such latent thoughts from answer-only rewards is difficult: a single final answer mixes thought quality with answer-sampling noise. We propose \textbf{Latent Thought Credit (LTC)}, a hierarchical credit-assignment framework for latent reasoning. For each prompt, LTC samples multiple latent thoughts, fixes the context after each thought, and estimates thought-level expected reward by averaging rewards over multiple answers generated from that fixed context. LTC uses thought-level advantages to optimize the latent-thought phase, answer-level advantages to optimize the answer phase, and an advantage-weighted thought-matching objective that helps the policy reproduce high-credit latent thoughts. We instantiate LTC in a GRPO-style on-policy training framework and evaluate it across mathematical reasoning and STEM multiple-choice tasks. LTC achieves the best average accuracy among the compared methods, while ablations and fixed-context diagnostics show that multi-answer estimation reduces reward-estimation error and mitigates ambiguous or incorrect thought-level credit.

</details>


### [123] [When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01619)
*Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateAuditor，一种从存储状态到草稿反向审计的机制，用于修复记忆增强代理中的隐式策略适应（IPA）差距。在STALE基准上严格单查询VTA得分.736，比基线上限+5.0点，增益几乎全部来自IPA和前提抵抗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强代理即使知道用户存储状态已过时，仍会围绕旧值进行规划，这被称为隐式策略适应（IPA）差距。现有草稿锚定验证只检查响应内容，而在开放式响应中过时依赖通常是未说出的，因此无法有效识别和修复过时状态。

Method: StateAuditor采用从存储状态到草稿的反向审计方向：LLM从带时间戳的证据中提出候选的旧到新转换，确定性代码将每个引语固定到单个条目，检查新证据确实更新，且只允许这些已验证的转换触发修复。验证的是来源和时序，而非语义上的取代关系。

Result: 在STALE完整协议（400场景、50会话历史）上，严格单查询VTA得分为.736，对照锁定前代版本.686，配对增益+5.0点（95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]），几乎全部来自IPA和前提抵抗。第三方模型族的基准判断器复现了增益（.738 vs .680）。匹配对照仅得.692（+0.6，不显著），证明增益来自转换机制而非额外上下文。在独立的HorizonBench上，完整管道提升当前偏好准确率（用户聚类p<.01），但对照实验显示大部分外部增益来自草稿侧审计；更难的作者编辑生命周期数据集无增益，限制了主张范围。

Conclusion: StateAuditor通过验证来源和时序而非语义取代，有效缩小了IPA差距。增益主要归因于转换机制本身。作者不声称对通用代理记忆有效，仅针对特定场景提出有界的主张。

Abstract: Memory-augmented agents can know that a user's stored state is outdated and still plan around the old value. The STALE benchmark calls this the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. We identify one structural contributor: draft-anchored verification checks what a response says, and in an open-ended response the stale dependency is usually unsaid. StateAuditor therefore audits in the opposite direction, from stored state to draft. An LLM proposes candidate old-to-new transitions from timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quotation to a single entry, checks that the new evidence really is newer, and lets only these verified transitions trigger repair. What is verified is provenance and chronology - not semantic supersession. On STALE's full protocol (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, one independent response per query), strict single-query VTA scores .736 against .686 for our locked predecessor under the same judge: a +5.0-point paired gain (95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]) coming almost entirely from IPA and premise resistance (PR). The benchmark's own judge, from a third model family, reproduces the gain (.738 vs. .680). On an independent cross-family preference-evolution benchmark (HorizonBench), the full draft-audit-repair pipeline over a gold-derived structured store raises current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01), though a matched control shows most of this external gain is the draft-side audit itself; a harder authored lifecycle set gives no gain, bounding the claim while false invalidation stays controlled. On STALE, by contrast, a matched control (same evidence, adapter, and call budget) scores only .692 (+0.6 over the predecessor, n.s.), attributing the STALE gain to the transition machinery rather than added context or calls. We make no claim about general-purpose agent memory.

</details>


### [124] [Salami Attack: Stealthy Collusive Memory Poisoning against OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01637)
*Zheng Lin,Yuzhe Huang,Zhenxing Niu,Xianmin Ye,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCollusion，一个自动化红队框架，通过"萨拉米策略"将对抗目标切分为多个看似无害的记忆片段，利用良性外观记忆的联合效应来实现协作式记忆投毒攻击，在OpenClaw基准上实现81.3%的记忆保存率和75.0%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆投毒攻击主要依赖单条恶意记录，忽视了组合式威胁：多条看似良性的记忆可能共同诱导不安全行为。这构成了一个被忽略的攻击面，需要系统化的框架来揭示和评估此类风险。

Method: MemCollusion框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 萨拉米策略——将对抗目标切分为个体无害但集体有害的记忆碎片；(2) 四个设计约束用于构建记忆联盟；(3) 五种理论驱动的记忆组合策略；(4) 一个微调的生成器。同时开发了MoltLab，一个Moltbook的受控复现环境，用于在跨会话场景中评估记忆投毒攻击。

Result: 在OpenClaw上使用两种骨干模型、覆盖48个场景进行评估。在最强记忆保存设置下，MemCollusion实现平均Memory Save Rate 81.3%和Attack Success Rate 75.0%，并且在良性记忆稀释和记忆级防御条件下依然保持有效。

Conclusion: 协作式记忆投毒攻击对LLM代理构成了真实且持续的威胁，现有防御措施不足以应对此类组合式攻击，需要针对记忆联盟的新型防御机制。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM agents to retain useful information across sessions, but also creates an attack surface through which adversaries may poison an agent's persistent memory to steer its behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks mainly rely on individually malicious records, overlooking a compositional threat: multiple benign-looking memories may jointly induce unsafe behavior. In this paper, we introduce MemCollusion, an automated red-teaming framework for constructing collusive memory poisoning attacks. MemCollusion applies salami tactics---a strategy that slices an adversarial objective into small, individually innocuous pieces---to generate memory fragments that are individually benign looking but collectively harmful. It constructs memory coalitions using four design constraints, five theory-informed strategies, and a fine-tuned generator. To assess collusive memory poisoning in a realistic cross-session setting, we develop MoltLab, a controlled research reproduction of Moltbook, in which crafted platform content must first be observed and distilled into persistent memory before influencing the agent's behavior in a separate session. We evaluate MemCollusion on OpenClaw using two backbone models across 48 scenarios. Under the strongest memory-saving setting, MemCollusion achieves an average Memory Save Rate of 81.3% and an Attack Success Rate of 75.0%, and remains effective under both benign memory dilution and memory-level defenses.

</details>


### [125] [GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01645)
*Abhinav Pothuri,Zhe Jiang,Zelin Xu,Di Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GISAgentBench，一个包含349个多步GIS任务的基准测试集，任务来源于GIS Stack Exchange并在真实公共数据上实例化，每个任务配有可执行的参考轨迹和精确的地面真值输出文件，支持严格的、确定性的、容差感知的输出匹配评估。对六个LLM模型的评估表明，最优智能体在严格评分下仅完成32.7%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GIS智能体基准测试集大多来自教科书、教程或LLM生成的种子，规模有限且轨迹深度不足，更重要的是没有提供地面真值输出，只能依赖代码相似性、轨迹匹配或LLM/VLM评判器等代理信号，这会将工作流相似性与任务正确性混为一谈。需要一个具有精确地面真值输出的真实GIS工作流基准。

Method: 从GIS Stack Exchange收集并策展349个多步GIS任务，在六个选定地理区域的真实公共数据上实例化。每个任务附带可执行的参考轨迹和精确的地面真值输出文件，采用严格的、确定性的、容差感知的输出匹配机制而非LLM评判。评估了六个LLM模型的性能。

Result: 六个LLM模型的评估表明，真实GIS工作流仍具挑战性：最佳智能体在严格容差感知评分下仅完成32.7%的任务，尽管大多数模型产生的输出接近地面真值。

Conclusion: GISAgentBench填补了GIS智能体评测中缺乏地面真值输出的空白，揭示了当前LLM代理在执行真实世界多步GIS工作流方面仍存在显著的性能差距，为未来GIS自动化研究提供了可靠的评测基准。

Abstract: Geographic Information System (GIS) professionals rely on multi-step spatial analysis workflows to support decision-making in urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. The process is tedious, time-consuming, and error-prone. While recent large language model (LLM) agents equipped with external tools have the potential to automate geospatial analysis, their ability to perform realistic GIS workflows remains largely unexplored. Existing GIS agent benchmarking datasets are mostly drawn from textbooks, tutorials, or LLM-generated seeds and remain limited in size and trajectory depth. More importantly, none provides ground truth outputs. They therefore rely on surrogate signals such as code similarity, trajectory matching, or LLM and VLM judges, which can conflate workflow resemblance with task correctness. To address this gap, we introduce GISAgentBench, a benchmark of 349 multi-step GIS tasks curated from GIS Stack Exchange and instantiated on real public data across six selected geographic areas of interest. Each task ships with an executable reference trajectory and an exact ground truth output file, enabling strict, deterministic, tolerance-aware output matching beyond LLM judging. Evaluations of six LLM models reveal that realistic GIS workflows remain challenging: the best agent completes only 32.7% of tasks under strict tolerance-aware scoring, although most models produce outputs that are close to the ground truth.

</details>


### [126] [LongCat Sparse Attention: Taming the Lightning via Streaming-aware Hierarchical Cross-Layer Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01662)
*Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Hong Liu,Cunguang Wang,Xiang Li,Duyue Ma,Guanyu Wu,Yifan Lu,Fengcun Li,Yerui Sun,Peng Pei,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCat稀疏注意力（LSA），一种硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过三种互补策略（流感知索引、跨层索引、分层索引）解决DeepSeek稀疏注意力（DSA）中Lightning索引器带来的O(L²)计算开销和硬件低效内存访问问题，在69B至560B参数规模上达到与全注意力相当的性能，支持百万token上下文训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek稀疏注意力（DSA）通过Lightning索引器实现了高效长上下文建模，但在实际部署中仍受限于两个系统级瓶颈：索引器的O(L²)评分开销过高，以及索引器输出导致的硬件低效、不连续内存访问模式。这些问题阻碍了DSA在真实硬件上的高效落地。

Method: LSA框架包含三种正交且互补的策略：(1) 流感知索引（Streaming-Aware Indexing）：将分散的KV条目选择性转换为硬件对齐的连续布局，实现合并的HBM访问；(2) 跨层索引（Cross-Layer Indexing）：通过跨层蒸馏，复用单层索引结果于连续多层，摊销索引开销；(3) 分层索引（Hierarchical Indexing）：采用从粗到细的评分方案，逐步缩小每个查询的候选集，大幅减少索引计算量。

Result: 在69B-A3B到560B-A27B的大规模扩展实验中，LSA在通用和长上下文基准上均持续达到与全注意力相当的性能。LSA支持原生训练至百万token上下文长度，并支撑了LongCat-2.0（1.6T-A48B）的开发。同时开源了集成LSA的LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse（69B-A3B）模型。

Conclusion: LSA通过硬件-算法协同设计的三种互补策略，有效解决了DSA在系统层面的索引开销和内存访问瓶颈，在保持模型质量的同时显著提升了稀疏注意力在长上下文场景下的部署效率，并通过开源和Large-scale模型验证了其实际可行性。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) enables efficient long-context modeling through its Lightning Indexer. However, practical deployment remains constrained by the indexer's expensive $O(L^2)$ scoring overhead and the hardware-inefficient, discontinuous memory-access patterns induced by its outputs. To address these system-level bottlenecks, we introduce LongCat Sparse Attention (LSA), a hardware-algorithm co-designed framework comprising three complementary and orthogonal strategies: (1) Streaming-Aware Indexing, which selectively converts scattered KV entries into hardware-aligned contiguous layouts to enable coalesced HBM access; (2) Cross-Layer Indexing, which amortizes indexing overhead by reusing the results produced by a single layer across consecutive layers, supported by cross-layer distillation; and (3) Hierarchical Indexing, which adopts a coarse-to-fine scoring scheme to progressively narrow the candidate set for each query, thereby substantially reducing indexing computation. Extensive scaling experiments, ranging from 69B-A3B to 560B-A27B models, demonstrate that LSA consistently achieves performance on par with full attention across both general-purpose and long-context benchmarks. Moreover, LSA supports native training with context lengths of up to one million tokens and underpins the development of LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B). To facilitate further research, we also introduce and open-source LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse (69B-A3B), which integrates LSA into LongCat-Flash-Lite and incorporates an updated long-context training corpus.

</details>


### [127] [Allocation Before Ranking: Decoupled Token Compression for OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01665)
*Zhenghui Guo,Yilin Yang,Yuanbin Man,Miao Yin,Weidong Shi,Rabimba Karanjai,Omprakash Gnawali,Chengming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Macer是一种免训练的token压缩方法，针对Omni大语言模型中音频-视频多模态token压缩问题，提出先分配显式的音频和视频预算，再在模态内进行分配归一化的排序，替代传统的共享top-K排序策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OmniLLM的token压缩被简化为单一的显著性排序问题（对每个多模态token打分并保留top-K），但同一注意力分数同时决定两个维度：各模态获得的保留容量分配，以及模态内哪些token被保留。共享top-K规则继承了音频优先的分配先验，导致保留容量在音频token上被过早消耗，视频token没有机会参与竞争，违反了直觉且分配不优。

Method: 提出Macer压缩器：（1）首先为音频和视频分配显式的token预算；（2）在各模态特定浅层执行分配归一化（allocation-normalized）的模态内排序。整个方法为免训练（training-free）设计。

Result: 在音频主导、音频-视频联合、视觉主导和视频中心的多个基准上，Macer在降低token成本的同时保持精度。25%保留率下，Qwen2.5-Omni-7B保留98.7%全token性能，Qwen2.5-Omni-3B保留97.3%。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，25%设置即达到OmniZip的45%保留率性能且FLOPs更低。在OmniVinci-9B上，分配先于排序的原则相比共享top-K最多提升12.9个百分点。

Conclusion: Macer通过将模态间容量分配与模态内token选择解耦，验证了"先分配后排序"原则在跨模态token压缩中的有效性，为OmniLLM的token压缩提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: Token compression in OmniLLMs is typically posed as a single saliency-ranking problem: score each multimodal token, keep the top-K. We argue this abstraction is mis-specified. The same attention score simultaneously decides two things: how much retained capacity each modality receives, and which tokens within a modality are kept. A shared top-K rule therefore inherits this audio-favoring allocation prior, spending retained capacity on audio before video tokens have a chance to compete. We propose Macer, a training-free compressor that first assigns explicit audio and video budgets, then performs allocation-normalized ranking within each modality at modality-specific shallow layers. Macer significantly reduces token cost while preserving accuracy across audio-grounded, audio--video joint, visual-dominant, and video-centric benchmarks. At 25 % retention, Macer preserves 98.7 % of full-token performance on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and 97.3 % on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B. On Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, this 25 % setting reaches OmniZip-level performance at 45 % retention while using lower FLOPs. On OmniVinci-9B, the same allocation-before-ranking principle improves over shared top-K ranking by up to 12.9 points.

</details>


### [128] [TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01667)
*Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TCPO是一种面向验证器引导的多轮强化学习场景的回合级信用分配方法，通过将验证器分数转化为回合级别的优势信号来解决"密集反馈不等于密集信用"的问题。实验表明TCPO在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld智能体任务上匹配或超越了最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮设置下，模型每轮结束后会收到验证器评分，虽然提供了密集的反馈信号，但这些分数衡量的是当前输出的质量，而非当前回合对后续改进轨迹的贡献。这种"反馈密集但信用稀疏"的矛盾使得现有的信用分配方法无法准确评估每个回合的实际价值，导致策略优化效率受限。

Method: TCPO将信用分配问题建模为"分数到信用"的转换（score-to-credit conversion），通过基于参考的对比构建回合级优势，包含三个互补的组成部分：(1) 回溯信用（retrospective credit）——捕捉相对于最佳历史状态的即时进展或退步；(2) 后见之明的延迟信用（hindsight delayed credit）——识别在当下看似无改进但后期产生回报的回合；(3) 选择性固定历史反事实估计（selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation）——在同一历史条件下精炼高惊讶度回合的信用分配。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld智能体任务上的实验表明，TCPO在不同模型规模、任务领域和验证器类型下均优于或匹配最强基线。在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上达到最优或并列最优的best-turn Pass@8，减少了达到成功所需的回合数，并提升了多轮智能体性能。

Conclusion: 研究者认为"分数到信用"的转换（score-to-credit conversion）是验证器引导的多轮策略优化中的核心要素，TCPO的三类信用信号互为补充，为多轮强化学习中的信用分配提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Verifier-guided reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving LLM reasoning. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier-guided multi-turn RL. TCPO casts credit assignment as score-to-credit conversion and constructs turn-level advantages through reference-based comparisons: retrospective credit captures immediate progress and regression relative to the best prior state; hindsight delayed credit identifies non-improving turns with later payoff; and selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation refines high-surprisal turns under the same history. Experiments on math reasoning, code generation, and AppWorld agent tasks show that TCPO improves or matches the strongest baselines across model scales, task domains, and verifier types. TCPO achieves the best or tied-best best-turn Pass@8 on Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, reduces turns to success, and improves multi-turn agent performance. These results highlight score-to-credit conversion as a central ingredient for verifier-guided multi-turn policy optimization.

</details>


### [129] [When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01679)
*Qiuyang Zhan,Rui Zhang,Sheng Guo,Lepeng Zhao,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"权威崩溃"（authority collapse）问题：持久记忆系统在将交互历史整合为可复用记忆时，保留了记忆内容却丢失了其来源的授权约束，导致存储记忆拥有超出其来源所允许的权威。作者引入AuthMem-Bench基准测试，通过7个记忆系统整合器和7个LLM骨干验证了49个配置中有48个存在权威崩溃，并在端到端评估中将未经授权的操作率从16.9%降至0.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆使LLM代理能够跨任务自适应，但记忆整合过程中存在一个隐含的授权边界——即存储的信息日后能否被消费为用户事实、可验证观察或长期指令。当整合过程保留声明却丢失了其来源约束时，会产生"权威崩溃"，使得记忆隐含的权威高于其来源所允许的范围，这是一个此前未被系统识别的安全缺陷。

Method: 作者提出AuthMem-Bench，一个受控配对基准，在执行控制变量法（固定焦点声明和下游任务，仅变化来源权威）的同时评估三类指标：写入时崩溃、下游授权错误和自动权威保留。评估覆盖7种基于广泛使用的记忆系统的整合器和7种LLM骨干，并在受控行动落地评估和端到端评估两个层面进行验证。

Result: 在49个评估配置中观察到48个存在权威崩溃；受控行动落地评估中，缺乏权威元数据的崩溃记忆导致平均未经授权行动率为50.3%；端到端评估中，自动预测并持久化的权威标签将未经授权行动率从16.9%降至0.0%，而良性任务成功率基本不变。

Conclusion: 记忆驱动的适应必须同时保留"学到了什么"以及"在何种权威下可被复用"。权威标签的自动预测与持久化能有效消除权威崩溃带来的未经授权行动，且不会牺牲良性任务性能。

Abstract: Persistent memory allows (self-evolving) LLM agents to adapt across tasks by consolidating heterogeneous interaction histories into reusable facts, preferences, observations, and rules. Yet consolidation also imposes an implicit authorization boundary: it determines whether stored information may later be consumed as a user fact, an attested observation, or a standing instruction. We identify authority collapse, in which consolidation preserves a claim while erasing the source constraints governing its authorized use, causing the stored memory to imply greater authority than its source permits. We introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that holds the focal claim and downstream task fixed while varying only source authority. It evaluates write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation. Across seven consolidators based on widely used agent-memory systems and seven LLM backbones, we observe authority collapse in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In a controlled action-grounded evaluation, collapsed memories without authority metadata yield a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was
  learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [130] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GABench，一个面向智能体图分析的综合基准，涵盖三种图类型、四类图分析任务、84个可执行工具，并构建了10,400个具有可验证真实答案的任务。实验发现现有LLM智能体在图分析任务上仍表现不佳，harness选择显著影响性能，且图分析效果更依赖工具调用质量而非数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图分析基准存在两个主要限制：(1)对图任务和图类型的覆盖有限，难以全面评估LLM智能体；(2)通常将图分析建模为基于文本的问答，图信息直接嵌入提示中，限制了端到端智能体能力的评估。作者希望建立更全面的基准来评估LLM智能体在真实图环境中的代理能力。

Method: GABench涵盖三种图类型（如简单图、异质图等），覆盖四类图分析任务：图检索、图论、图机器学习和图开放问答。提供84个可执行工具用于访问图数据和执行多样图操作。基于这些工具构建了智能体图分析任务生成流水线，生成10,400个带有可验证真实答案的任务。

Result: 评估各种前沿LLM和智能体harness后发现：(1)现有LLM智能体在复杂图分析任务上仍表现困难；(2)harness选择对性能有显著影响，但现有harness在复杂图任务上仍有限；(3)图分析效果更依赖工具调用质量而非调用数量。

Conclusion: GABench提供了一个更全面的智能体图分析评估框架，实验发现的三个关键结论为LLM智能体在图分析领域的开发与评估提供了实践性见解。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在重规划、重试、委托、并发和崩溃恢复等场景下，即使每个令牌是单次使用的，也会因新的令牌标识符导致同一用户授权被重复请求和执行，这种现象称为"语义重放"。作者提出CapLease，一种授权消费层，通过绑定认证用户确认与规范动作、强制执行事务性的Issue-Prepare-Commit转换，实现跨令牌的持久单调状态，从而防止重复授权和重复外部效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次使用令牌（single-use token）只在令牌标识符层面限制消费，无法阻止LLM智能体在语义层面（如重新规划、重试失败操作、委托任务、崩溃恢复）以新令牌标识符重新请求同一授权，导致一个用户授权被执行多次，即"语义重放"问题。这暴露了系统级的安全缺陷，需要新的解决思路。

Method: 提出CapLease授权消费层，遵循提案级和权威级防御，将经过认证的用户确认绑定到规范化的动作上，并强制执行事务性的Issue-Prepare-Commit状态转换机制，同时结合幂等接收器（idempotent sink）来消除重复的外部副作用。其核心思想是维护跨令牌的单调持久状态（记录授权动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算），而非仅仅依赖令牌表示形式。

Result: 在LLM智能体的重规划、重试、委托、并发、确认重放和崩溃恢复等场景中，标识符局部的令牌（identifier-local tokens）允许新的语义重发；而CapLease和同等有状态的服务器账本（Server Ledger）都能阻止重复准入，并在配合幂等接收器时消除重复的外部效果。

Conclusion: 可重复性抵抗的智能体执行，其系统需求在于持久的授权状态（durable authorization state）而非令牌表示的形式本身；单纯依赖单次使用令牌表示不足以防止语义重放。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [132] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可执行分析知识表示（EAKR），将结构化证据转化为可执行元分析所需的决策知识显式表示，并基于大型语言模型与确定性服务构建MetaSynDec智能体框架。在58个综合单元上验证，67.9%实现完整对象保真度、75.0%实现精确证据集一致性，显著优于直接LLM生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于知识的科学分析面临根本性挑战：结构化证据本身并不包含可执行计算所需的分析知识。证据分配、分析对比、结局与时间点对齐、效应量公式化以及方法学可采性等决策必须在统计执行前显式化。现有自动化方法常将这些决策嵌入模型输出、生成代码或工作流痕迹中，而非表示为可独立验证的知识。

Method: 提出EAKR（Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation），一种机器可操作的知识表示，用于将结构化证据转化为可执行的元分析。EAKR表示证据、关系、数值输入、约束、溯源及未解决问题。在MetaSynDec中实现：LLM提出结构化更新，确定性服务负责基于schema和契约的验证与执行。

Result: 在58个综合单元中，MetaSynDec构建了所有EAKR，57个进入统计执行。56个有足够信息定义参考分析对象的单元中，38个（67.9%）实现完整对象保真度，42个（75.0%）实现精确证据集一致性，平均Jaccard相似度为0.909。生成与已发布的置信区间在55个单元中有54个重叠（98.2%）。MetaSynDec在参考综合结构一致性上优于直接LLM生成（57/58对23/58；p<0.001）。

Conclusion: EAKR支持形式化验证、可追溯性、统计执行和相对于直接LLM生成的改进方法学一致性，为知识驱动的元分析自动化提供了可行性证据。

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [133] [LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01718)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaCache提出了一种新型语义缓存方案，通过同时检查查询及其前k个推测解码token的缓存命中情况，在理论上证明了对缓存碰撞攻击的形式化安全保障，并在实践中提升了缓存检索的相关性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义缓存方案（通过嵌入相似性复用响应）本质上容易受到缓存碰撞攻击——攻击者通过注入精心构造的查询来污染缓存，从而破坏后续合法请求的响应。作者旨在从原理上重新设计语义缓存，以提供对这些攻击的可证明安全保障。

Method: 关键洞见是：虽然攻击者对恶意查询有完全控制权，但其对响应（需同时满足多重语义约束）的控制力远弱得多。LaCache不仅检查查询的缓存命中，还额外检查其前k个（推测性解码的）token的缓存命中。这通过双重约束使恶意查询无法同时触发恶意响应又与良性查询发生碰撞。

Result: 形式上证明了不可能构造同时引发恶意响应且与良性查询碰撞的对抗查询；同时，增强了语义上下文的索引也提升了缓存检索的响应相关性。在多种LLM和基准上的实证评估验证了安全保证与效率提升。

Conclusion: LaCache为鲁棒的语义缓存提供了有前景的方向，在安全性和效率之间取得了平衡，代表了语义缓存从启发式防御走向形式化安全保障的重要一步。

Abstract: Semantic caching, which reuses responses to semantically similar requests via their embeddings, has seen growing adoption in LLM serving, offering faster responses and reduced costs. Yet existing schemes are fundamentally vulnerable to cache-collision attacks, wherein an adversary pollutes the cache by injecting crafted queries, corrupting responses to subsequent legitimate requests. We present LaCache, a novel semantic caching scheme that addresses this vulnerability through a conceptually simple yet principled redesign. The key insight is that while the adversary has full control over the adversarial query, it has far less control over its response, which must simultaneously satisfy multiple semantic constraints. Rather than checking only the cache hit of a query, LaCache additionally checks the cache hit of its first k (speculatively) decoded tokens. This design yields two concrete benefits. First, it provides formally guaranteed resilience against cache-collision attacks: we prove that it is impossible to craft adversarial queries that simultaneously elicit malicious responses and collide with benign queries. Second, the enriched index supplies additional semantic context for cache retrieval, improving response relevance. Empirical evaluation across diverse LLMs and benchmarks validates both LaCache's security guarantees and efficiency gains, pointing to a promising direction for robust semantic caching.

</details>


### [134] [DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01735)
*Jianyu Wu,Yizhou Wang,Encheng Su,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中的"特权幻觉"问题，指出教师的特权信息与学生在推理时的上下文信息不对称是根本原因，提出双重锚定策略蒸馏（DAPD）框架，通过双路径锚定（DPA）和双源锚定（DSA）消除信息不对称，在多种模型规模上显著优于OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在语言模型后训练中应用广泛，但其教师模型利用训练时的特权信息来强化学生，会导致一种"特权幻觉"现象：学生学到了依赖特权信息的行为，但在推理时无法从上下文中复现该行为，却表现得仿佛特权信息仍然可用，最终性能下降。作者旨在识别这一失败的根源并解决它。

Method: 提出DAPD统一框架，包含两个层面的锚定：(1) 双路径锚定（DPA）引入自条件桥接，沿两条信息匹配的路径对齐参考行为和 rollout 行为，防止特权依赖行为被迁移到推理时的学生中；(2) 双源锚定（DSA）在参考到 rollout 和 rollout 到参考两个方向应用这些路径，减少对特权参考引导的依赖，同时保留正确性监督。

Result: 实验表明DAPD显著缓解了特权幻觉，在Qwen3-4B上平均超越OPSD达+2.00分；其优势在不同规模上保持一致，4B规模达+2.69分，32B规模达+2.78分。

Conclusion: 信息不对称是OPSD中特权幻觉的根源，DAPD通过双路径和双源锚定有效消除这一不对称，在不同模型规模上均稳定提升性能，验证了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: On-policy (self) distillation (OPSD) is increasingly adopted for language-model post-training. It strengthens the teacher with privileged information but can induce a privilege illusion: the student learns privilege-dependent behavior it cannot reproduce from its inference-time context, yet behaves as if the training-time privileged information remained available, ultimately degrading performance. In this paper, we identify information asymmetry between the privileged teacher and the student at inference as the root cause of this failure in OPSD. To resolve this asymmetry, we propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD), a unified framework with two levels of anchoring. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge and aligns reference and rollout behavior along two matched-information paths, preventing privilege-dependent behavior from being transferred to the inference-time student. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) applies these paths in both reference-to-rollout and rollout-to-reference directions, reducing reliance on privileged reference guidance while preserving correctness supervision. Extensive experiments show that DAPD significantly alleviates privilege illusion, outperforming OPSD on Qwen3-4B by +2.00 points on average across tasks. Notably, its gains persist across scales, reaching +2.69 at 4B and +2.78 at 32B.

</details>


### [135] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoEvo-Mem，一种闭环框架，通过在训练过程中交替更新检索策略（轻量级残差路由器）和记忆库（记忆值与图关系），实现二者的协同演化，在七个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期LLM智能体方法通常分别优化检索访问（查询改写、自适应检索策略）或记忆演化（结构更新），忽略了二者之间存在的反馈循环：检索决定哪些记忆收到使用信号，而更新的记忆库又重塑未来的检索。这种分离导致性能受限。

Method: CoEvo-Mem包含三个关键组件：1) 冻结LLM为每个查询生成路线特定的查询改写和路由先验；2) 轻量级残差路由器在线纠正路由决策；3) 检索到的上下文作为两个学习过程的耦合接口——任务结果给路由决策分配信用，轨迹条件化反馈更新记忆值和图关系。为缓解耦合导致的非平稳性，采用交替优化策略：固定记忆库更新路由器，固定检索策略演化记忆库。

Result: 在七个不同的基准测试上，CoEvo-Mem取得了最先进的性能，验证了检索-记忆协同演化的有效性。

Conclusion: 检索策略与记忆库的协同演化（而非分离优化）对长周期LLM智能体性能至关重要，CoEvo-Mem通过闭环反馈机制在多个基准上证明了该范式的优越性。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [136] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemSIF记忆框架，通过结构化交互记忆（Topical Segments + Event Trajectories）和双轨事实记忆（CoreFact + ActiveFact）分别缓解LLM智能体长时交互中的时间-结构错位（TSM）和延迟效用显现（DUM）问题，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上超越最强基线2.29%-8.79%和2.87%-6.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的长期记忆系统存在两个反复出现的错位模式：TSM（时间邻近性与主题/事件关联性不一致）和DUM（写入时的显著性无法可靠预测未来查询效用），这些模式限制了长期记忆在长时交互中的有效性。

Method: 提出MemSIF框架，包含两部分：1）结构化交互记忆：将原始交互组织为保持局部主题连贯性的Topical Segments和维持跨时间事件连续性的Event Trajectories；2）双轨事实记忆：CoreFact记忆在写入时整合稳定的模式化信息，ActiveFact记忆按需形成事实，并通过多源历史支持和重复查询需求的双重标准来提升事实的复用。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S两个基准上、覆盖五种骨干LLM的实验中，MemSIF在所有设置下均取得最高Total ACC，相比最强基线在LoCoMo上提升2.29%-8.79%，在LongMemEval-S上提升2.87%-6.15%。

Conclusion: 将结构化交互记忆与双轨事实记忆相结合，能有效缓解TSM和DUM错位问题，验证了该框架对提升LLM智能体长期记忆能力的有效性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [137] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-Lock，首个基于强化学习的互锁装配生成框架，将互锁装配生成视为顺序决策问题，通过结构化动作分块与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习高效搜索大规模组合空间，在现有方法耗时过长或失败的挑战性场景中能有效生成互锁装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁装配因其结构稳定性被广泛应用于现实场景，但生成互锁装配本质上是一个形状分解问题，即在体素网格中将目标3D物体划分为规定数量的互锁部件。现有方法依赖手工设计的搜索启发式，在复杂情况下耗时过长甚至无法找到有效解。作者观察到该问题本质上是一个顺序决策问题，因此动机是利用强化学习来自动学习生成策略，摆脱对手工启发式的依赖。

Method: RL-Lock将互锁装配生成问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，其中智能体逐步决策每个体素所属的部件。方法结合了结构化动作分块（structured action chunking）与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习，以高效导航互锁装配生成的大规模组合搜索空间。

Result: 实验表明RL-Lock能够有效生成互锁装配，尤其在现有方法耗时过长甚至找不到有效解的挑战性场景中表现出明显优势。

Conclusion: RL-Lock证明了强化学习在互锁装配生成任务中的可行性，能够在不依赖手工搜索启发式的条件下，在困难案例中超越现有方法的表现。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [138] [Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01755)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Kaixuan Wang,Guli Zhang,Hongyan Xie,Yakun Zhu,Hao Geng,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFT-RLVR方法，解决自动驾驶VLA模型中由于教师模型暴露于日志中的真实未来轨迹而导致的"轨迹锚定偏差"（trajectory anchoring bias）问题，并通过AD-MCQ（自动驾驶多项选择题）将规划任务转化为对显式轨迹候选的选择，实现可验证的自动驾驶推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在自动驾驶中利用CoT监督增强VLM推理能力，但注释管线常将教师模型暴露于已记录的GT未来轨迹，导致轨迹锚定偏差：教师模型合理化已揭示的结果而非从场景证据推断决策，产生因果不忠实的CoT和更严重的幻觉，尤其在因果挑战性场景中。

Method: 1) 移除GT轨迹暴露以消除捷径；2) 提出AD-MCQ，将规划建模为在显式轨迹候选中的选择，避免开放式轨迹生成；3) 提出DEFT-RLVR，将未来轨迹从决策前锚点转变为决策后验证目标，用于RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）。

Result: DEFT-RLVR提升了自动驾驶推理能力，同时保持甚至增强通用视觉能力；AD-MCQ支持仅用VLM推理，且可通轨迹候选构造控制难度，提供灵活、可扩展、可扩展的可验证自动驾驶推理基础。

Conclusion: AD-MCQ和DEFT-RLVR为可验证的自动驾驶推理提供了灵活、可扩展且可扩展的基础，有效缓解轨迹锚定偏差并提升推理的因果忠实性。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD) increasingly utilize chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision to enhance the reasoning capabilities of their Vision-Language Model (VLM) components, yet existing annotation pipelines commonly expose the teacher model to the logged ground-truth (GT) future trajectory. We empirically show that this induces trajectory anchoring bias: teacher models rationalize the revealed outcome rather than infer a decision from scene evidence, producing less causally faithful CoTs and substantially more severe hallucinations, especially in causally challenging scenes. Removing the GT trajectory eliminates this shortcut, but open-ended trajectory generation entangles high-level decision-making with precise geometric synthesis and low-level dynamics. To make trajectory-level driving decisions verifiable without requiring open-ended trajectory synthesis, we introduce Autonomous-Driving Multiple-Choice Question (AD-MCQ), which casts planning as selection among explicit trajectory candidates. Taking this a step further, we propose Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for RLVR (DEFT-RLVR) to transform future trajectories from pre-decision anchors into post-decision verification targets. Experimental results show that DEFT-RLVR improves AD reasoning while preserving or even enhancing general visual capabilities. With VLM-only inference and controllable difficulty through candidate construction, AD-MCQ provides a flexible, scalable, and extensible foundation for future research on verifiable AD reasoning.

</details>


### [139] [Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01767)
*Dongqi Wang,Weiwei Chen,Han Zhou,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的框架，通过融合超过1100万条食品检查记录与统计年鉴中的人口、经济与环境数据，实现城市级别的食品风险细粒度预测。采用三阶段预训练设计，借助Wilson区间提供的部分监督和半监督标签精炼来应对样本稀疏问题。2022年数据实验表明该框架显著优于基线，与浙江省市场监管局的实地实验中检测率和资源配置效率均优于人工方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品安全是重要的公共卫生挑战，但在检查资源有限、区域采样数据稀疏的情况下，传统的风险评估方法难以实现精准、及时的风险识别，亟需一种能够有效利用大规模历史检查数据并应对数据稀疏性的预测框架。

Method: 基于Transformer的框架，整合超过1100万条食品检查记录以及统计年鉴提取的人口、经济和环境指标；采用三阶段预训练策略，利用Wilson区间捕获安全与风险排名的部分监督信号，并结合半监督标签精炼以充分利用小样本历史记录。

Result: 2022年数据实验显示所提方法显著优于各基线；与浙江省市场监管局合作的实地实验表明，与人工制定的方案相比，该方法能提高检出率并更高效地分配检查资源。

Conclusion: 将大规模公共检查数据、Wilson区间置信度建模与先进深度学习相结合，能够实现对食品安全威胁更早、更细粒度的识别，推动从被动应对向主动、数据驱动的食品安全监管模式转变。

Abstract: Ensuring food safety represents a critical public health challenge, particularly when inspection resources are limited and regional sampling data are sparse. This study proposes a Transformer-based framework capable of forecasting fine-grained, city-level food safety risks by unifying over 11 million inspection records with supplemental demographic, economic, and environmental indicators extracted from the Statistical Yearbook. A three-stage pretraining design leverages partial supervision from the Wilson interval (capturing both safety and risk rankings), together with semi-supervised label refinement, to effectively utilize historical records even when local sample sizes are insufficient. Experimental evaluations on data from 2022 show that the proposed approach outperforms baselines significantly. A subsequent field experiment in collaboration with the Zhejiang Provincial Administration for Market Regulation further demonstrates improved detection rates and more efficient allocation of inspection resources compared to a manually developed plan. Observations of regulatory decision-making reveal a threshold-based heuristic employed by inspectors, hinting that additional training or decision-support interfaces could further enhance the impact of AI-generated risk scores. Overall, these findings underscore that a rigorous integration of large-scale public inspection data, Wilson interval-based confidence modeling, and advanced deep learning can facilitate earlier and more granular identification of food safety threats. By reducing reliance on reactive measures alone, the proposed framework has the potential to advance proactive, data-driven oversight of the global food supply.

</details>


### [140] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAMES是一个闭环框架，用于从现有资产冷启动可部署的LLM智能体技能，并通过共识驱动的变异、基于帕累托的精度-成本选择以及防回归保障来持续演进，同时保持审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地运行受策略约束的企业工作流（如文档审计），需要一致地应用规则、逐值落地并保持可审计性。但改进这些智能体很困难：运营反馈稀疏且无标注，编辑一条规则可能使不相关案例回退，且精度提升不能以推理成本膨胀或丧失审计性为代价。

Method: FRAMES通过以下方式实现闭环演进：(1) 从已有资产冷启动可部署技能；(2) 基于共识的变异（consensus-based mutation）生成改进；(3) 在精度与成本间进行Pareto选择；(4) 提供防回归（anti-regression）保证；(5) 全程保持可审计性。

Result: 在内部生产系统上部署后，FRAMES在基准方法中达到了最优的精度-成本权衡，并且在tau-bench上复现了相同的提升。

Conclusion: FRAMES证明了可以在不依赖大量标注数据、不增加推理成本、不牺牲审计性的前提下，通过闭环自动演化持续改进企业级LLM智能体，并在内部与公开基准上均取得最佳性能。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [141] [REFLEX: Rethinking MoE Inference as Refinement-Aware Compute Allocation in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01784)
*Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Yiming Zhang,Jiazheng Liu,Hongyu Zhang,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REFLEX，一种无需训练的专家分配方法，针对扩散语言模型（DLM）中Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）推理的细化感知计算分配问题。REFLEX保持默认路由器不变，通过粗到细的层级结构重组专家计算，利用前沿进度分数解决活动块优先级，在LLaDA-MoE和LLaDA2.0-mini上平均减少15%的专家计算量，同时保持甚至提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扩散语言模型中，每次去噪前向传播会同时处理所有token位置，但不同token位置的细化需求差异显著。而默认的固定token-choice路由为所有token分配统一的专家预算，导致专家计算与细化需求不匹配，即"一刀切"的专家分配无法适应异构的细化状态。

Method: REFLEX是一种无需训练的方法，保持默认路由器不变，围绕演化中的细化过程重组专家计算。具体而言，它引入粗到细的专家预算分配层级，将计算与块相对细化角色对齐，并使用前沿进度分数（Frontier-Progress Score）来解析活动块的优先级。

Result: 在LLaDA-MoE和LLaDA2.0-mini两个代表性MoE扩散语言模型上，多个基准测试显示REFLEX平均减少15%的专家计算分配，同时在大多数基准上保持甚至提升生成质量。与自回归式可变专家路由方法相比，REFLEX在质量-计算权衡上表现更一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在扩散语言模型的MoE推理中，应将专家计算分配视为跨异构token细化状态的细化感知计算分配问题，而非简单的固定路由。根据每次去噪前向传播中暴露的异构细化需求来分配专家计算是更优的策略。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models increase parameter capacity by activating only a small subset of experts for each token. This conditional-computation paradigm has enabled autoregressive language models to scale model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. In diffusion language models (DLMs), however, each denoising forward jointly revisits all token positions despite their sharply different refinement demands, while the default fixed token-choice routing assigns them a uniform expert budget, creating a mismatch between expert computation and refinement demand. We argue that MoE inference in DLMs should therefore be viewed as refinement-aware compute allocation across heterogeneous token refinement states. We propose REFLEX (\textbf{RE}finement-aware \textbf{FLEX}ible expert allocation), a training-free method that keeps the default router unchanged while reorganizing expert computation around the evolving refinement process. Specifically, REFLEX introduces a coarse-to-fine hierarchy for expert-budget allocation that aligns computation with block-relative refinement roles while using the Frontier-Progress Score to resolve active-block priorities. Across multiple widely used benchmarks on two representative MoE-based DLMs, LLaDA-MoE and LLaDA2.0-mini, REFLEX reduces allocated expert computation by 15\% on average while preserving or even improving generation quality on most benchmarks relative to default routing. Compared with autoregressive-style variable-expert routing methods, REFLEX also yields a more consistent quality--computation trade-off, further supporting the importance of allocating expert computation according to the heterogeneous refinement demands exposed within each denoising forward.

</details>


### [142] [Can You Trust the Confidence? ConfBench for Vision-Language Models on Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01792)
*Priyashree Roy,Sujitha Martin,Mohammad Rostami,Spencer Romo,Renhao Xue,Bob Strahan,Diego A. Socolinsky,Boyi Xie,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ConfBench，首个针对关键信息抽取（KIE）的校准专用基准，通过20种受控退化流程构建1,346个变体和70K+实体级评估，系统评测七种VLM在多种置信度估计方法下的校准质量，并引入ECARB指标用于直接衡量路由决策的操作收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档基准以干净高质量样本为主，低准确率区域过于稀疏，导致无法评估模型在低置信度区域的校准表现，无法支撑IDP系统在自动化与人工审查之间的可信路由决策。

Method: 构建ConfBench：对多样化文档集施以20种受控退化流水线，生成跨全精度谱系的1,346个变体与70K+实体级评估；评测四种专有和三种开源VLM，对比口头化（verbalized）与对数概率（log-probability）两类置信度估计方法，覆盖三种输入模态；并提出ECARB（审查预算指标）将判别性提升转化为运营节省。

Result: 发现：(i) OCR+图像模态给出更准确的置信度；(ii) 模型能力是主导因素——Claude家族内置信度质量随能力单调提升，而跨家族参数量预测力差；(iii) 校准质量差异极大（从近乎完美到严重过度自信），逐模型事后校正可重标定绝对置信度以支持阈值路由而不改变基于排序的操作指标；(iv) 首token聚合的对数概率法一致优于均值token和边际聚合。

Conclusion: ConfBench作为首个校准专用KIE基准公开发布，为可信IDP部署中置信度估计器和校准方法的系统化研究提供基础设施，ECARB指标为运营层面的路由优化提供了新的评估视角。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) with vision-language models (VLMs) hinges on confidence scores trustworthy enough to route extractions between automation and human review. Existing document benchmarks are dominated by clean, high-quality samples, leaving low accuracy regions too sparse for calibration assessment. We introduce ConfBench, the first calibration-specific benchmark for key information extraction (KIE), built by applying 20 controlled degradation pipelines to a diverse document set, yielding 1,346 variants and 70K+ entity-level evaluations spanning the full accuracy spectrum. We evaluate four proprietary and three open-weight VLMs under verbalized and log-probability confidence estimation methods across three input modalities, and find: (i) OCR+Image modality results in more accurate confidence estimates; (ii) model capability is the dominant factor: within the Claude family confidence quality scales monotonically with capability, while across families parameter count is a poor predictor; (iii) calibration quality varies widely across models, from near-perfect to severely overconfident, and per-model post-hoc correction rescales these absolute confidence values for threshold-based routing without altering ranking-based operational metrics; and (iv) log-probability with first-token aggregation consistently outperforms mean-token and margin aggregations. We also introduce ECARB, a review-budget metric translating discriminative gains into operational savings. We release ConfBench publicly to enable systematic study of confidence estimators and calibration methods for trustworthy IDP application deployment.

</details>


### [143] [CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01802)
*Junru Song,Wenhao Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuekai Qiu,Feifei Wang,Weien Zhou,Tingsong Jiang,Ying Wen,Yang Li,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoNav-UAV，将空中目标导向视觉语言导航任务建模为高、低空双无人机之间的Stackelberg博弈，并通过迭代Stackelberg学习实现双智能体的协同适应，在AerialVLN基准上显著超越单智能体和双智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中平台的VLN任务需要同时解决全局探索与目标定位（需要高空视野）和近距离无碰撞接近（需要低空精细控制）这两个相互交织且难以在同一智能体中调和的过程。现有方法要么将地面VLN范式迁移到低空无人机并用外部辅助补偿低效探索，要么采用双无人机互补高度但依赖特权信息且独立训练两个智能体，无法实现协作所需的相互适应。

Method: CoNav-UAV将任务显式建模为高空"领导者"与低空"追随者"之间的Stackelberg博弈，系统仅依赖机载视觉和语言输入。通过迭代Stackelberg学习求解该博弈：领导者基于记忆的上下文学习（in-context learning）精炼高层视觉语言推理，追随者通过DAgger式专家蒸馏更新精确运动控制，交替更新驱动两个智能体收敛到Stackelberg均衡。

Result: 在AerialVLN基准的三个高保真城市场景中，CoNav-UAV一致优于单智能体和双智能体基线：学习场景成功率提升最高达30.8个百分点，跨场景迁移下提升9.0个百分点，且仅使用约3倍少的适应数据。进一步分析验证了领导者和追随者更新的互补增益，并揭示了不同VLM骨干下的稳健增益与差异化学习动态。

Conclusion: CoNav-UAV通过Stackelberg博弈框架有效调和了全局探索与近距离着陆两个矛盾目标，证明了双智能体协同（而非独立训练）对空中VLN任务的关键价值，且该方法在数据效率和跨场景泛化上均有显著优势。

Abstract: Target-oriented vision-and-language navigation (VLN) on aerial platforms is attracting growing attention for missions such as disaster rescue, infrastructure inspection, and security patrol. In this task, an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) needs to locate targets given only a concise description of their appearance and surroundings. This requires global exploration and grounding as well as collision-free close-range approach, two interleaved processes difficult to reconcile within a single agent. Most existing methods transfer the ground VLN paradigm to a low-altitude UAV and compensate for its inefficient exploration with external assistance. A recent attempt deploys two UAVs at complementary altitudes yet still relies on privileged information and trains its two agents independently, precluding any mutual adaptation essential for cooperation. Here we propose CoNav-UAV, which explicitly models the task as a Stackelberg game between a high-altitude leader and a low-altitude follower, with the system operating on onboard visual and linguistic inputs alone. To solve this game, we introduce Iterative Stackelberg Learning. The leader's high-level vision-language reasoning is refined via memory-based in-context learning, while the follower's precise motion control is updated via DAgger-style expert distillation. The alternation drives both agents toward a Stackelberg equilibrium. CoNav-UAV consistently outperforms single- and dual-agent baselines across three high-fidelity urban scenes from the AerialVLN benchmark. Success rate improves by up to 30.8 points on the learning scene, and 9.0 points under cross-scene transfer while using about 3x less adaptation data. Further analyses validate the complementary gains of the leader and follower updates and reveal robust gains yet distinct learning dynamics across VLM backbones.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Cross-Benchmark Generalization in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00181)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了强化学习在自包含环境中可能因利用环境特定规律（如工具模式、评分器解析、任务模板）而获得虚假能力提升的问题，提出用跨基准迁移的行为证据来判别真实技能习得。作者在363个长时程MCP任务上，采用SFT后接RL的两阶段流程，对Qwen3.5-122B-A10B混合专家模型进行后训练，结果显示模型在五个外部评估基准上均获得提升（最高+9.6pp），即使训练数据中不含软件工程任务，SWE-Bench Pro和Terminal-Bench 2仍有显著改善。成对轨迹分析发现四种跨域迁移的行为模式：更谨慎的局部目标形成、构建目标相关的工作状态、通过局部修复保持父目标稳定、以及完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自包含环境中训练的RL策略可能通过利用环境特定的规律（如工具模式、评分器解析、任务模板）获得奖励，而非真正习得可迁移的技能，且分布内留出集共享这些规律，难以检测是否有真实能力提升。因此需要一个行为层面的判断标准——即训练后的代理如何行动——并将跨基准迁移作为检验迁移能力的关键场所。

Method: 使用两阶段SFT-then-RL流程，在363个跨27个类别的长时程MCP（Model Context Protocol）任务上对开放权重的混合专家模型Qwen3.5-122B-A10B进行后训练。训练中不引入任何外部基准任务或评分器，外部分数也不参与奖励、超参数选择、检查点选取或停止决策。训练前仅基于Toolathlon表现选择基础模型家族和SFT教师模型。训练后，在五个外部评估基准上以贪婪pass@1评估性能，并开展成对轨迹分析以识别可迁移的行为模式。

Result: 训练后的模型在五个外部基准上均超过基础模型：Toolathlon（+9.6pp）、τ²-Bench（+5.3pp）、BFCL-V4（+3.5pp）、SWE-Bench Pro（+5.8pp）和Terminal-Bench 2（+2.8pp）。特别值得注意的是，两个软件工程基准在训练数据完全不包含软件工程任务的情况下仍然有显著提升。成对轨迹分析识别出四种反复出现的行为差异（局部目标形成、工作状态构建、父目标稳定、完成验证），它们在办公工作流和代码场景中以类似形式出现。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了描述性证据，表明长时程多工具后训练能够改变代理的工作方式，且这种改变可以迁移到训练领域之外。这为区分"环境规律利用"与"真实技能习得"提供了行为层面的判别依据，表明跨基准迁移是检验RL后训练是否产生可迁移能力的有效视角。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning (RL) in self-contained environments, a policy can get rewards by exploiting environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) rather than by acquiring transferable skill, and an in-distribution holdout shares those regularities. We argue that the discriminating question is behavioral, namely how a trained agent acts, and that cross-benchmark transfer is the right place to look for it. We post-train an open-weight mixture-of-experts model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon Model Context Protocol (MCP) tasks across 27 categories, using a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline. Toolathlon performance informed the initial base-family and SFT-teacher choices, but no external-benchmark task or grader entered training and no external score informed the reward, training hyperparameters, trained-checkpoint selection, or stopping. At greedy pass@1, the trained model improves over the base on five reported external evaluations: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), $τ^2$-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Both software-engineering benchmarks improve despite the training collection containing no software-engineering tasks. An exploratory paired-trajectory analysis identifies four recurring behavioral differences (more careful local-goal formation, building goal-relevant working state, keeping parent goals stable through local repairs, and verifying completion) that appear in analogous forms across office workflows and code. These results provide descriptive evidence that long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change ways of working that transfer beyond its training domain.

</details>


### [145] [Source Code Authorship Attribution Does Not Generalize from Competitions to Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00227)
*Serhii Yemets,Marek Horváth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对预训练Transformer模型CodeBERT进行微调，评估其在源代码作者归属任务上的表现。模型在GCJ竞赛数据上表现优异（10作者92.6% Top-1，1000作者70.7% Top-1），但在真实课程作业数据集上几乎等同于随机基线（0.2%和0.06% Top-1），且该差距在所有评估的模型家族中一致存在，表明基于GCJ的基准高估了作者归属在实际教育场景中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码作者归属被广泛认为可用于教育场景（如学术诚信检测），但现有基准大多基于编程竞赛数据（如GCJ），缺乏对真实课程作业场景的验证。研究者旨在系统性地评估预训练Transformer模型在竞赛数据与真实课程数据上的表现差异，揭示基准与目标任务之间的不匹配问题。

Method: 对预训练Transformer模型CodeBERT进行微调，训练数据来自三个来源：Kaggle公开的GCJ提交数据、人工整理的GCJ档案、以及一所技术大学收集的真实课程作业数据集。同时构建了多模型基准（multi-model benchmark），覆盖多个模型家族在多种数据集配置下的表现，以验证性能差距的普遍性。

Result: CodeBERT在GCJ多轮数据上达到92.6% Top-1（10作者）和70.7% Top-1/88.2% Top-10（1000作者）。但在课程作业数据集上，相同流程仅达到0.2% Top-1（690作者的封闭作业）和0.06% Top-1（812作者的开放作业），与随机基线持平。多模型基准印证了这一差距在所有评估的模型家族中均存在。

Conclusion: GCJ类竞赛基准系统性高估了源代码作者归属在真实教育场景中的实用性。除非在目标课程作业上下文中进行验证，否则这些基准不应作为该技术实际能力的依据。数据集和任务属性的差异是造成这一性能落差的关键因素。

Abstract: Source code authorship attribution aims to identify the author of a program fragment from its writing style. We fine-tune the pre-trained transformer CodeBERT on three sources of data: publicly available Google Code Jam (GCJ) submissions from an open Kaggle repository, a curated GCJ archive, and institutional coursework datasets collected at a technical university. On multi-round GCJ data, CodeBERT reaches 92.6% Top-1 accuracy for 10 authors and retains 70.7% Top-1 (88.2% Top-10) for 1000 authors. On the examined coursework datasets, the same pipeline performs at or below the corresponding chance baselines: 0.2% Top-1 on a closed-assignment dataset of 690 authors and 0.06% Top-1 on open-ended assignments evaluated over 812 authors. A companion multi-model benchmark provides consistent cross-model evidence across additional dataset configurations, indicating that the observed performance gap is not specific to CodeBERT and persists across the evaluated model families. We analyze dataset and task properties that plausibly explain this gap and argue that GCJ-based benchmarks overestimate the practical applicability of authorship attribution in educational settings unless they are validated on the target coursework context.

</details>


### [146] [Modelling Android applications through static analysis and systematic exploratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00228)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了DroidGraph，一个结合传统静态分析与高效系统性探索测试的框架，用于生成Android应用的全面控制流模型。该模型覆盖从底层方法语句到高层UI结构的详细信息，可支持自动化测试生成。在19个应用上的评估表明，DroidGraph的探索测试比常见的随机探索平均多交互18%的应用，且少用345次交互；结合动态分析结果后，能额外发现平均51个组件和49%更多的界面回调链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用开发节奏快、发布频繁，需要自动化测试生成来支撑。基于模型的测试生成是最常见且有效的方法之一，但Android平台给应用的理解与建模带来诸多困难：静态分析难以应对Android事件驱动特性和多样化的功能实现机制；动态分析（如随机测试生成器）速度慢、效率低，且缺乏对应用知识的利用。

Method: DroidGraph框架采用两条互补路径：（1）传统静态分析，解析Android应用的字节码与清单文件；（2）高效的系统性探索测试（动态分析），以比随机探索更少的交互次数覆盖更多应用内容。最终将两者的结果整合，生成从底层方法语句到高层用户界面结构的全面控制流模型。

Result: 在19个多样化应用上的实验表明：探索测试平均比常见随机探索多交互18%的应用，且节省345次交互；动态分析结果补充静态分析后，平均额外发现51个组件和49%更多的界面回调链接。

Conclusion: DroidGraph通过结合静态分析与高效的系统性探索测试，能够生成Android应用的全面控制流模型，有效弥补单一分析方法的不足，可为自动化测试生成提供更完善的应用模型支持。

Abstract: Mobile application development is a fast paced industry with frequent releases. While the development pace increases, so too does the need for automated test generation. Model-based test generation is one of the most common and successful approaches to support this need. Understanding and modelling the application under test is integral to producing comprehensive, dependable and effective tests. Unfortunately, mobile platforms such as Android, introduce a host of difficulties. Static analysis struggles with Android's event-based nature and the growing variety of mechanisms available for developers to implement different features. Additionally, dynamic analysis, implemented by popular random test generators, is slow, inefficient, and limited by a lack of application knowledge.
  This paper introduces DroidGraph, a framework to generate a comprehensive control flow model of Android applications using traditional static analysis and efficient systematic exploratory tests. DroidGraph provides a detailed model of an Android application, from low level method statements to high level user interface structures. This model can be used to support automated test generation. We apply DroidGraph to 19 diverse apps and show that our efficient exploratory tests, on average, interact with 18% more of the app than commonly used random exploration in 345 less interactions. Integrating the dynamic analysis results provided by these tests complements our static analysis, and uncovers on average 51 more components and 49% more interface callback links in the application code.

</details>


### [147] [PADRAIG: Precise Android Automated Input Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00230)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PADRAIG，一个基于模型的Android自动化测试输入生成框架，通过利用AUT的详细控制流模型生成测试，相比SOTA工具平均提高16%的代码行覆盖率，同时降低84%的测试生成运行时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android自动化测试输入生成虽已研究十多年，但现有方法各有缺陷：随机输入生成虽然易于维护但效率低下；系统化和基于搜索的方法虽然能产生有效测试但需要过长的生成运行时；基于模型的方法虽有额外建模开销但测试生成更快。因此需要一种能够兼顾覆盖率和生成效率的方法。

Method: PADRAIG是一个基于模型的测试输入生成框架，核心思想是利用AUT的详细控制流模型来指导测试生成，从而在降低测试生成运行时的同时提高代码行覆盖率。实验选取19个来自F-Droid应用商店的随机Android应用，与3个分别使用不同测试输入生成技术的SOTA工具进行对比。

Result: 在19个随机选取的Android应用上，PADRAIG平均比SOTA工具多覆盖16%的代码行，并且测试生成运行时平均减少84%。

Conclusion: PADRAIG证明了基于详细控制流模型的测试输入生成方法能够在覆盖率和生成效率上同时超越现有SOTA技术，为Android自动化测试生成提供了一种更优的权衡方案。

Abstract: Android automated test input generation has been a highly researched topic for over a decade and has shown promising results with a variety of approaches. Random input generation is commonly used and the easiest to maintain, but ultimately inefficient. Systematic and search-based approaches produce effective tests but require a disproportionally large generation runtime. Model-based approaches have the additional overhead of modelling the application under test (AUT) but they result in a faster test generation.
  In this paper we present Precise AnDRoid Automated Input Generation (PADRAIG), a model-based test input generation framework that uses a detailed control flow model of the AUT to generate tests that can achieve higher line coverage, with a lower test generation runtime than the state of the art. We compare the line coverage achieved, and the generation runtime of PADRAIG against 3 state of the art tools, each of which uses a different test input generation technique. Our results, using 19 randomly selected Android apps from the F-Droid application store, show that PADRAIG achieves, on average, 16% more coverage of the AUT than the state of the art and it can generate tests with, on average, 84% less runtime.

</details>


### [148] [Improving Mobile User Interface Testing with Model Driven Monkey Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00236)
*Jordan Doyle,Takfarinas Saber,Paolo Arcaini,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的控制流结构来表示Android应用的代码及交互元素，通过将这一结构知识赋予Exerciser Monkey测试工具（形成增强版Monkey++），采用深度优先搜索代替随机事件生成，使测试套件速度提升一个数量级并实现用户交互元素的完全覆盖，从而显著提高Android应用自动化测试的有效性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Android应用测试工具（如Exerciser Monkey）随机生成用户输入事件，缺乏对底层应用结构的了解，导致测试速度慢、bug发现能力差。虽然开发人员可以预定义测试场景，但这是一项劳动密集型工作，亟需一种自动化的、基于应用结构知识的测试方法。

Method: 作者提出一种能够表示Android应用代码及其所有交互元素的控制流结构，并以此为基础改进Exerciser Monkey工具（命名为Monkey++）。Monkey++利用该控制流结构获取测试环境知识，并采用深度优先搜索（DFS）策略来遍历控制流结构，替代原有纯随机的事件生成方式，生成更有效的测试序列。

Result: 实验表明，随机性质的Exerciser Monkey生成的测试套件速度慢且覆盖率低；而基于控制流结构进行深度优先搜索生成的测试套件速度快一个数量级（order of magnitude），并能实现用户交互元素的完全覆盖（full coverage）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于应用控制流结构的搜索驱动测试方法能显著改善Android自动化测试的效果与效率，不仅为改进Exerciser Monkey提供了可行路径，也为未来基于更精准目标搜索的Android自动化测试技术奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Testing mobile applications often relies on tools, such as Exerciser Monkey for Android systems, that simulate user input. Exerciser Monkey, for example, generates random events (e.g., touches, gestures, navigational keys) that give developers a sense of what their application will do when deployed on real mobile phones with real users interacting with it. These tools, however, have no knowledge of the underlying applications' structures and only interact with them randomly or in a predefined manner (e.g., if developers designed scenarios, a labour-intensive task) -- making them slow and poor at finding bugs.
  In this paper, we propose a novel control flow structure able to represent the code of Android applications, including all the interactive elements. We show that our structure can increase the effectiveness (higher coverage) and efficiency (removing duplicate/redundant tests) of the Exerciser Monkey by giving it knowledge of the test environment. We compare the interface coverage achieved by the Exerciser Monkey with our new Monkey++ using a depth first search of our control flow structure and show that while the random nature of Exerciser Monkey creates slow test suites of poor coverage, the test suite created by a depth first search is one order of magnitude faster and achieves full coverage of the user interaction elements. We believe this research will lead to a more effective and efficient Exerciser Monkey, as well as better targeted search based techniques for automated Android testing.

</details>


### [149] [SMS Opt-In/Opt-Out Consent Record Architecture in Enterprise CRM Systems: Compliance Patterns for Multi-Tenant Managed Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00248)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向CRM原生消息系统的生产级同意记录（Consent Record）架构，用于满足TCPA等法规对SMS通信的明确同意和即时退出要求。该架构基于一个服务医疗、金融和销售运营等多个独立企业组织的消息传递托管包中的实践模式泛化而来，涵盖数据模型设计、基于关键词的同意捕捉、基于哈希的唯一性去重策略、发送时的抑制执行以及多租户约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCPA等法规要求企业消息系统必须获取并记录明确的用户同意，且需立即响应退出请求。然而CRM平台虽提供了丰富的联系人和潜在客户数据模型，却未将同意状态建模为第一类记录类型。在多租户托管包场景下，包不能假设或修改安装组织的模式，这一缺口在架构上变得至关重要。

Method: 论文提出了一套生产级同意记录架构，包含以下核心设计：(1) 数据模型设计，将同意状态作为一等公民记录；(2) 基于关键词的同意捕捉机制；(3) 基于哈希的唯一性策略实现去重；(4) 在消息发送时强制执行抑制规则；(5) 充分考虑多租户约束对设计决策的影响。

Result: 论文呈现了泛化自CRM原生消息托管包的实践模式，该包已服务于医疗、金融服务和销售运营等多个独立企业组织，证明了该同意记录架构在实际生产环境中的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该架构在无法修改租户模式的多租户受限环境下，为合规约束下的企业消息系统提供了可复用的同意管理与执行模式，填补了CRM原生数据模型在同意状态建模上的空白。

Abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) impose strict consent requirements on enterprise messaging systems: organizations must obtain and record explicit opt-in consent before sending SMS communications, and must immediately honor opt-out requests. While CRM platforms provide rich contact and lead data models, they do not natively model consent state as a first-class record type. This gap becomes architecturally significant in multi-tenant managed packages distributed via enterprise application marketplaces, where the package cannot assume or modify the installing organization's schema. This paper presents a production consent record architecture, generalized from patterns implemented within a CRM-native messaging managed package serving independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, financial services, and sales operations. We describe the data model design, keyword-based consent capture, a hash-based uniqueness strategy for deduplication, suppression enforcement at message send time, and the multi-tenant constraints that shaped these design decisions.

</details>


### [150] [LoopsBench: From Harness Engineering to Loop Engineering in Benchmarking Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00267)
*Han Li,Zhemin Fang,Rili Feng,Yingqi Zhao,Jiaheng Liu,Pengfei Gao,He Ye,Dayi Lin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出LOOPSBENCH，一个面向编码智能体循环工程（loop engineering）评估的长时程基准，包含112个真实来源任务，覆盖8种编程语言和9个领域，并验证了当前最强配置仅能解决25%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体基础设施正从harness工程转向loop工程，以支持持续的长时程软件开发。现有基准多聚焦于局部任务或最终结果状态，无法为智能体的持续执行过程提供足够的洞察，因此需要专门针对循环工程评估的长时程基准。

Method: 每个任务构建为一个依赖DAG（有向无环图），其中每个节点是可独立测试的开发单元，边由源代码证据支持的前提关系构成。基准包含112个来自真实来源的任务，覆盖8种编程语言和9个领域。运行时采用流感知设计：沿就绪前沿释放测试，并保留已完成节点作为回归测试义务。评估了前沿编码智能体与广泛使用的循环实现组合。

Result: 最强配置（Opus-4.7配合Claude Code和外层延续机制）仅解决25.00%的任务。记录的规划只恢复了源码中前提DAG的一部分，且回归事件在评估的所有循环配置中均可见。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体及其循环实现在长时程软件开发任务上的表现仍有很大提升空间，LOOPSBENCH为循环工程提供了可复现的评估基础，并已开源全部数据与代码。

Abstract: Coding agent infrastructure is shifting from harness engineering toward loop engineering as coding agents are deployed for sustained long-horizon software development. Existing benchmarks often center on localized tasks or end-state outcomes, offering limited insight into sustained execution. We introduce LOOPSBENCH, a long-horizon benchmark for loop engineering in coding agent evaluation. Each task is a dependency DAG over separately testable development units with source-evidenced prerequisite edges. LOOPSBENCH comprises 112 tasks from authentic sources spanning 8 programming languages and 9 domains. Its flow-aware runtime releases tests along the ready frontier and retains completed nodes as regression obligations. We evaluate frontier coding agents paired with widely used loop implementations. The strongest configuration, Opus-4.7 with Claude Code and outer continuation, resolves 25.00% of tasks. Recorded plans recover only part of the source-recovered prerequisite DAG, and regression events remain visible across the evaluated loop profiles. We open source the benchmark data and code, including all tasks, more than 5,300 development units, and executable tests, at microsoft/Loopsbench.

</details>


### [151] [OrEdge: Efficient Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection in Distributed Software Systems via Orthogonal-Domain Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00309)
*Amr M. Zaki,Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Honggeun Ji,Komal Sarda,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OrEdge，一个轻量级的实时多模态分布式软件系统异常检测框架，利用正交域时序表征替代昂贵的注意力/图架构，将重建模型压缩至最多9.6K参数（现有方法为20K-143K），在Raspberry Pi上实现亚秒级推理并将延迟降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法依赖计算昂贵的注意力机制和图结构架构，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上；同时多模态观测数据（日志、指标、轨迹）的联合分析需要高效方案来捕获时序依赖并降低冗余。

Method: OrEdge采用正交域时序表示，通过OrEdgeCore（轻量级正交域重建模块）联合分析日志、指标和轨迹等多模态数据，捕获重复性时序模式同时抑制瞬态变化，实现低复杂度异常检测。

Result: 在三个真实微服务数据集（MSDS、SN、TT）上取得与现有方法相当的检测性能，模型参数缩减至9.6K以内（对比20K-143K），在Raspberry Pi上实现亚秒级推理，延迟降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 正交域时序建模是注意力/图架构的有效替代方案，在边缘环境的实时多模态异常检测中实现了检测精度与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce Orthogonal-Edge (OrEdge), a lightweight framework for real-time anomaly detection in multi-modal distributed software systems. Unlike existing approaches that rely on computationally expensive attention- and graph-based architectures, OrEdge leverages orthogonal-domain temporal representations to achieve accurate anomaly detection with substantially lower computational complexity and model size. It jointly analyzes heterogeneous monitoring data, including logs, metrics, and traces, to identify abnormal software behavior, capture temporal dependencies, and reduce redundancy across observability signals. At its core, OrEdge incorporates OrEdgeCore, a lightweight orthogonal-domain reconstruction module that captures recurring temporal patterns while suppressing transient variations. Evaluated on three real-world microservice datasets (MSDS, SN, and TT), OrEdge achieves competitive detection performance while reducing the reconstruction model size to at most 9.6K parameters, compared with 20K--143K parameters in existing methods. This compact design enables efficient deployment on resource-constrained edge devices: on Raspberry Pi platforms, OrEdge achieves sub-second inference and reduces inference latency by over an order of magnitude compared with existing approaches. Extensive ablation studies, sensitivity analyses, orthogonal basis evaluations, and qualitative case studies further validate the effectiveness of each design component. Overall, OrEdge demonstrates that orthogonal-domain temporal modeling provides an effective alternative to computationally intensive attention- and graph-based architectures, achieving a favorable balance between detection accuracy and computational efficiency for real-time multi-modal anomaly detection in edge environments. The code is available at https://github.com/theamrzaki/MicroService_Twin_Original.

</details>


### [152] [Artificial Intelligence and Modeling & Simulation: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00366)
