Conclusion: 研究表明调用图增强的收益具有任务特异性，不应盲目期望其泛化到所有下游逆向工程任务。调用图的利用应针对上下文依赖型场景（如命名空间相关函数）进行设计，而非作为通用改进手段。同时，语义与语法任务间的内在权衡提示模型设计需要更精确的任务定位。

Abstract: Binary function embedding models are trained to encode the semantics of binary code in such a way that they can be generalized to a variety of reverse engineering tasks, such as binary code search, vulnerability detection, or malware classification. While many models only take the function in question as contextual input, there have been successful attempts to improve function embeddings by leveraging information from the call graph. In this study, we dissect the implications of these embedding refinements. We conduct experiments using a range of graph-based models on the embeddings generated by two state-of-the-art binary function embedding models. Integrating inter-procedural context, we show that improvements on binary code similarity detection (BCSD) will not necessarily generalize to downstream tasks, neither of semantic nor of syntactic nature. More generally, we find that optimizing for semantic similarity tasks correlates with worse performance on syntactic tasks. By conducting an explanatory analysis on the dataset, we find that the call graph-based enhancements significantly enhance the robustness of embeddings, particularly in scenarios where the initial models struggle. Furthermore, we observe that the added context is more beneficial for namespace-related functions than for those focused on individual logic, confirming that the call graph can be leveraged most effectively in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [183] [Lossless Tensor Compression as Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02162)
*Jieke Shi,Junda He,Wenjia Jiang,Weifeng Sun,Shidong Pan,Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Peixin Zhang,Yifan Jia,Zhou Yang,Thong Hoang,Xiwei Xu,Zhenchang Xing,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Brevis 将无损张量压缩建模为程序合成问题，通过类型化 DSL 捕捉张量中重复区域和浮点字段等结构，利用生产先验指导有界 A* 搜索合成紧凑的可自解压程序，在 10 个公开检查点上实现 33.93% 的存储缩减，优于 zstd、gzip 等通用压缩器和 ZipNN、DFloat11 等张量专用压缩器，同时达到 3.60 GB/s 压缩和 6.61 GB/s 解压吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型检查点在数量和规模上持续增长，导致存储、传输和部署成本日益高昂。通用压缩器虽能减少存储需求但忽略张量结构，而现有张量专用压缩器依赖固定且格式特定的流水线，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: Brevis 将无损张量压缩形式化为程序合成问题：设计一个类型化领域特定语言（DSL），通过一组可逆算子捕捉反复出现的张量结构（如重复区域和浮点字段）；给定张量后，合成一个自包含的 DSL 程序实现逐位精确重建；从少量代表性张量样本学习的检查点特定生产先验引导有界 A* 搜索，合成紧凑程序，后续可直接执行实现逐位精确解压。

Result: 在覆盖语言、音频和图像生成模型的 10 个公开检查点上，Brevis 将 2.13 TB 数据压缩至 1.41 TB，实现 33.93% 的存储缩减；比 zstd、gzip 等四种通用压缩器生成的小档案缩减最多达 30.87%；生成的档案小于 ZipNN 和 DFloat11 等张量专用压缩器；在实际并发配置下达到 3.60 GB/s 压缩和 6.61 GB/s 解压吞吐，同时保证逐字节保真。

Conclusion: Brevis 通过程序合成而非固定流水线的方式解决了张量无损压缩问题，兼具压缩率和对多种张量结构的自适应能力，在压缩比与吞吐性能上均优于现有通用及张量专用压缩方法，为大规模模型检查点的存储与传输提供了高效且无损的解决方案。

Abstract: Model checkpoints are growing in both number and size, which makes archival, transfer, and deployment increasingly costly. General-purpose compressors can reduce storage requirements but ignore tensor structure, whereas existing tensor-specific compressors rely on fixed and format-specific pipelines. We present Brevis, which formulates lossless tensor compression as program synthesis. We design a typed domain-specific language (DSL) that captures recurring tensor structures, such as repeated regions and floating-point fields, through a set of reversible operators. Given a tensor, Brevis synthesizes a self-contained DSL program that reconstructs it bit-exactly. A checkpoint-specific production prior, learned from a small representative sample of tensors, guides a bounded A* search to synthesize compact programs, which can later be executed directly for bit-exact decompression. On 10 public checkpoints spanning language, audio, and image generation models, Brevis reduces 2.13 TB of checkpoint data to 1.41 TB, a 33.93% storage reduction. It produces archives up to 30.87% smaller than those of four general-purpose compressors, including zstd and gzip, and smaller archives than the tensor-specific compressors ZipNN and DFloat11. Under a practical concurrency configuration, Brevis achieves 3.60 GB/s compression and 6.61 GB/s decompression while preserving every source byte.

</details>


### [184] [ECLAIR: A Causally-Grounded AI Framework for Scientific Discovery in Empirical Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02323)
*Alejandro Velasco,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Dipin Khati,David N. Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ECLAIR框架，将大语言模型作为科学代理集成到软件工程实证研究的各个阶段（从假设生成到分析解释），以因果推断原则为基础并采用人在环路设计，通过案例研究展示了LLM在因果推理支持下如何为软件现象提供原则性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域长期依赖科学方法来指导实证研究，但现代软件系统的复杂性常常阻碍科学方法的系统性应用。需要一种结构化的方法论，将LLM的能力融入科学过程，同时通过因果推理和人机协同来防范自动化推理不健全的风险。

Method: ECLAIR是一个以因果推断为基础的AI框架，将LLM作为活跃的"科学代理"，贯穿科学过程的每个阶段（从假设生成到分析与解释）。框架采用人在环路设计以保障推理的可靠性。通过案例研究（考察提示设计对两个LLM代码生成准确性的影响）进行验证，分析指令风格、长少量样本和签名增强提示等因素的因果效应。

Result: 案例研究结果表明，在两种LLM中，指令式提示、更长的少量样本提示和签名增强提示对代码生成准确性均产生较小的负向因果效应。这展示了因果推理如何为解释软件现象发生的原因提供原则性基础。

Conclusion: ECLAIR是首个在软件工程中将LLM嵌入科学方法的因果驱动结构化方法论，围绕经验SE研究的知识论需求设计，为严谨的AI辅助研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The scientific method has long guided empirical research in Software Engineering (SE), but the complexity of modern software systems often hinders its systematic application. This paper introduces _ECLAIR_, a causally grounded AI framework that integrates Large Language Models (_LLMs_) into every stage of the scientific process, from hypothesis generation to analysis and interpretation. _ECLAIR_ treats _LLMs_ as active **scientific agents** operating under the principles of causal inference, within a human-in-the-loop design that safeguards against the risks of unsound automated reasoning. We demonstrate the framework through a case study examining how prompt design influences code generation accuracy in two _LLMs_. Results show that, for both models, instruction-style, longer few-shot, and signature-augmented prompts yield small negative causal effects on accuracy, illustrating how causal reasoning provides a principled foundation for explaining _why_ software phenomena occur. This study presents the first causally grounded structured methodology for embedding _LLMs_ within the scientific method in SE, designed around the epistemological demands of empirical SE research, establishing a basis for rigorous AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [185] [On Automated and Explainable Provenance of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02329)
*Alejandro Velasco,Nathan Wintersgill,Trevor Stalnaker,Oscar Chaparro,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个研究愿景，主张下一代代码生成AI工具必须建立在"可解释来源追踪"（explainable provenance）的基础上，通过自动化的事后追溯机制，将生成的代码与其提示组件、训练数据实例、全局数据特征及内部模型组件之间的因果关系链接起来，以解决AI生成代码来源不透明的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成AI虽极大改变了软件开发流程，却带来关键透明度问题：AI生成代码的来源对开发者、部署组织及合规专业人员均不透明。现有缓解措施仅能事后标记问题输出，无法解释模型为何产生该输出，也无法指导未来生成的改进，亟需可解释的溯源机制。

Method: 该研究以美国NSF资助的研究项目为基础，通过实证研究（针对软件开发人员、模型用户和合规/法律专业人员）证明溯源信息的实际必要性；提出四个可追溯性维度来刻画问题；设计结合大规模实证研究与事后因果分析和可解释性技术的研究方案。

Result: 基于实证证据表明现有工具缺失溯源信息是实际痛点；系统化地表征了四个可追溯性维度；勾勒了涵盖大规模实证研究与事后因果/可解释性技术的综合研究计划，并识别出实现该愿景所面临的关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 下一代CodeGenAI工具应构建在可解释溯源的基础之上，这是满足开发者、组织和合规需求的实际必要条件。作者呼吁社区共同解决所识别的开放挑战，以将这一愿景变为现实。

Abstract: Generative AI for code generation has transformed software development, but it has also introduced a critical transparency problem: the origins of AI-generated code are opaque to the developers who use it, the organizations that deploy it, and the compliance professionals responsible for ensuring its legal and quality standards. Existing mitigations flag problematic outputs after the fact without explaining why a model produced them or how future generation could be improved. We present a research vision, grounded in a U.S. NSF-funded research grant, that argues that the next generation of CodeGenAI tools must be built on a foundation of explainable provenance: automated, post-hoc traceability that links generated code back to the prompt components, training data instances, global data features, and internal model components that caused its generation. We grounded this vision in empirical evidence from studies of software developers, model users, and compliance/legal professionals, which show that provenance information is a practical necessity that current tools do not provide. We characterize the problem across four traceability dimensions, outline a research program combining large-scale empirical studies with post-hoc causal and interpretability techniques, and identify the key open challenges that the community must address to realize this vision.

</details>


### [186] [Profitability of Open-Source Software Product Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02398)
*Shivendu P. Singh,Narayan Ramasubbu,Chris F. Kemerer,M. Zia Hydari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于2001至2025年间977家美国高科技企业的数据，发现开源产品开发平均可将企业毛利率提升4-5%，其收益主要通过劳动力生产率提高实现，且需要外部志愿者贡献达到约35%的阈值并依赖企业资源配置，尤其是与持续内部研发相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科技企业在开放平台上（如GitHub）邀请外部开发者参与产品开发，这种开放性在产品开发中是否真正有经济回报是一个尚未得到充分实证检验的重要问题。

Method: 对2001至2025年间977家美国高科技企业进行实证分析，考察开源采用对企业毛利率的影响，并分析其作用机制（劳动力生产率）、外部贡献阈值条件（约35%）以及企业资源配置的调节作用，同时采用组态分析方法识别高盈利路径。

Result: 开源采用平均使企业毛利率提高4-5%；收益主要通过更高劳动力生产率实现；收益仅在外部志愿者贡献占比约35%时出现；存在多种盈利路径，但与持续内部研发配对是所有高盈利组态的核心里昂条件。

Conclusion: 对管理者而言，开源应被视为与企业资源相匹配的知识整合战略，而非单纯的低成本外部劳动力来源；对政策制定者而言，应重视开源生态系统的经济价值，并制定政策促进技能型志愿者社区和公私开源基础设施建设。

Abstract: Many technology firms now build software products in the open, inviting outside developers to contribute alongside their employees on platforms like GitHub. Does this openness in product development pay off? Analyzing 977 U.S. high-tech firms from 2001 to 2025, this study finds that open-source adoption raised firms' gross margins by 4-5% on average. These gains flow largely through higher labor productivity, as firms integrate external contributors' diverse knowledge into internal workflows, broadening the organizational knowledge base without a commensurate rise in labor costs. However, the payoff emerges only when outside volunteers supply a meaningful share of the work (around 35% in this sample), and hinges on the firm's resource configuration. While there are multiple pathways to profitability, pairing open-source product development with sustained internal R&D is a core condition present in all high-profitability configurations. For managers, the lesson is to treat open source as a knowledge-integration strategy matched to the firm's resources, not merely a means of sourcing external effort at lower cost. For policymakers, the findings underscore the broad economic value of open-source ecosystems and the need for policies that foster skilled volunteer communities and public-private open-source infrastructure.

</details>


### [187] [SWE-Touch: Benchmarking Coding Agents When Users Touch the Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02499)
*Yuqiao Tan,Jinxiang Meng,Fangyu Lei,Minzheng Wang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Touch框架，通过注入经过验证的"反编辑"(Counter-Edits)来压力测试编码智能体在共享工作区中理解与响应代码变更的能力。在SWE-bench Verified及两个长时程基准上评估九种编码模型，发现反编辑使平均解决率下降7.7个百分点，失败原因归结为智能体对工作区演化的感知不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级基准通常在智能体独立工作或用户仅以消息形式参与的条件下评估编码智能体，但真实软件开发中用户可能在工作区中直接查看和修改代码。这种共享工作区场景下的智能体能力尚未被系统评估，因此需要设计一个专门压力测试该场景的框架。

Method: SWE-Touch框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从多条修复轨迹中挖掘任务关键区域；(2) 使用独立的"用户补丁生成器"(User Patch Generator)构建与任务完成相冲突但合理的编辑；(3) 当智能体到达相关代码时，注入带有上下文用户消息的反编辑。在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE上对九个编码模型进行系统评估。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，反编辑使平均解决率下降7.7个百分点，且在SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE两个长时程基准上也持续存在性能退化。轨迹分析揭示失败原因：智能体可能保留冲突代码，或替换代码但未充分重新检查仓库，也未用针对性测试验证修订后的代码。

Conclusion: 强大的自主性能并不能保证共享工作区协作所需的实时状态感知和自适应行为。未来优化的关键能力包括：检测工作区变更、协调冲突编辑与任务目标、以及验证受影响行为。

Abstract: Real-world software development requires coding agents to operate in shared workspaces where users may inspect and modify code during an ongoing task, yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate agents working alone or restrict user participation to messages. This leads us to ask: how do coding agents understand and respond to code changes in a shared workspace? We introduce SWE-Touch, a framework that stress-tests this setting through validated Counter-Edits: plausible edits to task-relevant code that conflict with task completion. SWE-Touch mines task-critical regions from multiple repair trajectories, uses a separate User Patch Generator to construct the edits, and injects them with contextual user messages when agents reach the relevant code. We evaluate nine coding models on SWE-bench Verified, with additional experiments on longer-horizon tasks from SWE-Bench Pro and DeepSWE. Counter-Edit lowers average resolve rate by 7.7 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, with degradation also persisting on both longer-horizon benchmarks. Trajectory analysis links these failures to limited awareness of the evolving workspace: agents may retain conflicting code or replace it without sufficiently re-inspecting the repository and validating the revised code with targeted tests. These findings show that strong autonomous performance does not yet ensure the state awareness and adaptive behavior needed for shared-workspace collaboration, and point to detecting workspace changes, reconciling conflicting edits with the task, and verifying the affected behavior as key capabilities for future optimization.

</details>


### [188] [Decomposing the Doer Effect in Programming Practice: Code Writing Stands Out Among Active Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02541)
*Arun Balajiee Lekshmi Narayanan,Gillian Gold,Jordan Barria-Pineda,Quinn K Wolter,Peter Brusilovsky,Paulo Carvalho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探究"行动者效应"是否适用于计算机科学教学，以及不同类型的主动练习是否效果不同。基于334名学生在11个学期的Java课程日志数据，发现主动练习的效果是被动活动的3.2倍，其中代码编写是最强的预测因子，其他活动类型没有显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"行动者效应"文献将"做"统一归类为主动练习，但未区分不同形式主动练习的效果差异。作者希望探究是否某些主动练习类型（如代码编写、代码追踪、代码补全等）比其他类型更有效。

Method: 分析了334名学生在11个学期的Java入门与中级课程中使用交互式练习系统的日志数据。该系统包含五种内容类型：代码编写、代码追踪、代码补全、代码可视化和代码解释。通过对比主动练习与被动的学习结果关联性，并对不同主动练习类型进行区分分析。

Result: 研究发现主动练习活动与学习效果的关联是被动活动的3.2倍。在主动练习中，代码编写与后测成绩提升的关联最强，而其他活动类型未表现出可比的关联性。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了具有挑战性且伴有反馈支持的练习活动（如代码编写问题）在教学中的重要性，扩展了"行动者效应"在计算机科学教育中的应用，并细化了对不同主动练习形式效果差异的理解。

Abstract: The "doer effect" suggests that actively doing practice activities is more strongly associated with learning outcomes than passively viewing content. In the doer effect literature, "doing" refers specifically to active practice. However, this categorization treats different forms of active practice as equivalent, leaving open whether some types of active practice are more effective than others. In this paper, we investigate whether the doer effect extends to computer science instruction and whether some forms of doing stand out compared to other forms. We analyze log data from 334 students across 11 semesters of introductory and intermediate Java who used an interactive practice system with five content types: Code Writing, Code Tracing, Code Completion, Code Visualizations, and Code Explanations. Consistent with prior doer effect work, we find that active practice activities were associated with 3.2 times better learning outcomes than passive activities. Interestingly, among the active practice, code writing was the most strongly associated with improved posttest performance, while no other activity type showed a comparable association. These results highlight the importance of challenging, feedback-supported practice activities, such as code writing problems.

</details>


### [189] [ACEM: A Cost Estimation Model for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02582)
*Mohammad El-Ramly*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ACEM（Agentic成本估算模型），针对自主AI代理主导开发的新范式，将开发总成本分解为LLM令牌消耗、HITL监督和基础设施三个可加维度，并引入Revision Factor、Context Factor和HITL Intensity Score三个新构念，将传统规模度量（如用例点、故事点、功能点）映射到令牌消耗以复用既有项目的范围数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件成本估算模型（如COCOMO II、功能点、故事点）假设开发工作量主要由设计、编码和测试中的人力驱动。然而，在Agentic软件工程中，自主AI代理承担大量实现工作，产生了传统模型中不存在的成本维度：LLM令牌消耗、人机协同监督成本及代理编排基础设施成本。这些成本具有非确定性特征——相同任务可能消耗不同令牌、遵循不同推理路径、需要不同程度的纠偏，传统估算框架无法捕捉这些新现象，亟需新框架来弥合标准规模度量与这种新成本结构之间的鸿沟。

Method: ACEM将总成本分解为三个可加维度：LLM成本、HITL成本和基础设施成本。它引入三个新构念：(1) Revision Factor (RF)，建模输出拒绝和重试导致的令牌开销；(2) Context Factor (CF)，捕捉上下文积累导致令牌消耗上升的效应；(3) HITL Intensity Score (HIS)，一个四级监督分类方案。此外，ACEM将用例点、故事点和功能点映射到预估令牌消耗量，使组织能够复用已有的项目范围数据。

Result: ACEM作为一个完整指定的模型结构和校准方法论被提出，常量保留为符号形式等待实证数据填充。作为早期提案，论文邀请研究社区使用真实项目数据进行校准、测试和扩展。

Conclusion: 本文是一个开放性的早期框架提案，没有提供实证结果，其核心贡献在于提出了适应Agentic开发范式的成本估算概念框架，并预留校准和验证的空间供社区后续完善。

Abstract: Traditional software cost estimation models, such as COCOMO II, Function Points, and Story Points, assume that development effort is primarily driven by human labor in design, coding, and testing. Agentic software engineering, where autonomous AI agents perform substantial implementation work and humans focus on planning, specification, and validation, challenges this assumption. New cost dimensions arise: large language model (LLM) token consumption across agent actions, Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) oversight effort, and infrastructure costs for agent orchestration and tooling. These costs are nondeterministic: identical tasks may consume different tokens, follow divergent reasoning paths, and require varying human correction, phenomena absent in traditional development. A new framework is needed to bridge standard sizing metrics with this cost structure.
  This paper proposes ACEM (Agentic Cost Estimation Model), which decomposes total agentic development cost into three additive dimensions: LLM, HITL, and infrastructure cost. ACEM introduces three constructs for agentic dynamics: the Revision Factor (RF), modeling token overhead from output rejection and retries; the Context Factor (CF), capturing rising token consumption as context accumulates; and the HITL Intensity Score (HIS), a four-level oversight classification scheme. It further maps Use Case Points, Story Points, and Function Points to estimated token consumption, enabling organizations to reuse existing project-scoping data for agentic cost forecasting.
  ACEM is presented as a fully specified model structure and calibration methodology, with constants left symbolic pending empirical grounding. As an early-stage proposal, it invites the research community to calibrate, test, and extend the model through real project data.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Hierarchical BM25: Lexical Search at Billion-Document Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00229)
*Umesh Deshpande,Swaminathan Sundararaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化BM25（Hierarchical BM25）索引方法，通过粗粒度索引选定约1K个主题均衡的文档组，再在选定的组内进行穷举搜索，以解决十亿级文档规模下精确词法检索的延迟和内存瓶颈问题。该方法将常驻内存从约400GB降至约4.4GB（与语料规模无关），16词查询在十亿文档上约300ms完成，吞吐量提升4.7-5.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿级文档的平面BM25索引需要约400GB内存或每次查询4-12秒的磁盘访问，无法在交互延迟预算内实现精确的top-k词法检索。现有方案要么内存随语料规模线性增长，要么磁盘延迟过高，都需要新的方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用层次化结构：常驻粗索引将全部文档划分为约1K个主题相关、大小均衡的文档组。选择访问哪些组依据两个信号：(1)每个查询词在组内的总词频；(2)对于分布过于分散、词频总和无法充分反映信息的词项，检查多个词是否同时出现在同一文档中。选定组后进行穷举搜索，使用约100KB的全局统计信息评分，保证返回分数与平面索引完全一致，近似仅存在于组选择阶段。

Result: 常驻内存约4.4GB且与语料规模无关；十亿文档上的16词查询约300ms完成（吞吐量为平面多线程索引的4.7-5.6倍）；缓存预热后每秒约32次查询，而平面索引不到3次。50万文档配置下，访问5-10%的聚类可恢复穷举结果分数的0.83-0.92。十亿规模的召回率和与文档重排序BlockMax-WAND的直接比较尚待研究。

Conclusion: 层次化BM25通过将近似限制在组选择阶段而非评分阶段，在内存恒定（~4.4GB，与语料规模无关）的前提下大幅提升了交互式检索的吞吐量，为十亿级词法检索提供了一种实用的权衡方案。未来工作包括验证十亿规模召回率以及与传统高性能基线BlockMax-WAND的直接对比。

Abstract: A flat BM25 index over one billion documents occupies about 400 GB. Holding it in memory requires DRAM proportional to corpus size. Serving it from disk takes 4-12 seconds per query. Exact top-k lexical retrieval at this scale is therefore impractical within an interactive latency budget.
  Hierarchical BM25 gives up exact ranking in exchange for fixed bounds on memory and latency. A resident coarse index selects which of ~1K topical, size-balanced document groups a query visits, using two signals: the total frequency of each query term within a group, and, for informative terms spread too thinly across groups for frequency totals to reflect, whether several of them appear together in one document. Selected groups are then searched exhaustively and scored against ~100 KB of global statistics. Every returned score therefore equals the flat index's score, and the approximation is confined to selection alone. The resident footprint is ~4.4 GB, independent of corpus size. Sixteen-term queries over one billion documents return in ~300 ms (4.7x to 5.6x the throughput of a flat multi-threaded index), and a warmed cache sustains ~32 queries per second versus under 3 for flat indexing. At a 500K-document configuration, visiting 5-10% of clusters recovers 0.83-0.92 of the exhaustive result score. Billion-scale recall and a direct comparison against document-reordered BlockMax-WAND remain open.

</details>


### [191] [CeQe: Grounding Lexical Retrieval in Semantic Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00452)
*Adam Kahirov,Umesh Deshpande,Swaminathan Sundararaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Encoder Query Expansion (CE-QE)方法，利用交叉编码器对语义检索结果的token级归因来提取关键词，并将其附加到BM25查询上进行查询扩展，从而弥补词法检索的语义词汇鸿沟问题。该方法在七个BEIR数据集上显著提升了词法召回率，且不修改底层BM25索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BM25等词法检索方法仅依赖精确关键词匹配，当相关文档使用与查询不同的措辞表达同一答案时，BM25无法召回这些文档，而后置的排序或融合阶段也无法恢复从未进入候选集的文档。现有方案各有缺陷：伪相关反馈复用BM25自身可能有误的结果导致查询漂移；生成式查询扩展（如HyDE、Query2doc）依赖大语言模型的参数知识，可能引入语料库中不存在的词汇。

Method: CE-QE的工作流程为：首先将查询送入语义检索器获取top结果，然后使用交叉编码器对查询和这些结果逐token计算相关性归因，从中选出交叉编码器认为关键的词汇，将其逐字附加到BM25查询中进行扩展。关键设计在于：扩展词完全从已检索到的段落中逐字复制，确保不引入语料库之外的词汇；扩展种子来自语义检索结果而非BM25自身结果，避免自我强化的查询漂移；额外开销仅为混合pipeline中本就运行的交叉编码器的归因提取。

Result: 在七个BEIR数据集上，CE-QE显著提升了查询和答案词汇出现分歧时的词法召回率（例如NQ数据集Recall@100从0.32提升至0.47）。其分数融合变体SESF在Recall@100上比交叉编码器分数融合高出2.5%，在nDCG@10上分别比SPLADEv2和ColBERTv2高出5.3%和4.6%，且底层BM25索引完全无需修改。

Conclusion: CE-QE通过利用交叉编码器的token级归因进行词法查询扩展，以极低的额外成本有效弥合了词法与语义检索之间的词汇鸿沟，同时避免了伪相关反馈的查询漂移和生成式扩展的语料外词汇注入问题，是一种轻量且有效的混合检索增强方案。

Abstract: Lexical retrieval (BM25) captures exact keyword matches and weights terms by corpus-wide significance, but it is blind to the semantic vocabulary gap: when a relevant document phrases an answer differently from the query, BM25 never retrieves it, and no amount of downstream reranking or fusion can recover a document that was never in the candidate set. We present Cross-Encoder Query Expansion (CE-QE), which reads the per-token relevance attributions of a cross-encoder applied to top semantic search results, selects the terms the cross-encoder treats as decisive, and appends them to the BM25 query. Unlike classical pseudo-relevance feedback, which reuses BM25's own (possibly wrong) top results, CE-QE seeds expansion from the semantic retriever's results, avoiding self-reinforcing query drift. Unlike recent generative query expansion (HyDE, Query2doc), which prompts a large language model to hallucinate text from its parametric knowledge, every CE-QE expansion term is copied verbatim from a retrieved passage, so it cannot introduce vocabulary the corpus does not contain, and its only added cost is attribution extraction on a cross-encoder a hybrid pipeline already runs for reranking. On seven BEIR datasets, CE-QE improves lexical recall substantially where query and answer vocabulary diverge (e.g., NQ Recall@100 from 0.32 to 0.47), and its score-fusion variant (SESF) beats cross-encoder score fusion by 2.5% on Recall@100 and beats SPLADEv2 and ColBERTv2 by 5.3% and 4.6% on nDCG@10, while leaving the underlying BM25 index completely unmodified.

</details>


### [192] [A Context-Aware Cultural Heritage Guide Powered by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00549)
*Liliana Ardissono,Fabio Ferrero,Angelo Geninatti Cossatin,Claudio Mattutino,Noemi Mauro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Triangolazioni（一个文化遗产Web应用）的扩展，通过松散耦合、与LLM无关的架构，利用大语言模型为策展内容补充上下文相关的外部信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产Web应用中的策展内容往往缺乏上下文相关的外部信息支持，需要一种灵活的方式将大语言模型的能力集成到现有系统中，以丰富用户的浏览体验。

Method: 采用松散耦合（loosely-coupled）的架构扩展Triangolazioni系统，该架构与具体LLM无关（agnostic），支持上下文相关的信息搜索和呈现。系统可以灵活接入不同的大语言模型，而不需要对核心系统进行大规模改动。

Result: 成功实现了对Triangolazioni的扩展，使其能够利用LLM补充上下文相关的外部信息，同时保持了架构的灵活性和对具体LLM的无关性。

Conclusion: 通过松散耦合、LLM无关的架构设计，可以有效将大语言模型的能力集成到文化遗产Web应用中，实现上下文相关的信息增强，同时保持系统的可扩展性和可维护性。

Abstract: We present an extension of Triangolazioni (a Cultural Heritage webapp) to enrich curated content with context-dependent, external information provided by Large Language Models (LLMs) within a loosely-coupled architecture agnostic to the LLM. The system supports context-dependent information search and presentation within an architecture agnostic to the exploited LLM.

</details>


### [193] [PHA-Net: Prototype-based Hierarchical Alignment Network for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00551)
*Xiaolun Jing,Kezhao Yin,Xinxing Yang,Genke Yang,Jian Chu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原型的分层对齐网络（PHA-Net），用于解决文本-视频检索中跨模态语义对齐问题。该方法通过模态共享原型作为桥接，高效优化文本和视频表示，并引入原型支持的分词合并模块和原型对比损失，在四个基准数据集上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最佳文本-视频检索方法需要在个体/局部/全局三个级别同时对跨模态语义进行对齐，但存在两个关键问题：1）简短文本与丰富视频之间固有的语义不匹配；2）传统分层框架仅优化视觉表示且计算成本高昂。此外，聚类token中不平衡的语义分布会损害检索性能，弱语义token对检索无所助益。

Method: PHA-Net包含三个核心组件：1）引入多个模态共享原型作为跨模态对齐的桥梁，同时高效优化文本和视频表示；2）提出原型支持的token合并模块，利用原型语义指导增强强语义token并抑制弱语义token；3）设计原型对比损失，确保相同原型下的文本和视觉原型相似度高于不同原型之间的相似度，从而促使文本和视觉原型关注不同的语义信息。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上验证了PHA-Net的有效性：MSR-VTT（8.8%）、ActivityNet（19.2%）、VATEX（0.7%）和Charades（4.9%）的召回总和均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过原型桥接、token合并和原型对比损失的协同设计，PHA-Net有效解决了文本-视频检索中的跨模态语义对齐和语义分布不平衡问题，同时兼顾了计算效率和检索性能。

Abstract: With the emergence of large-scale image-text pre-training models, e.g., CLIP, text-video retrieval has experienced substantial advances in recent years. Existing best-performing methods involve aligning cross-modal semantics at individual, local, and global levels simultaneously, raising concerns about the intrinsic semantic mismatch between concise texts and rich videos. A canonical approach is to integrate multiple language-video attention modules into the hierarchical framework while this paradigm only optimizes visual representations with prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a new prototype-based hierarchical alignment network (PHA-Net) to align individual/local/global level representations across modalities. Concretely, we introduce multiple modality-shared prototypes as the bridge to efficiently optimize text and video representations for cross-modal alignment. Then, we argue that the imbalanced semantic distribution in clustered tokens may undermine retrieval performance, as tokens with weak semantics are of little interest. To reduce the impact of these tokens, a proposed prototype-supported token merge module is responsible for enhancing tokens with strong semantics and suppressing others with weak semantics via prototype semantics guidance. Moreover, we devise a prototype contrastive loss to encourage textual and visual prototypes to focus on different semantic information. The idea of this auxiliary loss is to ensure higher similarity between textual and visual prototypes from the same prototype than those from different prototypes. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks confirm the effectiveness of our PHA-Net, which achieves significant improvements in the sum of all recalls on MSR-VTT (8.8%), ActivityNet (19.2%), VATEX (0.7%), and Charades (4.9%). Code is available at https://github.com/JingXiaolun/PHA-Net.

</details>


### [194] [GARDRec: Decision-Level Graph Grounding for Large Language Model Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00669)
*Yong Wang,Hongliang Sun,Jinlan Liu,Hua Zhang,Dianbo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GARDRec，一种图增强的大语言模型推荐框架，通过构建语义-结构项目表征、个性化图上下文和连续多模态提示，将结构化用户-项目关系注入冻结LLM，提升下一项目推荐的排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图增强的LLM推荐系统主要将知识图谱作为提示层的证据，排序决策缺乏结构化用户-项目关系的强约束。这在下一项目推荐中尤为关键，因为模型需要在相同用户上下文下比较候选项目，同时保留时间偏好、协作信号和属性匹配信息。

Method: GARDRec包含四个核心组件：(1) 从文本节点特征和图传播构建语义-结构项目表征；(2) 从时间加权历史和一阶邻域推导个性化图上下文；(3) 通过连续多模态提示将图派生表征与冻结LLM对齐；(4) 通过后期决策分支注入显式交互和匹配特征，并利用候选间注意力和受限生成似然支持最终排序。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据及多个LLM骨干网络上进行实验，GARDRec在候选排序性能上普遍优于代表性基线方法。消融和诊断分析验证了图投影、邻域检索、显式决策特征、排序损失和生成校准各自的贡献。

Conclusion: GARDRec将图结构信息与LLM能力有效结合，通过结构化图约束来增强LLM的排序决策，为图增强LLM推荐提供了新的框架方向，同时各模块的消融分析证明了设计选择的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for recommendation by interpreting item descriptions, user instructions, and external knowledge through natural-language prompts. However, existing graph-augmented LLM recommenders often use knowledge graphs mainly as prompt-level evidence, leaving ranking decisions weakly constrained by structured user-item relations. This is problematic for next-item recommendation, where the model must compare candidates under the same user context while preserving temporal preference, collaborative signals, and attribute matches. To address this issue, we propose \emph{GARDRec}, a Graph-grounded Adaptive Reasoning and Decision-aware Recommendation framework for LLM-based next-item ranking. GARDRec constructs semantic-structural item representations from textual node features and graph propagation, derives personalized graph contexts from temporally weighted histories and first-order neighborhoods, and aligns graph-derived representations with a frozen LLM through continuous multimodal prompts. Explicit interaction and matching features are injected through late-stage decision branches, while inter-candidate attention and restricted generative likelihood support final ranking. Experiments on three public benchmarks with multiple LLM backbones show that GARDRec generally improves candidate-ranking performance over representative baselines. Ablation and diagnostic analyses verify the contributions of graph projection, neighborhood retrieval, explicit decision features, ranking loss, and generative calibration.

</details>


### [195] [A Triple-Robustness Analysis of Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Multi-Hop Requirements Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00705)
*Meftun Akarsu,Burak Özdemir,Doğancan Büyükçolak,Recep Kaan Karaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对GraphRAG与向量RAG结论不一致的问题，提出了包含嵌入器、语料库、评估器（及引用质量测量点）在内的三重稳健性分析框架，通过约1万次运行和5000余次忠实性判断，系统揭示了架构排序随测量点翻转、答案级引用胜负具有嵌入器稳健性但依赖语料与层位、判断器不稳定等关键发现，并论证了RAG架构结论需在此类稳健性水平下方可采信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有GraphRAG与向量RAG对比研究的结论相互矛盾，其证据通常绑定单一语料库、单一嵌入器和单一评估器，且忽略了引用质量测量位置的影响。作者旨在通过系统性的多维度稳健性分析，解释既往分歧来源，并为RAG架构评估建立更严格的实验标准。

Method: 采用三重稳健性分析：固定五流水线架构矩阵，分别变化嵌入器（本地e5-small vs Azure text-embedding-3-small）、语料库（DO-178C类别边需求 vs 通过MuSiQue构建的维基百科段落链）和评估器（GPT-5.4 x GPT-4.1配对，在双语料上评估），共进行2×4,440主矩阵运行、600次跨语料运行和5000余次忠实性判断；同时用稠密嵌入训练路由器进行跳数分类，并开展判断器自一致性（κ）和跨时段重判稳定性测试。

Result: (C2a) GraphRAG图遍历在精确率0.12-0.23时淹没上下文窗口，但合成器选择性引用精确率达0.48-0.65；将检索集合计为归因集合会反转架构排名，部分解释了既往报告分歧。(C1) 答案级引用胜者依语料和层位而定但稳健于嵌入器：GraphRAG在短跳DO-178C查询上与baseline持平，在所有MuSiQue层位获胜，代理流水线仅在3跳以上需求查询领先。(C2b) 忠实性依语料而定：DO-178C上随跳距下降（四个判断器×嵌入器组合中三个趋势p<0.05），Wikipedia链上无塌陷。(C3) 单一LLM判断器忠实性对检索状态脆弱：GPT-5.4跨嵌入器自κ仅0.137（41%判定变化），而同日重测下限为0.76；冻结输入十一周后重判，两判断器κ≤0.14。(C4) 基于稠密嵌入的学习路由器在跳数分类上达到宏F1 0.86。

Conclusion: RAG架构结论必须在多重稳健性检验——包括对引用测量位置的稳健性——通过之后才应被采信；本文揭示的测量点翻转、语料条件性、嵌入器稳健性与判断器脆弱性说明，单语料单评估器的对比研究不足以支撑可靠的架构排名结论。

Abstract: Reported verdicts on GraphRAG versus vector RAG disagree, and the evidence is typically tied to a single corpus, embedder, and judge -- and, we show, to where citation quality is measured. We present a triple-robustness analysis that holds a five-pipeline architecture matrix fixed and varies embedder (local e5-small vs. Azure text-embedding-3-small), corpus (DO-178C typed-edge requirements vs. Wikipedia paragraph chains via MuSiQue), and judge (paired GPT-5.4 x GPT-4.1 on both corpora), over 2x4,440 main-matrix runs, 600 cross-corpus runs, and over 5,000 faithfulness judgments. (C2a) GraphRAG's graph walk floods the context window at precision 0.12-0.23, but the synthesizer cites selectively at precision 0.48-0.65; scoring the retrieved set as the attribution set inverts the architecture ranking, which reconciles part of the disagreement in prior reports. (C1) Answer-level citation winners are corpus- and stratum-conditional but embedder-robust: GraphRAG ties vanilla on short-hop DO-178C queries and wins every MuSiQue stratum, while agentic pipelines lead only on 3+-hop requirements queries. (C2b) Faithfulness is corpus-conditional: on DO-178C it declines with hop distance (trend p<0.05 in three of four judge x embedder combinations); on Wikipedia chains neither judge shows a collapse. (C3) Single-judge LLM faithfulness is fragile to retrieval state: GPT-5.4's self-kappa across embedders is 0.137 (41% verdict change) against a same-day test-retest floor of 0.76, and re-judging frozen inputs eleven weeks later gives kappa <= 0.14 for both judges. A learned router on dense embeddings alone reaches macro-F1 0.86 on hop classification (C4). We argue that RAG architecture claims should be tested at this level of robustness -- including robustness to the citation-measurement point -- before they are trusted.

</details>


### [196] [Hierarchical Residual Policy Optimization for Generative Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00750)
*Kaifeng Guo,Yiming Yang,Jingtong Gao,Guolei Zeng,Fukang Yang,Yukang Liang,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HRPO框架，将生成式推荐器的物品级反馈转化为稠密、令牌对齐的层级残差信用信号，通过残差回报策略优化实现令牌级信用分配，在公开数据集和商业系统A/B测试中均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器通过自回归解码语义标识（SIDs）选择物品，但现有的监督式下一令牌预测训练仅模仿日志轨迹，不直接优化下游效用。后训练方法面临的关键挑战是：日志反馈仅在最终曝光物品上观测到，导致大多数方法在物品层面操作，将同一终局信号广播到所有SID令牌上，造成令牌级信用分配稀疏、高方差且依赖网络层的问题。

Method: HRPO框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 通过基于特征用户聚类的组级奖励平滑，估计SID前缀级效用；(2) 将前缀效用分解为残差令牌信用，并累积为credit-to-go信号；(3) 采用残差回报策略优化（RRPO），使用裁剪更新、组归一化优势函数和KL正则化约束来优化残差信用，保证训练稳定性。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验以及大规模商业系统的在线A/B测试中，HRPO与会话级效用和关键业务指标上均表现出持续一致的提升。

Conclusion: HRPO有效解决了生成式推荐器后训练中的令牌级信用分配问题，通过将稀疏物品级反馈转化为稠密、令牌对齐的学习信号，实现了保守的令牌级策略改进，且代码和人工制品已开源供复现。

Abstract: Generative recommenders select items by autoregressively decoding semantic identifiers (SIDs), whose token positions induce a coarse-to-fine hierarchy over the item space. In practice, SID decoders are trained via supervised next-token prediction, which imitates logged trajectories rather than directly optimizing downstream utility. This motivates post-training with outcome feedback to guide decoding toward higher utility. However, logged feedback is only observed for the final exposed item, causing most post-training methods to operate at the item level and broadcast the same terminal signal across all SID tokens. As a result, token-level credit assignment becomes sparse, high-variance, and layer-dependent. To this end, we propose Hierarchical Residual Policy Optimization (HRPO), a post-training framework that converts item-level outcomes into dense, token-aligned learning signals for conservative token-wise improvement. Specifically, HRPO first estimates SID prefix-level utilities via group-wise reward smoothing over feature-based user clusters. It then decomposes these utilities into residual token credits and accumulates them into credit-to-go signals. Finally, Residual-Return Policy Optimization (RRPO) optimizes the residual credits using clipped updates, group-normalized advantages, and KL regularization to preserve stability. Experiments on a public dataset and an online A/B test in a large-scale commercial system show consistent gains in session-level utility and key business metrics. Source code and the archived artifact are available for reproduction.

</details>


### [197] [Exponential Reward Weighting for Fine-Tuning Generative Recommenders under Sparse and Noisy Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00816)
*Keertana Chidambaram,Sanath Kumar Krishnamurthy,Qiuling Xu,Ko-Jen Hsiao,Moumita Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出指数奖励加权微调（Exp-RSFT），直接对日志奖励进行优化并以温度参数λ正则化噪声，理论证明其次优性可分解为覆盖代价和噪声代价，λ在两者间平衡实现最优权衡，在多个基准和工业数据集上优于PPO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中用户反馈稀疏且嘈杂，后训练的生成式推荐器面临两大挑战：基于日志交互训练奖励模型难以泛化，而直接优化不完美的奖励会导致奖励过度优化。现有方法如PPO和DPO常因过度优化不可靠的奖励模型而降低推荐质量。

Method: 提出Exp-RSFT方法，核心思想是对每条日志交互施加exp(r/λ)权重进行直接奖励优化，温度参数λ作为对奖励噪声的正则化器。理论上证明次优性可分解为覆盖代价（源于日志策略的局限性）和噪声代价（源于不完美反馈），并通过λ平衡二者。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个大规模工业数据集上验证：性能随λ呈现倒U型趋势，与理论预测一致。Exp-RSFT持续提升排序性能，而PPO和DPO常因过度优化不可靠的奖励模型而降低推荐质量。该方法无需在线探索或偏好数据。

Conclusion: Exp-RSFT提供了一种无需学习奖励模型即可直接优化日志奖励的有效方案，通过温度参数λ实现覆盖代价与噪声代价的最优权衡，在生成式推荐的后训练中展现出更强的鲁棒性和更好的推荐性能。

Abstract: In recommendation systems, users interact with only a small fraction of a vast item catalog, producing feedback that is both sparse and noisy. This challenges post-training generative recommenders: reward models trained from logged interactions often fail to generalize, while directly optimizing imperfect rewards can lead to reward over-optimization. We propose Exponential reward-weighted fine-tuning (Exp-RSFT), where each logged interaction is weighted by $\exp(r/λ)$, avoids this failure by optimizing directly on the logged rewards, with the temperature $λ$ regularizing against their noise. We theoretically show that Exp-RSFT's suboptimality decomposes into two costs: a coverage cost arising from limitations of the logging policy and a noise cost from imperfect feedback. The temperature $λ$ balances these competing effects, yielding an optimal tradeoff between exploiting high-reward behavior and robustness to noise. Across three public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, we verify this theoretical prediction: performance follows an inverted-U trend as a function of $λ$, while PPO and DPO often over-optimize unreliable reward models and degrade recommendation quality. Exp-RSFT consistently improves ranking performance without requiring online exploration or preference data.

</details>


### [198] [Tevatron Meets Megatron: Expert-Parallel LLM Reranker Training on an Academic Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00916)
*Zhichao Xu,Xueguang Ma,Shengyao Zhuang,Luyu Gao,Wenqian Ye,Yu Wang,Jamie Callan,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Tevatron 3.0 将 Megatron-Core 训练后端集成到 Tevatron 重排器训练框架中，解决了现有 FSDP/DeepSpeed 后端在大规模 MoE 训练上的不足。实验表明其与 FSDP 质量相当，单节点配置快 22%，支持专家并行训练 30B MoE 重排器，并发现 MoE 重排器在激活参数量减半的同时匹配 dense 8B 的质量且推理吞吐更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代重排技术（billion-scale 交叉编码器、MoE 骨干、蒸馏）的算力需求已超出大多数学术团队的训练基础设施。现有 Tevatron 重排器训练依赖 Hugging Face Trainer + DeepSpeed 或 PyTorch FSDP1，但这些后端缺乏对大规MoE训练的高效支持，因此需要新的训练后端。

Method: 将 Megatron-Core 训练后端集成到 Tevatron 3.0 中，保留原有数据管线、评估流程和 Hugging Face 兼容的检查点。利用专家并行（expert parallelism）支持 MoE 模型训练，支持 LoRA 和全参数微调，并与现有 FSDP 配置进行基准对比。

Result: Megatron 与 FSDP 在数据并行设置下重排器质量和训练效率相当；推荐单节点配置下快 22%；专家并行使训练 30B 参数 Qwen3-30B-A3B MoE 重排器成为可能（FSDP1 不可行）。MoE 重排器匹配 dense 8B 质量，但激活参数量不到一半，且推理吞吐显著更高。

Conclusion: Tevatron 3.0 提供了可复现的大规模 MoE 重排器训练基础设施，填补了学术小组与工业级训练能力之间的鸿沟；MoE 架构在重排任务中展现出优于 dense 模型的效率-质量权衡，框架和检查点将开源发布。

Abstract: Modern reranking recipes---billion-scale cross-encoders, mixture-of-experts (MoE) backbones, and distillation against strong teachers---have outpaced the training infrastructure available to most academic groups. Existing Tevatron reranker training relies on the Hugging Face Trainer with DeepSpeed or PyTorch FSDP1, but these backends lack efficient support for large-scale MoE training. We present Tevatron 3.0, which integrates a Megatron-Core training backend into Tevatron while preserving its data pipeline, evaluation workflow, and Hugging Face-compatible checkpoints. We benchmark existing distributed training configurations against the new backend, showing that Megatron matches FSDP reranker quality and training efficiency under comparable data-parallel settings, is up to 22% faster in the recommended single-node configuration, and supports both LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning. Crucially, expert parallelism enables training a 30B-parameter Qwen3-30B-A3B MoE reranker, which is infeasible with PyTorch FSDP1. Using this framework, we conduct a controlled comparison of MoE versus dense models, LoRA versus full-parameter tuning, and distillation versus contrastive training on BEIR-15 with three first-stage retrievers, and report serving throughput for Hugging Face and vLLM. We find that the MoE reranker matches dense 8B quality while activating less than half as many parameters and achieving substantially higher inference throughput. We will release the framework and trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [199] [GRACE: Generative Recommender Acceleration Engine for Real-Time Ads Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00938)
*Zhou Fang,Yuhang Huang,Ang Zhang,Yihan He,Ruichao Xiao,Chao Li,Yavuz Yetim,Sibyl Yang,Xiaohan Wei,Fei Tian,Liang Wang,Liyuan Li,Nathan Yan,Gaoxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRACE是一个面向广告生成式检索的在线服务系统，通过Generative Target Matching（GTM，基于位图和布隆过滤器的SID前缀个性化过滤）解决广告资格（eligibility）问题，并通过针对宽波束、短序列解码场景重新设计的注意力内核、KV缓存和波束搜索优化，在GH200上将解码器延迟降低11.1倍，满足高吞吐实时广告检索的延迟和算力要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成式推荐系统应用于高流量、实时广告检索面临两大服务挑战：一是资格（eligibility）问题，即每个生成的广告必须满足广告主的受众定向规则；二是算力问题，需要满足严格的延迟和GPU成本要求，同时通过宽波束解码每请求生成数千条广告。现有约束解码在广告级别目标匹配方面的通过率不足，而LLM等大规模模型的推理成本过高。

Method: GRACE包含两大核心设计：（1）Generative Target Matching（GTM），扩展了目录有效约束解码，利用从定向规则派生的位图（bitmask）和布隆过滤器（Bloom filter）匹配器，在Semantic ID（SID）前缀级别进行个性化过滤；（2）针对encoder-decoder Transformer的推理优化，围绕宽波束、短序列解码场景重新设计解码器，包括注意力内核、KV缓存和波束搜索优化，相比FlashAttention这类通用内核更适合该场景。

Result: SID级别的GTM将最终广告级别的目标匹配通过率从仅约束解码的23.55%提升至40.42%。在NVIDIA GH200上，相比FlashAttention-2和FlashAttention-3中更快的一个基线，GRACE将交叉注意力延迟提升68.0倍，自注意力延迟提升23.4-25.8倍（跨解码步数）。综合优化使解码器延迟降低11.1倍，使广告生成式检索保持在延迟和算力要求范围内。

Conclusion: GRACE证明了通过结合SID前缀级的目标匹配过滤与针对宽波束短序列解码场景定制化的推理优化，生成式广告检索可以在工业级高流量场景下同时满足资格约束和实时性能要求。其设计选择encoder-decoder架构而非LLM，在保持轻量化的同时实现了显著的服务优化收益。

Abstract: Productionizing generative recommenders for high-volume, real-time ads retrieval creates two serving challenges: eligibility, ensuring that each generated ad is eligible for the request under the advertiser's audience targeting rules, and compute, which requires meeting strict latency and GPU cost requirements while remaining capable of generating thousands of ads per request with wide-beam decoding. This paper presents GRACE, a serving system for ads generative retrieval that addresses both challenges. For eligibility, GRACE introduces Generative Target Matching (GTM), which extends catalog-valid constrained decoding with personalized filtering over Semantic ID (SID) prefixes using bitmask and Bloom filter matchers derived from targeting rules. SID-level GTM improves final ad-level target matching pass rate from 23.55% to 40.42% over constrained decoding alone. For compute-cost and latency, GRACE targets encoder-decoder Transformers, which are more lightweight than LLMs. It redesigns the decoder around the wide-beam, short-sequence regime, covering attention kernels, KV cache, and beam search optimizations. On NVIDIA GH200, compared with the faster of FlashAttention-2 and FlashAttention-3 baselines, GRACE improves cross-attention latency by 68.0 times and self-attention latency by 23.4-25.8 times across decode steps. Together, these changes reduce decoder latency by 11.1 times, keeping ads generative retrieval within latency and compute requirements.

</details>


### [200] [UniHEAR: Unified Heterogeneous-Source Attentive Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01147)
*Ganzhong Luo,Yang Ren,Hanyong Wang,Shuyu Zheng,Menglong Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniHEAR 是一个用于知识型视觉问答（KB-VQA）的统一轻量级异源实体检索与重排序框架，通过粗检索描述符、检索引导的注意力模态门控、熵加权源融合以及混合训练策略，解决了单源检索瓶颈和检索源盲重排序两个关键问题，在 E-VQA 和 InfoSeek 上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强系统在 KB-VQA 中存在两个关键缺陷：（1）依赖单一检索模态造成"单源检索瓶颈"，会遗漏仅通过互补来源才能访问的真实实体；（2）双塔点式重排序器存在"检索源盲重排序"问题，忽略检索来源和候选级检索先验，导致模态依赖冗余。这两个问题严重限制了实体检索与回答的准确性。

Method: UniHEAR 构建了一个统一轻量级异源实体检索和重排序框架，包含以下核心组件：（1）为每个候选实体构造 Coarse Retrieval Descriptor（粗检索描述符）；（2）引入 Retrieval-Guided Attentive Modality Gating（检索引导的注意力模态门控），基于描述符调节模态注意力权重；（3）采用 Entropy-Weighted Source Fusion（熵加权源融合）整合粗检索先验；（4）通过对比学习与辅助模态保留损失的混合训练策略，在单一模型中统一实体级和章节级检索。

Result: 在 E-VQA 和 InfoSeek 数据集上的大量实验表明，UniHEAR 在检索和 VQA 性能上均达到 SOTA，Recall@1 分别比最强基线提升 6.7 和 1.2 个百分点，同时保持轻量级重排序架构。

Conclusion: UniHEAR 通过统一建模多源检索信息和模态门控机制，有效克服了 KB-VQA 中单源检索瓶颈与检索源盲重排序的局限，证明了融合异源检索先验与轻量级统一架构在知识型视觉问答中的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires retrieving relevant entity knowledge from external sources to answer visually grounded questions. Existing retrieval-augmented systems suffer from two critical limitations. First, relying on a single retrieval modality creates a Single-Source Retrieval Bottleneck, missing ground-truth entities that are only accessible through complementary sources. Second, dual-tower pointwise rerankers suffer from Retrieval-Source-Blind Reranking, as they overlook retrieval origins and candidate-level retrieval priors, leading to redundant modality reliance. To address these challenges, we propose UniHEAR, a unified lightweight framework for heterogeneous-source entity retrieval and reranking. UniHEAR constructs a Coarse Retrieval Descriptor for each candidate entity, and introduces Retrieval-Guided Attentive Modality Gating to condition modality attention weights on this descriptor, further complemented by Entropy-Weighted Source Fusion of coarse retrieval priors. A hybrid training strategy combining contrastive learning with an auxiliary modality-preserving loss unifies entity-level and section-level retrieval within a single model. Extensive experiments on E-VQA and InfoSeek demonstrate that UniHEAR achieves state-of-the-art retrieval and VQA performance, improving Recall@1 by 6.7 and 1.2 points over the strongest baselines while maintaining a lightweight reranking architecture. Code and model are available at https://github.com/iven-luo/UniHEAR.

</details>


### [201] [Auditing Semantic Gains in Sequential Recommendation: A Lightweight Recovery Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01260)
*Kong Wang,Zhongke He,Xiang Chen,Hongwei Zeng,Kai Deng,Long Wang,Kehua Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LIME-Rec通过组合三个轻量级专家（SASRec序列专家、ItemCF共现专家和基于冻结bge嵌入的语义专家）进行可审计的分数级融合，在三个Amazon数据集上超越了最强的ID-only基线7.0%-12.0%，表明先前语义/生成式检索推荐系统的性能提升可能源于离线物品表征而非复杂的在线语言建模或语义ID生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期语义和生成式检索推荐系统声称相较于ID-only序列基线有显著提升，但这些提升究竟归因于语言模型推理、语义ID生成、端到端语义架构、更强的离线物品表征，还是语义与协同信号的互补，这一点尚不明确。作者希望厘清这种归因模糊性，避免将性能提升错误地归功于复杂机制。

Method: 提出LIME-Rec轻量级可审计恢复测试：将三个独立专家（SASRec序列专家、ItemCF共现专家、基于冻结BAAI/bge-base-en-v1.5物品嵌入的语义专家）的全目录分数按用户归一化后，通过可审计的分数级融合与有界历史校准组合起来。融合门控和校准头仅在验证集上拟合，服务时不需要语言模型推理，且各专家贡献可单独检查。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Toys和Sports上，LIME-Rec的R@10分别为0.0996、0.1105和0.0593，超出最强对比基线7.0%-12.0%。三专家融合（无历史校准）持续优于校准后的SASRec，说明校准本身不解释恢复效果。随机置换物品-文本嵌入对应关系使R@10下降13.6%-17.5%，表明增益依赖真实的物品-文本语义对应而非额外的表征容量。

Conclusion: 在将性能提升归因于服务时语言建模、语义ID生成或更重的语义机制之前，应首先排除轻量级离线物品表征恢复和透明融合的解释。LIME-Rec证明简单的离线语义表征与可审计融合即可达到甚至超越复杂语义推荐系统的效果。

Abstract: Recent semantic and generative-retrieval recommenders report substantial improvements over ID-only sequential baselines, but it remains unclear whether these gains arise from language-model reasoning, semantic-ID generation, end-to-end semantic architectures, stronger offline item representations, or complementary semantic and collaborative signals. We investigate this attribution ambiguity through LIME-Rec, a lightweight and auditable recovery test. LIME-Rec combines three independent experts: a SASRec sequential expert, an ItemCF co-occurrence expert, and a semantic expert based on frozen BAAI/bge-base-en-v1.5 item embeddings. Their full-catalog scores are normalized per user and combined through auditable score-level fusion followed by bounded history calibration. The fusion gate and calibration head are fitted on validation data only, require no serving-time language-model inference, and keep each expert contribution separately inspectable. On Amazon Beauty, Toys, and Sports, LIME-Rec achieves R@10 scores of 0.0996, 0.1105, and 0.0593, outperforming the strongest comparison baseline by 7.0%-12.0%. Three-expert fusion without history calibration consistently outperforms calibrated SASRec, showing that calibration alone does not explain the recovery. Randomly permuting item-text embeddings across item IDs reduces R@10 by 13.6%-17.5%, indicating that the gains depend on genuine item-text correspondence rather than additional representation capacity. These results suggest that lightweight recovery from offline item representations and transparent fusion should be ruled out before improvements are attributed to serving-time language modeling, semantic-ID generation, or heavier semantic machinery.

</details>


### [202] [Collaborative Memory Augmentation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01315)
*Enze Liu,Zhen Tian,Wayne Xin Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OMEGA是一个面向生成式推荐系统的协作记忆增强框架，通过潜在上下文压缩、协作记忆库、目标感知检索和上下文感知门控交叉注意力融合模块，将显式跨用户协作信号注入生成式推荐中，在多个真实数据集上显著超越现有先进GR模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐(GR)框架主要建模个体用户序列，局限于受限的内部参数空间，未能显式利用跨用户的协作信号，导致推荐性能受限。

Method: (1) 潜在上下文压缩：利用可学习查询令牌将序列化用户行为蒸馏为紧凑表示，降低存储开销；(2) 协作记忆库：聚合压缩表示形成显式的全局行为模式存储库；(3) 目标感知检索：同时考虑序列级和目标级相似性来检索相关记忆；(4) 上下文感知集成：通过门控交叉注意力机制自适应融合检索到的协作记忆与本地用户上下文，抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实证评估表明，OMEGA显著优于现有先进的生成式推荐模型，验证了外部记忆作为生成范式的补充手段的有效性。

Conclusion: 外部协作记忆能够有效弥补生成式推荐中隐式参数知识与显式协作信号之间的鸿沟，为生成式推荐提供了一种增强其协作建模能力的可行路径。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has exhibited great potential by modeling item transitions as a sequence-to-sequence task. Despite the success of GR, existing frameworks primarily focus on modeling individual user sequences within a constrained internal parametric space, failing to explicitly leverage cross-user collaborative signals. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{OMEGA}, a cOllaborative MEmory augmentation framework for Generative recommendAtion. OMEGA bridges the gap between implicit parametric knowledge and explicit collaborative signals. We first introduce a latent context compression method that utilizes learnable query tokens to distill sequential user behavior into compact representations, significantly reducing storage overhead. These compressed representations are aggregated into a collaborative memory bank, serving as an explicit repository of global behavioral patterns. To ensure precise knowledge acquisition, we design a lightweight and target-aware retrieval mechanism that identifies pertinent memories by considering both sequence-level and target-level similarities. Furthermore, a context-aware integration module, equipped with a gated cross-attention mechanism, is employed to adaptively fuse the retrieved collaborative memories with the local user context while mitigating the interference of noisy patterns. Empirical evaluations on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that OMEGA significantly outperforms existing advanced GR models, validating the potential of external memory as a complement to the generative paradigm.

</details>


### [203] [Real-Time Hybrid Retrieval in Hyperbolic Space for Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01450)
*Aradhya Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全在双曲几何Lorentz模型中运行的混合文档检索系统，用于检索增强生成（RAG）。该系统通过学习得到的HyTE-H变换将预训练词嵌入投影到双曲空间，采用BM25词汇评分与Lorentz内积相似度两阶段检索管线，通过参数α融合两种分数。在BEIR基准的五个数据集上，仅使用词嵌入（无微调编码器或交叉注意力重排序器）即达到0.654、0.304、0.342、0.150和0.217的NDCG@10分数，并支持实时索引及边缘设备上的交互式延迟检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密检索器受限于欧几里得空间，无法充分利用自然语言固有的层级结构。双曲几何具有指数体积增长特性，能更好地匹配语言的分层组织，因此作者旨在构建一个将检索过程完全迁移到Lorentz双曲空间中的系统，以在不依赖昂贵微调编码器的情况下提升检索性能并降低资源需求。

Method: 系统包含以下核心组件：(1) 通过学习得到的HyTE-H变换将预训练词嵌入投影到Lorentz双曲空间；(2) 文档被分割为重叠块并以Lorentz嵌入进行索引；(3) 两阶段检索管线：第一阶段使用BM25进行词汇打分候选生成，第二阶段使用Lorentzian内积相似度对候选重排序；(4) 可调参数α用于混合BM25分数与双曲相似度分数，实现词汇与语义信号的联合。

Result: 在BEIR基准的五个数据集（SciFact、NFCorpus、ArguAna、SciDocs、FiQA）上，仅使用词嵌入且不借助微调神经编码器或交叉注意力重排序器，分别取得了0.654、0.304、0.342、0.150和0.217的NDCG@10成绩。系统支持实时索引用户提供的文档，并能在数万篇中等大小文档上实现资源高效查询，满足边缘设备上的交互式延迟要求。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了双曲Lorentz模型下的混合检索在无需微调编码器的情况下即可达到可用的检索性能，同时具备实时索引能力和轻量级部署优势，为资源受限场景下的RAG系统提供了可行的双曲几何解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid document retrieval system designed for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that operates entirely within the Lorentz model of hyperbolic geometry. Unlike conventional dense retrievers confined to Euclidean space, this system projects pretrained word embeddings into hyperbolic space through a learned HyTE-H transformation, whose exponential volume growth suits the hierarchical organization of natural language. Documents are segmented into overlapping chunks, indexed by their Lorentz embeddings, and retrieved through a two-stage pipeline that first applies BM25 lexical scoring, then re-ranks candidates using Lorentzian inner-product similarity. A tunable parameter $α$ blends the BM25 score with the hyperbolic similarity score. The system was evaluated on five datasets from the BEIR benchmark suite, SciFact, NFCorpus, ArguAna, SciDocs, and FiQA, achieving NDCG@10 scores of 0.654, 0.304, 0.342, 0.150, and 0.217 respectively with word embeddings alone, without fine-tuned neural encoders or cross-attention rerankers. The system supports real-time indexing of user-supplied documents and resource-efficient querying over tens of thousands of moderately sized documents, so hyperbolic retrieval can run on edge devices at interactive latencies.

</details>


### [204] [Floor, Ceiling, and the Fusion Gap: How Much of Crowd Reading Attention Can Machines Predict?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01704)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为"预测读者会高亮哪些句子"这一任务建立了性能下界（朴素截断）和上界（裂半预测者），并发现两者之间存在+0.2028 AP的语义性差距。前沿语言模型零样本可达35-53%，而五模型融合加位置先验可达60%，超越最佳单模型且通过预注册复现验证；将融合蒸馏到8B开源学生模型可保留90%优势，证明人群高亮信号存在于文档级结构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数若无参照系则毫无意义：不知道朴素方法能得多少分，也不知道理论上限有多高，就无法解读任何模型的真实表现。作者利用一种罕见的真实标注——120篇网页文档中，一群互不知情、未受指导、无偿的读者出于自身目的高亮的句子——来同时构建性能下界（floor）和上界（ceiling），从而为"预测人群阅读高亮"这一任务提供完整的性能锚点。

Method: 下界采用朴素截断（取文档开头）；上界采用裂半oracle（一半人群预测另一半人群的高亮）。通过位置/长度特征回归、多款前沿语言模型零样本评测、五模型跨厂商融合（加位置先验），以及将融合蒸馏到8B开源学生模型（全局与局部上下文两种变体），系统性地剖析缺口内部结构，并用域聚类、预注册复现、消融实验等验证结果稳健性。

Result: 下界与上界之间差距为+0.2028 AP，其中位置和长度特征仅能恢复5%。前沿语言模型零样本达到35-53%（远高于经典基线，远低于人群）；LLMLingua-2（SOTA提示压缩器）低于下界，与随机选择无异。五模型跨厂商融合加位置先验达到60%，超越最佳单模型+0.0159（Holm p=0.019），且通过预注册的217篇独立文档复现确认（+0.0179，Holm p=0.042）。将融合蒸馏为全局上下文的8B学生可保留90%优势并达到与最强单模型统计对等，而局部上下文学生仅保留63%。

Conclusion: 人群高亮的可预测性缺口本质上是语义性的而非位置性的；前沿模型在此任务上远优于经典基线但明显落后于人群本身；多个不同模型的简单平均是已知成本最低的改进手段；人群高亮信号蕴含在文档级结构中，局部上下文不足以捕捉。融合蒸馏的全局上下文学生能以开源8B模型达到与最强闭源单模型相当的性能。

Abstract: A benchmark score means nothing without knowing what a trivial method achieves and what the best possible method could achieve. We construct both bounds for a task with a rare kind of ground truth: predicting which sentences a crowd of readers -- highlighting for their own purposes, unpaid, uninstructed, and blind to each other -- marked in 120 web documents. The floor is naive truncation (lead); the ceiling is a split-half oracle: half the crowd predicting the other half. The gap between them is +0.2028 AP [+0.1698, +0.2342, domain-clustered], and three findings structure it. First, the gap is semantic: position and length features recover 5% of it. Second, frontier language models reach 35-53% of it zero-shot -- far above classical baselines, far below the crowd; a state-of-the-art prompt compressor (LLMLingua-2) lands below the floor, indistinguishable from random selection. Third, an unweighted cross-vendor fusion of five frontier rankings plus a position prior reaches 60%, beating the best single model by +0.0159 [+0.0044, +0.0269; Holm p=0.019] -- a gain that survives ablation of its best member, split-half arm selection, prompt paraphrase, and label, gate, and seed perturbations, and was CONFIRMED by a pre-registered replication on 217 independent documents (+0.0179, Holm p=0.042). Finally, the bracket compresses: distilling the fusion into one open-weight 8B student that reads the whole document retains 90% of the fusion's edge and reaches statistical parity with the strongest single frontier model (+0.0070 [-0.0068, +0.0200]), where a local-context student retains only 63% -- the crowd's signal lives in document-level structure, and the cheapest known improvement is to ask several different models and average.

</details>


### [205] [MODE: Mutual Optimality in Direct Effects of Reciprocal Recommendations in Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01731)
*Yoji Tomita*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MODE方法，用于匹配平台（如招聘和约会平台）的互惠推荐系统，通过形式化"直接效应最优性"概念，计算用户间在直接效应上互惠最优的推荐，在合成和真实数据上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配平台（如招聘、在线约会）日益普及，成功的关键在于设计互惠推荐系统（RRSs），需同时考虑双方用户偏好并防止机会过度集中在少数热门用户上。然而，过度关注缓解集中度可能导致部分用户的推荐结果不理想，引发不满。因此需要在个体推荐质量与公平性之间取得平衡。

Method: 形式化定义了"直接效应最优性"（optimality of direct effects）概念，即给定对其他用户的推荐后，个体用户推荐列表的直接效应最优。基于此提出MODE方法，计算所有用户在直接效应上互惠最优的推荐。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，MODE在直接效应的互惠最优性方面超越现有方法，处理速度更快，且能实现更高的预期匹配数量。

Conclusion: MODE有效解决了互惠推荐中个体推荐质量与机会均匀分配之间的权衡问题，在保证直接效应互惠最优性的同时实现更高效的匹配与更快的计算速度。

Abstract: Matching platforms such as job posting services and online dating platforms have become widely used over the past decade. For a matching platform to be successful, it is crucial to design appropriate reciprocal recommendation systems (RRSs) that consider the preferences of users on both sides (job candidates and employers) and prevent opportunities from being concentrated too heavily on a few popular users. However, prioritizing concentration mitigation too much can lead to recommending undesirable results to some individual users, resulting in their dissatisfaction. In this paper, we formulate the concept of ``optimality of direct effects'' of the recommendation list for an individual user, given the recommendations to other users. Furthermore, we propose a novel method, MODE, that computes mutually optimal recommendations in direct effects. Experiments with synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that MODE surpasses other existing methods in terms of mutual optimality of direct effects, exhibits faster processing speeds, and enables a higher expected number of matches.

</details>


### [206] [X-KGRank: A Knowledge Graph RAG Framework for Explainable Recommendations via Pattern Mining and LLM Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01732)
*Meenakshi Rajpurohit,Jainish Patel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: X-KGRank是一个知识图谱检索增强框架，统一了结构化协同过滤与LLM解释生成，在MovieLens-1M上通过LightGCN排序器和选择性路由策略实现NDCG@10=0.2956、Recall@10=0.5371，较流行度基线提升17.1%，并验证了1.5B小模型在解释质量上可媲美7B模型但更易产生事实性幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统存在根本性矛盾：协同过滤能捕捉行为信号但无法提供推理依据，LLM能生成流畅解释但会幻觉且与用户历史脱节。两者均无法满足用户对推荐结果可解释性和可质询性的需求。

Method: 从MovieLens-1M构建包含9762节点、999264边的异构知识图谱（三种关系类型），持久化于Neo4j。训练具有SBERT初始化内容和评分加权BPR目标的LightGCN排序器，并采用流行度选择性路由策略：长尾物品通过KG路径检索增强生成，流行物品直接从预训练知识中回答，从而将KG增强生成减少约50%。

Result: 在99样本评估协议下，NDCG@10=0.2956、Recall@10=0.5371，两指标较流行度基线提升17.1%；NDCG@20提升15.6%（0.3449 vs 0.2983）；MRR提升14.6%（0.2435 vs 0.2124）。跨三个LLM后端的16个案例中，Qwen2.5-1.5B在启发式解释质量上（0.97）反而超过Mistral-7B（0.94），但定性分析显示小模型更容易产生事实性捏造。

Conclusion: X-KGRank证明知识图谱检索增强能够有效弥合协同过滤与LLM解释之间的鸿沟：既保留行为信号的排序能力，又提供可溯源、被用户历史所grounded的推荐解释。但小规模LLM在解释流畅性上可媲美大规模模型的同时，仍面临事实性幻觉的挑战，提示未来需要在幻觉抑制方面进一步投入。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems produce predictions that users cannot interrogate. The two dominant improvements, collaborative filtering and LLM-based reasoning, each fall short: collaborative filtering captures behavioural signals but offers no reasoning, while large language models (LLMs) generate fluent explanations but hallucinate and are poorly grounded in a user's history. We present X-KGRank, a knowledge graph retrieval augmented framework that unifies structural collaborative filtering with LLM-based explanation. From the MovieLens-1M dataset (6,040 users, 3,704 items, 988,129 interactions) we construct a heterogeneous knowledge graph of 9,762 nodes and 999,264 edges spanning three relation types (RATED, HAS_GENRE, and CO_RATED) persisted in Neo4j. We train a LightGCN ranker with content-aware SBERT initialization and a rating weighted BPR objective, and apply a popularity selective routing strategy that grounds long-tail items (1,855 of 3,704) in knowledge-graph paths while serving popular items from pre-trained knowledge, reducing KG-augmented generations by roughly 50%. On the MovieLens-1M test set under a 99-sample protocol, X-KGRank achieves NDCG@10 = 0.2956 and Recall@10 = 0.5371, improving over a strong popularity baseline by 17.1% on both metrics, by 15.6% on NDCG@20 (0.3449 vs. 0.2983), and by 14.6% on MRR (0.2435 vs. 0.2124). Across three LLM backbones evaluated on 16 cases, a 1.5-billion-parameter model (Qwen2.5-1.5B) matches a 7-billion-parameter model (Mistral-7B) on heuristic explanation quality (0.97 vs. 0.94), yet qualitative analysis shows the smaller model is more prone to factual fabrication.

</details>


### [207] [SPEAR: Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval for Community Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01738)
*Wenbin Wu,Yuzhong Wu,Yufan Xu,Kuan Fang,Xing Xu,Cheng Ye,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR框架，一种选择感知的端到端个性化查询改写与检索架构，解决电商搜索中改写优化与检索效果分阶段优化导致的结构性错位问题。SPEAR通过双嵌入骨干网络（带辅助损失和梯度隔离）、乘法门控聚合器和动态改写选择器三个组件，分别解决召回语义被排名信号侵蚀、泛化词捷径和请求级适配问题。在10万工业搜索会话的离线评测和在线A/B测试中均显著提升检索效果和用户参与度，已部署于得物社区搜索平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中查询改写与检索效果被分开优化（production baseline分阶段训练），导致两个阶段结构上不对齐。虽然基于路径（path-based）的架构可以端到端统一两者，但这些架构是为个性化设计的，其中相关性不是显式约束。搜索场景额外要求改写必须忠实于用户原始查询意图。若直接移植，模型会学习一种捷径——泛化词主导效应，即倾向于输出在路径上得分高但与查询意图偏离的泛化改写词。

Method: SPEAR包含三个组件：(1) 双嵌入骨干网络，通过辅助损失和梯度隔离保护召回侧语义不被CTR驱动的排名信号侵蚀；(2) 乘法门控聚合器，要求改写只有在置信度和商品相关性都强时才获得高分，消除泛化词捷径；(3) 动态改写选择器，联合生成请求级改写权重和用户-查询条件化的尺度与偏置项，使改写偏好和相关性校准都能适配每个具体请求。

Result: 在10万条工业搜索会话的离线评测中，SPEAR相比生产基线将改写语义相似度@10提升+18.2，点击召回@10提升+99.5。在线A/B测试中，查询视角CTR提升+0.259，平均阅读深度提升+0.733，证明改进的改写选择能转化为更强的检索效果和更深的用户参与。系统已于2025年全面部署于得物社区搜索平台。

Conclusion: SPEAR通过针对性的三组件设计，解决了端到端改写-检索架构移植到搜索场景时面临的泛化词捷径、语义侵蚀和请求级适配问题，验证了端到端统一优化改写与检索在电商搜索中的有效性，并完成工业部署。

Abstract: Query reformulation bridges user intent and retrieval in e-commerce search, yet production systems optimize rewrite quality and retrieval effectiveness separately, leaving the two stages structurally misaligned. Path-based architectures unify them end-to-end but were designed for personalization, where relevance is not an explicit constraint-search additionally requires the rewrite to remain faithful to the user's stated query intent. Transplanted directly, these models learn a shortcut we term the generic-word dominance effect: they favor generic rewrites that score well on paths but drift from query intent. To address this, we propose SPEAR (Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval), which integrates three components that each target one failure mode: (1) a dual-embedding backbone with auxiliary loss and gradient isolation that shields recall-side semantics from being eroded by CTR-driven ranking signals; (2) a multiplicative gating aggregator that lets a rewrite score high only when both its confidence and item relevance are strong, eliminating the generic-word shortcut; (3) a Dynamic Rewrite Selector that jointly generates request-specific rewrite weights and user-query-conditioned scale and bias terms, allowing both rewrite preference and relevance calibration to adapt to each request. Offline evaluation on 100K held-out industrial search sessions shows that the proposed framework improves rewrite semantic similarity@10 by +18.2 and click recall@10 by +99.5 over the production baseline. In online A/B testing, SPEAR achieves +0.259 in query-view CTR and +0.733 in average reading depth, confirming that improved rewrite selection translates into stronger retrieval and deeper user engagement. The proposed SPEAR system has been fully deployed in Dewu's community search platform since 2025. Our code is available at https://github.com/mallocagi1-cell/spear.

</details>


### [208] [HyperAgent4POI: Dynamic Semantic Message Passing on Multi-Agent Hypergraphs for Missing-Modality Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01846)
*Jinze Wang,Yuze Liu,Tiehua Zhang,Jiong Jin,Zhu Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HyperAgent4POI，利用动态语义消息传递（DSMP）在超图每层内完成模态补全和软关联细化，解决POI推荐中文本和视觉内容缺失导致的表示弱化问题。通过冻结Llama骨干结合角色适配器生成节点-超边消息，基于语义超边motif进行软关联评分与模态补全，最终缓存节点表示实现无LLM调用的在线高效排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实LBSN服务中，POI的文本和视觉内容往往不完整，导致模态缺失使POI表示弱化、语义证据减少，并且无法为高层用户-POI交互建模提供可靠依据，影响推荐排序效果。

Method: 提出HyperAgent4POI框架：1) 采用Dynamic Semantic Message Passing（DSMP）在每个超图层内同时进行模态补全和软关联细化；2) 使用冻结的Llama骨干网络搭配角色特定适配器，通过持久节点代理生成节点到超边的消息；3) 由消息构成语义超边motif，用于引导软关联评分和模态补全；4) 最终节点表示被缓存供在线排序使用，避免在线LLM调用开销。

Result: 在三个真实LBSN数据集上，跨15个基线模型在不同模态缺失率下均取得一致的排序性能提升。在60%模态缺失率下，HyperAgent4POI在三个数据集上平均比最强基线提升8.2%的NDCG@20，且缓存推理保证了实际在线效率。

Conclusion: HyperAgent4POI通过DSMP统一处理模态补全与超图软关联细化，有效缓解模态缺失对POI推荐的影响，同时在显著提升排序性能的同时保持实际在线推理的高效性，验证了LLM与图神经架构协同在POI推荐中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation benefits from textual and visual content that describes venue semantics, yet such content is often incomplete in real-world services. Missing modalities weaken POI representations and reduce the semantic evidence available for ranking. The resulting representations also provide unreliable evidence for modeling higher-order user--POI interactions. We propose HyperAgent4POI, which uses Dynamic Semantic Message Passing (DSMP) to perform modality completion and soft incidence refinement within each hypergraph layer. Persistent node agents share a frozen Llama backbone and use role-specific adapters to produce node-to-hyperedge messages. Semantic hyperedge motifs formed from these messages guide soft incidence scoring and modality completion. Final node representations are cached for online ranking without LLM calls. Experiments on three real-world LBSN datasets show consistent ranking gains over 15 baselines across modality-missing rates, while cached inference provides practical online efficiency. Under a 60% modality-missing rate, HyperAgent4POI improves NDCG@20 over the strongest baseline by 8.2% on average across the three datasets.

</details>


### [209] [A Self-Triggered Agentic Push Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01949)
*Zhao-Yu Zhang,Qingying Chen,Chunyuan Zheng,Jing Zhou,Jian Sun,Siqi Chen,Leiying Chen,Chuan Zhou,Huiyou Jiang,Xin Tao,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STEPS是一个已在抖音全面部署、服务超过10亿用户的自触发端到端智能推送推荐系统。它通过两个基于决策Transformer的智能体（规划智能体和执行智能体）以及一个轻量级过滤智能体，将推送推荐重构为自触发的智能体过程，在"是否发送推送"和"何时再次调用系统"两个维度上形成闭环，兼顾实时效果与计算效率。在线A/B测试显示，用户活跃天数提升0.2843%，推送权限关闭率降低1.9089%，计算开销降低79.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推送系统存在两大被动范式：预计划频率方法（离线建模分配投放时间，缺乏实时适应性）和固定间隔触发方法（周期性轮询系统，在计算开销过大与最优时机捕捉不足之间存在严格两难）。此外，多阶段框架严重受困于局部最优问题。作者旨在克服这些局限，构建一个能够同时决策"是否推送"和"何时再次触发系统"的主动式推送系统。

Method: STEPS将推送推荐重构为自触发智能体过程，包含三个核心组件：(1) 规划智能体（Planning Agent）：基于决策Transformer，采用门控有序回归（gated ordinal regression）方法调度下一次系统调用时间；(2) 执行智能体（Execution Agent）：基于决策Transformer，根据轨迹奖励决定是否发送推送；(3) 轻量级过滤智能体（Filtering Agent）：控制计算开销，并作为防止不合理规划行为的关键安全防线。整个系统形成自我触发的闭环，平衡实时效果与效率。

Result: 在线A/B测试结果表明：STEPS显著提升用户活跃天数0.2843%，降低推送权限关闭率1.9089%，过滤智能体降低计算开销79.42%。该系统已在抖音平台全量部署，服务超过10亿用户。

Conclusion: STEPS通过自触发的端到端智能体架构成功解决了大规模推送系统中"何时推送"与"是否推送"的联合优化难题，在实时性和效率之间取得了良好的平衡，并在工业级大规模平台（抖音）上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Push notification is a critical recommendation scenario on large-scale platforms, allowing the system to proactively reach users outside the application to improve long-term re-engagement. However, designing an optimal push system requires handling a complex action space for the "whether and when" delivery problem under strict system resource constraints. Existing solutions typically fall into two passive paradigms: pre-planned frequency methods that allocate delivery times via offline modeling, limiting real-time adaptability; and fixed-interval triggering methods that periodically poll the system, creating a strict dilemma between excessive computational overhead and diminished optimal timing capture. Furthermore, such multi-stage frameworks severely suffer from local optima. To overcome these limitations, in this paper, we propose STEPS, a proactive, Self-Triggered End-to-end Agentic Push Recommendation System, which is already fully deployed at Douyin with over 1 billion users. STEPS reformulates push recommendation as a self-triggered agentic process in which the system decides not only whether to send a push, but also when to invoke itself again, thereby forming a closed loop that balances real-time effectiveness and efficiency. Specifically, STEPS consists of two decision transformer-based agents: a planning agent that schedules the next system invocation using a gated ordinal regression method, and an execution agent that decides whether to send a push based on trajectory rewards. Furthermore, we introduce a lightweight filtering agent to both control computational overhead and act as a crucial safeguard against unreasonable planning behaviors. Online A/B testing demonstrates that STEPS significantly increases user active days by 0.2843% and reduces the push permission disablement rate by 1.9089%, while the filtering agent reduces computational overhead by 79.42%.

</details>


### [210] [SmartGR: Hierarchy and Beam-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02048)
*Ziheng Zhang,Yu Cui,Bohao Wang,Yong He,Chao Yu,Chuan Yuan,Wujie Sun,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SmartGR是一个针对生成式推荐系统的知识蒸馏框架，通过层级感知的SID蒸馏和束搜索感知的排序蒸馏，在四个基准数据集上平均提升8.6%的性能并实现2.39倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型在扩展规模时能提升性能，但推理成本也随之大幅增加。知识蒸馏可以将大模型能力迁移到轻量模型中，但现有蒸馏方法未能解决两个GR特有的挑战：(1) 语义ID(SID)层级中蒸馏难度不均衡的问题；(2) 束搜索过程中前缀剪枝错误的问题。

Method: 提出SmartGR蒸馏框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Hierarchy-Aware SID Distillation，在语义ID层级结构中传递教师模型的建模能力；2) Beam-Aware Ranking Distillation，在束搜索过程中蒸馏教师模型的排序偏好。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SmartGR平均提升推荐性能8.6%，同时实现2.39倍的推理加速，验证了方法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: SmartGR通过针对生成式推荐特有挑战设计的蒸馏方法，实现了在保持推荐质量的同时显著降低推理成本，为GR模型的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm for recommender systems. Scaling up GR models can improve recommendation performance, but it also substantially increases inference cost. Knowledge distillation provides a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a large GR model to a lightweight one. However, existing distillation methods do not account for two GR-specific challenges: imbalanced distillation difficulty across the semantic ID (SID) hierarchy and incorrect prefix pruning during beam search. To address these challenges, we propose SmartGR, a novel distillation framework that utilizes Hierarchy-Aware SID Distillation to transfer the teacher's modeling capability across the hierarchy and leverages Beam-Aware Ranking Distillation to distill the teacher's ranking preferences during beam search. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of SmartGR, improving the performance by 8.6% while achieving a 2.39$\times$ inference speedup on average.

</details>


### [211] [Douyin Multimodal Embedding Model Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02148)
*Haonan Chen,Chu Li,Zhicheng Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Yuanjiang Wang,Shaohua Jiang,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Douyin多模态嵌入模型（DME），通过两阶段训练结合对比学习的高效性与CoT推理的精细判别能力。第一阶段进行大规模对比预训练建立统一的多模态嵌入空间；第二阶段通过"证据引导的类型化潜在推理"和"跨条件重构"两种机制补充语义充分性。DME在服务时与标准对比编码器一样高效（训练时额外开销），在MMEB-v2上达到SOTA（2B和9B变体分别74.8和78.4），并在抖音生产环境中部署，为搜索场景带来0.1%的LT在线收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM嵌入模型难以同时满足十亿级索引下的高效性和硬匹配所需的细粒度判别能力。对比模型虽然高效但依赖粗粒度的成对监督，无法区分细粒度差异；基于CoT的模型虽然通过显式生成提升判别力但难以在线服务。抖音、小红书、YouTube等真实平台需要同时具备两种优势的模型。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：Stage 1进行大规模对比预训练，建立覆盖广泛模态和任务的统一多模态嵌入空间；Stage 2通过两种训练机制补充语义充分性——(1)证据引导的类型化潜在推理（Evidence-Grounded Typed Latent Reasoning）：在隐藏空间中进行潜在推理以组织检索证据；(2)跨条件重构（Cross-Conditional Reconstruction）：通过跨方向自回归重构强制保留对端语义。两种机制仅在训练时生效，服务时仅增加边际查询端开销，因此DME与标准对比编码器一样高效。

Result: 在MMEB-v2基准上，DME的2B和9B变体分别达到74.8和78.4的SOTA成绩，在视频和视觉文档任务上表现尤其突出。在生产环境中，DME在抖音内部离线评估集上获得2.92%的相对提升，已部署于抖音的生成式搜索、图像搜索和AI搜索等场景，在线A/B测试中为抖音搜索带来0.1%的LT（Lifetime）收益。

Conclusion: DME证明了对比预训练与训练期潜在推理技术可以有机结合，在不牺牲在线推理效率的前提下实现细粒度多模态匹配，为工业级多模态嵌入模型提供了兼顾效率与精度的可行范式。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning is a cornerstone of modern AI. By encoding multimodal queries and targets into vectors, it powers industrial search and recommendation and underpins modern agents. Real-world platforms with complex modalities and massive-scale content, such as Douyin, Xiaohongshu, and YouTube, demand both efficiency under billion-scale indexing and fine-grained discrimination for hard matching. Existing MLLM embedding models rarely satisfy both. Contrastive models are efficient but rely on pair-level supervision too coarse for fine-grained distinctions, while CoT-based models improve discrimination through explicit generation impractical to serve online. We present Douyin Multimodal Embedding (DME), a model trained in two stages to combine both strengths. Stage 1 performs large-scale contrastive pre-training that establishes a unified multimodal embedding space with broad modality and task coverage. Stage 2 supplements semantic sufficiency, the property that an embedding is grounded in retrieval-relevant evidence and preserves fine-grained counterpart-side semantics, via two mechanisms. Evidence-Grounded Typed Latent Reasoning organizes retrieval evidence through hidden-space latent reasoning, and Cross-Conditional Reconstruction enforces counterpart-side semantics through cross-directional autoregressive reconstruction. Both act only during training and add only marginal query-side overhead, so DME serves as efficiently as a standard contrastive encoder. On MMEB-v2, DME reaches state-of-the-art results at comparable scales for its 2B and 9B variants (74.8 and 78.4), with especially strong video and visual-document tasks. In production, DME delivers a 2.92% relative gain on Douyin's in-house offline evaluation set, is deployed across Douyin scenarios such as generative, image, and AI search, and yields a 0.1% Lifetime (LT) gain in online A/B testing on Douyin search.

</details>


### [212] [Disentangled Contrastive Learning for Zero-Shot Multilingual Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02189)
*Chao Huang,Yufeng Chen,Changhao Guan,Guang Yang,Dongze Chen,Kaiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种解纠缠对比学习（DCL）方法用于多语言稠密检索，通过将多语言表示分离为语义子空间和语言子空间，减少语言特征对语义匹配的干扰，实现从英语到低资源语言的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言稠密检索面临低资源语言标注数据稀缺的挑战。现有方法在共享表示中学习语言无关的语义特征时，语义特征与语言特征纠缠在一起，干扰了语义相关性优化。

Method: 提出解纠缠对比学习（DCL），将多语言表示分离为语义子空间和语言子空间。设计基于分层语义对齐和语言去偏对比学习的解纠缠优化目标：在句子和词元级别对齐跨语言的检索相关语义，同时在语言子空间中捕捉语言特有变化，并与检索目标联合优化以促进零样本迁移。

Result: 在mMARCO和MIRACL数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法一致优于多个强基线，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离语义与语言特征并联合优化检索目标，DCL有效降低了语言对语义匹配的干扰，实现了鲁棒的跨语言零样本稠密检索。

Abstract: Multilingual dense retrieval aims to handle queries and documents across different languages based on a unified retriever model. The challenge lies in enabling robust retrieval transfer to low-resource languages where annotated retrieval data is often scarce. Although previous studies transfer high-resource supervision to low-resource languages in multilingual semantic representation learning, the shared representation often entangles semantic and linguistic features, which may interfere with optimizing semantic relevance for retrieval. Different from existing methods that focus on learning language-agnostic semantic features under such entanglement, we propose a disentangled contrastive learning~(DCL) method for multilingual dense retrieval by separating multilingual representations into semantic and linguistic subspaces. Specifically, we design disentangled optimization objectives based on hierarchical semantic alignment and language debiasing contrastive learning. By aligning retrieval-relevant semantics across languages at both sentence and token levels while capturing language-specific variations in the linguistic subspace, these objectives reduce language-induced interference in semantic matching. We jointly optimize them with the retrieval objective to facilitate stable zero-shot transfer from English supervision to multilingual dense retrieval. Extensive experiments on mMARCO and MIRACL show that our method consistently outperforms several strong baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalization ability.

</details>


### [213] [Advancing Relevance Measurement with Vision-Language Models for Web-Scale Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02446)
*Han Wang,Alex Whitworth,Pak Ming Cheung,Zhenjie Zhang,Krishna Kamath,Xi Chen,Roberto Konow,Kurchi Subhra Hazra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化相关性评估流水线，已部署于Pinterest搜索的在线A/B实验中，替代传统高成本的人工标注，显著提升评估效率并降低在线实验的最小可检测效应（MDE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关性评估是个性化搜索系统中的关键保障机制，但传统人工标注成本高、周期长，难以规模化扩展，亟需一种高效且可靠的自动化评估方案。

Method: 构建VLM-based自动化相关性评估管道，部署于Pinterest搜索的在线A/B实验中，并严格验证VLM生成的相关性判断与人工标注之间的一致性对齐。

Result: VLM评估与人类标注高度对齐，证明VLM能够提供可靠的实验相关性度量；同时显著提升评估效率，扩大查询集，优化采样设计，并大幅降低在线实验测量中的最小可检测效应（MDEs）。

Conclusion: VLM驱动的自动化相关性评估能够替代人工标注用于在线实验，不仅提升效率，还解锁了扩展查询集、优化采样等更多可能性，最终实现更高质量的相关性指标和更灵敏的在线实验测量。

Abstract: Relevance evaluation plays a crucial role in personalized search systems, serving as a guardrail alongside user engagement metrics to ensure that search results align with user queries and intent. While human annotation is the traditional method for relevance evaluation, its high cost and long turnaround time limit its scalability. In this work, we present a VLM-based automated relevance evaluation pipeline deployed within Pinterest Search for online A/B experiments. We rigorously validate the alignment between VLM-generated judgments and human annotations, demonstrating that VLMs can provide reliable relevance measurement for experiments while greatly improving the evaluation efficiency. Leveraging VLM-based labeling further unlocks opportunities to expand the query set, optimize sampling design, and efficiently assess a wider range of search experiences at scale. This approach leads to higher-quality relevance metrics and significantly reduces the Minimum Detectable Effects (MDEs) in online experiment measurements.

</details>


### [214] [Syntax Meets Semantics: Understanding Scientific Formulae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02457)
*Yuni Susanti,Moritz Schubotz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了学术文献中数学公式的语法与语义两种模态之间的表示对应关系，发现二者原生表示空间存在显著失配，但通过对比学习等表示对齐技术可以大幅提升跨模态检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术信息检索中，数学公式兼具结构化语法和语义载体的双重属性，尽管前人研究表明联合建模两种模态能提升检索效果，但两种模态底层表示之间的关系从未被系统性地研究过。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究语法与语义表示之间是否存在对应性以及这种失配能否被修复。

Method: 首先通过实证方法分析公式语法与语义原生表示空间之间的跨模态对应性；随后使用基于图的编码器表征语法结构、基于文本的编码器表征语义信息，并应用对比学习将两种表示空间对齐到共享空间中，比较对齐前后跨模态检索性能的变化。

Result: 实验发现语法和语义的原生表示空间之间存在极弱的可观察对应性，但潜在相关性很强，表明两种模态存在实质性表示失配。通过对比学习进行显式表示对齐后，跨模态检索性能得到显著提升，说明显式的表示学习能够恢复原生表示空间中缺失的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，公式语法与语义之间的表示失配是可以通过显式表示学习方法有效缓解的；对比学习诱导的共享表示空间能够弥合两种模态间的鸿沟，从而显著改善跨模态公式检索效果，为学术信息检索中公式的联合建模提供了新的实证依据和方法指导。

Abstract: Scientific formulae are a fundamental component of scholarly communication, yet their dual nature -- as structured syntax and carriers of semantics -- remains underexplored in scholarly information retrieval. Although prior studies show that jointly modeling syntactic and semantic modalities improves retrieval performance, the relationship between their underlying representations has not been systematically investigated. In this work, we empirically study cross-modal correspondence between formula syntax and semantics. We find that their native representation spaces exhibit extremely weak observable correspondence despite strong latent correlation, indicating a substantial representation mismatch between the two modalities.
