Under balanced preference groups, a single FedAvg model, despite starting at nearly random accuracy, surpasses reward models trained separately for each ground-truth group after only a few local optimization steps. We attribute this phenomenon to the flatness of the shared initialization: averaging across all clients learns richer shared representations that distinguish responses while canceling conflicting preference directions, leaving the model near a decision boundary that can be rapidly adapted. Group imbalance breaks this effect as the cancellation becomes asymmetric and leaves minority clients too far from the boundary to recover. Motivated by this observation, we propose FedGD (Federated Learning with Group Debiasing), which discovers latent preference groups during federated training and learns a single reward model using group-debiased client sampling. By counteracting the effect of group imbalance, FedGD learns an initialization that remains highly adaptable, enabling effective personalization without prior knowledge of the underlying groups.

</details>


### [313] [Meganeura: Portable GPU Training and Inference through Vulkan and Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01563)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meganeura提出了一种紧凑的原生编译器，通过Vulkan和Metal在消费级GPU上同时覆盖训练和推理部署。在50个设备-工作负载-模式组合中，48个通过了严格的f32和加速路径验证；相比PyTorch/torch.compile，编译时间大幅缩短（0.1-2.4秒对比6-96秒），二进制文件仅13 MiB，并在多个GPU平台上取得有竞争力甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和推理通常需要跨越不同的导出、转换和平台特定的运行时边界，导致部署复杂且效率低下。作者希望探索是否存在一个紧凑的原生编译器能够在消费级GPU上同时覆盖训练和推理两个阶段，从而简化端到端的机器学习工作流。

Method: Meganeura包含类型化静态图、自动微分、优化器、检查点、内存规划器和运行时，通过Vulkan和Metal底层API来编译专门的程序。实验在NVIDIA和AMD独立GPU、AMD APU、Apple silicon和Intel iGPU上对比了五个匹配的工作负载，并设计了严格的协议：区分严格f32与验证过的快速路径，并独立门控前向和反向传播。此外还包含一个Android XR物理部署案例研究，将Meganeura训练的decoder移植到Adreno/OpenXR应用。

Result: 在48/50的device-workload-mode组合中通过双门控验证；严格f32下，在20个GPU参考的最小时延单元中赢了12个，中位有效训练差距为1.8x。在AMD独立GPU上，5个推理工作负载中4个在ROCm PyTorch编译版的1.10x内，3个训练工作负载更快。加速合约下最差训练差距为4.6x。编译时间0.1-2.4秒（对比torch.compile的6-96秒），二进制13 MiB。性能差距主要定位在卷积导数和注意力反向计算。

Conclusion: 实验表明通用消费级图形API（Vulkan/Metal）可以支撑一个紧凑的"训练到部署"一体化栈，并在部分场景达到厂商级或更优性能。观察到的性能差距源于内核覆盖、调度和算术策略，而非API本身的根本性限制，说明该方向具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Training and deployed inference often cross export, conversion, and platform-specific runtime boundaries. Meganeura asks whether one compact native compiler can span both phases on consumer GPUs. Its typed static graph, automatic differentiation, optimizer, checkpoint, memory planner, and runtime lower specialized programs through Vulkan and Metal.
  We compare five matched workloads with PyTorch on NVIDIA and AMD discrete GPUs, an AMD APU, Apple silicon, and an Intel iGPU. The protocol separates strict f32 from validated fast paths and gates forward and backward independently. Forty-eight of 50 device-workload-mode cells pass both gates; the other two share one unresolved backward-reference disagreement on a newly supported APU. In strict f32, Meganeura wins 12 of 20 GPU-referenced minimal-latency cells and has a median valid training gap of 1.8x. On the discrete AMD GPU, four of five inference workloads are within 1.10x of compiled ROCm PyTorch and three training workloads are faster. Under accelerated contracts, the worst training gap is 4.6x.
  Compilation takes 0.1-2.4 seconds versus 6-96 seconds for torch.compile on supported GPU paths; the stripped binary is 13 MiB. Dispatch profiles localize the largest gaps to convolution derivatives and attention backward. A physical Android XR case study transfers a Meganeura-trained decoder into an Adreno/OpenXR application sharing the graphics queue. The results show that general consumer graphics APIs can support a compact shared train-to-deploy stack at useful, sometimes vendor-competitive performance. The measured gaps point to kernel coverage, scheduling, and arithmetic policy rather than an identified API limitation.

</details>


### [314] [Measuring in-context algorithmic reasoning in language models against an exact Bayes-optimal standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01575)
*Hector Zenil,Luan Ozelim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F-ICL基准，基于Turing完备机器sF上穷举的15亿个程序及其贝叶斯最优后验，评估37个开源及前沿LLM（0.8B-675B）在上下文学习中的归纳推理能力。结果显示模型虽能回答高达92%的查询，但45/46个模型的输出分布比"仅打印"参考机器更远离最优解，且存在更新非单调性（6545次求解到未求解的转变），表明LLM更像模式补全而非真正的算法推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏正确归纳推理的ground truth，难以区分LLM是真正的算法推理还是模式补全。作者通过构造带有封闭形式贝叶斯最优后验的基准来提供精确的ground truth，从而可以严格衡量模型的归纳推理质量。

Method: 利用Turing完全机器F的互补对称化版本sF（消除输出极性偏差），穷举枚举所有长度≤13的15亿个程序，在受限通用（Levin-Solomonoff）先验下封闭形式计算贝叶斯最优后验。每个任务配对其按位补码版本（最优解评分相同），通过原始-孪生差距（original-twin gap）隔离模型归纳偏差。共测试105种服务配置、37个开源模型和四家实验室的前沿系统。

Result: 模型正确回答高达92%的查询，但45/46个模型的分布比仅打印的参考机器更远离最优；参考机器本身是无循环的算法混合，表明模型的表现更接近无循环混合而非最优。更新非单调：6545次求解到未求解转变对比13702次增益。偏差不被准确率预测（Spearman ρ=-0.19, p=0.21），不随规模或前沿代际收敛，且被指令和推理后训练加剧。

Conclusion: LLM在F-ICL上的行为暴露了其缺乏真正的算法推理能力：它们的输出分布与贝叶斯最优解有系统性差距，表现为循环缺失的归纳偏差、非单调更新和不受规模/训练改善的固有问题。该基准揭示了模式补全与算法推理之间的可量化鸿沟。

Abstract: Whether large language models perform genuine algorithmic reasoning or mere pattern completion is hard to test, because most benchmarks lack a ground truth for correct inductive inference. We introduce F-ICL, an in-context-learning benchmark that supplies one exactly. Using the Turing-complete machine F, complement-symmetrised into sF to remove output-polarity bias, we exhaustively enumerate all 1.5 billion programs of length $L\le13$ and compute the Bayes-optimal posterior in closed form under a bounded universal (Levin--Solomonoff) prior; models are scored by how closely their served distributions approach it at matched evidence. Each task is paired with its bitwise complement, on which the optimum scores identically, so an original-twin gap isolates the model's inductive bias. Across 105 serving configurations spanning 37 open models (0.8B--675B) and frontier systems from four laboratories, models answer up to 92\% of queries correctly, yet 45 of 46 models yield distributions farther from the optimum than a keystroke reference, and their behaviour is bracketed by low-order prefix statistics fitted only on visible evidence. That reference is itself an algorithmic mixture, induced by a print-only machine with no loops, so the panel's implied measure sits closer to a loop-free mixture than to the loop-bearing optimum, independently of the reference machine. Updating is also non-monotone, which no prior explains: a Bayes-rational solved set can only grow in this realisable, noiseless setting, yet added examples produce $6{,}545$ solved-to-unsolved transitions against $13{,}702$ gains. The gap is not predicted by accuracy (Spearman $ρ=-0.19$, $p=0.21$), does not close with scale or across frontier generations in the serving modes that expose distributions, and is widened by instruction and reasoning post-training. F-ICL is released as an open, reproducible benchmark and toolkit.

</details>


### [315] [Latent-Regime Bias Auditing for Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01599)
*Arthur Chagas,Pedro Bento,Yan Aquino,Arthur Buzelin,Wagner Meira,Cristiano Arbex Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的审计框架，用于评估波动率预测在潜在市场状态（regime）下的可靠性，克服传统RMSE/MAE等聚合指标掩盖条件性失败的缺陷。框架通过时间序列表征学习、聚类识别状态、样本外分配状态，并比较聚合精度与状态条件偏差、尾部低估及经济损失。在加密货币和ETF数据上的实验表明，聚合精度优秀的模型仍可能存在严重的状态特定偏差和尾部低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波动率预测评估依赖RMSE、MAE等聚合精度指标，但这些指标会掩盖在不同市场状态下预测的条件性失效问题，而这对于风险管理至关重要。

Method: 1）对市场状态窗口学习时间序列表征；2）仅用训练信息将表征聚类为市场状态；3）样本外分配数据到相应状态；4）比较聚合预测行为与状态条件偏差、尾部低估偏差及对低估敏感的经济损失函数。

Result: 在加密货币和ETF资产的日频波动率预测实验中，具有竞争性聚合精度的模型仍可能表现出显著的状态特定偏差和严重的尾部低估。

Conclusion: 波动率预测评估不应只关注平均误差，还应识别预测在哪些市场状态下、以何种方式失效。该框架将预测评估从"哪个模型平均最准确"转变为"识别看似准确的预测在哪些市场状态下有条件地失败"。

Abstract: Volatility forecasts are commonly evaluated with aggregate accuracy metrics such as RMSE and MAE, but these metrics can hide conditional failures that matter for risk management. This paper proposes a model-agnostic audit framework for evaluating whether volatility forecasts remain reliable across latent market regimes. We learn time-series representations of market-state windows, cluster them into regimes using only training information, assign regimes out of sample, and compare aggregate forecast behavior with regime-conditional bias, tail-underprediction, and underprediction-sensitive economic losses. Applied to daily volatility forecasting across cryptocurrency and ETF assets, the audit shows that models with competitive aggregate accuracy can still exhibit substantial regime-specific bias and severe tail underprediction. The results suggest that volatility forecasting should be evaluated not only by average error, but also by where and how forecasts become unreliable. Our framework shifts forecast evaluation from asking which model is most accurate on average to identifying the market regimes in which apparently accurate forecasts fail conditionally. Reproducibility: https://github.com/arthurchagas1/Latent-Regime-Bias-Auditing-for-Volatility-Forecasting

</details>


### [316] [Online Algorithms via Minimax and Posterior Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01616)
*Thomas Kesselheim,Marco Molinaro,Kalen Patton,Sahil Singla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的在线算法竞争性分析框架：通过最小最大视角和Yao原理，将最坏情况竞争比分析约简为任意相关先验分布下的贝叶斯在线设计问题，并提出"后验匹配"（posterior matching）原则——在每个时步选择与离线最优解的后验过程最接近的可行在线动作。该原则在集合覆盖、负载均衡、匹配等经典在线分数优化问题中取得最优或近似最优的竞争比保证，并通过舍入约简将结果扩展到加权分页、星形度量MTS、租滑雪等随机化整数问题上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线算法的竞争性分析虽然核心，但上界分析往往高度依赖具体问题，缺乏统一的通用方法论。作者希望借助最小最大视角和Yao原理，构建一个可复用的统一分析框架，摆脱针对不同问题分别设计分析技巧的现状。

Method: 具体方法包括：(1) 利用Yao原理将最坏情况竞争分析约简为任意相关先验下的贝叶斯在线设计；(2) 定义离线最优分数解的后验过程X^(t)；(3) 提出后验匹配原则，即在每个时步选择在线可行约束下最贴近当前后验的动作；(4) 将竞争比保证的证明归结为后验过程产生的向量鞅的关键概率不等式；(5) 通过已知的舍入约简将分数结果转化为随机化整数算法保证。

Result: 该框架在多个经典在线分数优化问题（集合覆盖、负载均衡、匹配和更一般的资源分配问题）中取得最优或近乎最优的竞争比，恢复或改进了现有基于范数/凹目标的状态最优界；通过舍入约简，进一步在加权分页、星形度量的MTS以及租滑雪等问题上获得随机化整数算法保证。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个从任意相关先验的贝叶斯在线设计到信息论意义下最坏情况竞争比保证的可复用路线图，为在线算法的竞争性分析建立了统一的通用框架，有望推广至更广泛的优化问题类别中。

Abstract: Competitive analysis is central to the study of online algorithms, but upper bounds are often highly problem-specific. We develop a more unifying methodology via the minimax viewpoint. Guided by Yao's principle, we reduce worst-case competitive analysis to Bayesian online design under an arbitrary correlated prior over arrival sequences. For such a prior, let $X^*$ be the hindsight-optimal fractional solution for the realized instance, and let $X^{(t)}=\mathbb E[X^*\mid \mathcal F_t]$ be its posterior process. Our guiding rule is posterior matching: at each time $t$, choose the feasible online action that tracks the current posterior $X^{(t)}$ as closely as the online constraints permit.
  We show that this single principle yields optimal or near-optimal guarantees for several classical online fractional problems, including set cover, load balancing, matching and more general resource-allocation problems, recovering or improving state-of-the-art bounds in these settings with norm/concave objectives. Via known rounding reductions, it also yields randomized integral guarantees for weighted paging, MTS on star metrics, and ski-rental. At a technical level, our analysis reduces competitive guarantees to key probabilistic inequalities for the vector martingales generated by the posterior of the offline optimum. The resulting framework gives a reusable route from Bayesian online design under arbitrary correlated priors to information-theoretic worst-case competitive guarantees.

</details>


### [317] [QWRF-Net: A Quantum-Wavelet Framework with Rectified Flow for Short-Term Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01626)
*Zhuo Wang,Chaorong Li,Wenjie Luo,Chuanhu Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QWRF-Net，一种结合量子-小波分解与整流流的短临降水临近预报框架，通过小波子带分解和差异化量子调制增强多尺度降水表征，并利用整流流非自回归解码器实现稳定的多步生成，在KNMI雷达和SEVIR基准上取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短临降水临近预报对水文气象预警至关重要，尤其是在强对流降雨可能引发城市内涝、山洪等高影响灾害时。然而雷达降水场包含强耦合的多尺度结构，且预报质量在后续预报时效上往往退化，难以在整个预警相关时段内保持强降水核心及其空间组织，这是预警导向临近预报的关键挑战。

Method: QWRF-Net的核心方法包括：(1) 将潜在特征显式分解为小波子带以改善降水的条件表征；(2) 在分解后的潜在空间中进行差异化的量子启发式调制；(3) 通过基于整流流的非自回归解码器生成未来序列。

Result: 在KNMI雷达和SEVIR基准上采用统一评估协议进行实验，QWRF-Net取得良好的整体性能，在中高降水阈值、极端事件子集以及强降水核心和细尺度结构保持方面均有相对一致的提升。消融实验表明小波尺度解耦、差异化子带调制和基于流的生成在框架内提供了互补的收益。

Conclusion: 联合增强多尺度降水表征与稳定的多步生成是预警导向短临降水临近预报的有前景方向，所提改进也可能为下游水文和预警应用提供更有用的降水依据。

Abstract: Short-term precipitation nowcasting is important for hydrometeorological early warning, especially when intense convective rainfall may trigger urban flooding, flash floods, and other high-impact hazards. A key challenge in warning-oriented nowcasting is that radar precipitation fields contain strongly coupled multi-scale structures, while forecast quality often degrades at later lead times, making it difficult to preserve intense precipitation cores and their spatial organization over the full warning-relevant horizon. To address this problem, we propose QWRF-Net, a quantum-wavelet framework with rectified flow for short-term precipitation nowcasting. The core idea is to improve the conditional representation of precipitation by explicitly decomposing latent features into wavelet sub-bands and then performing differentiated quantum-inspired modulation in the decomposed latent space, before generating future sequences through a rectified-flow-based non-autoregressive decoder. Experiments on the KNMI radar and SEVIR benchmarks under a unified evaluation protocol show that QWRF-Net achieves favorable overall performance, with relatively consistent gains at medium-to-high precipitation thresholds, on an extreme-event subset, and in preserving intense precipitation cores and fine-scale structures. Ablation results further indicate that wavelet-based scale disentanglement, differentiated sub-band modulation, and flow-based generation provide complementary benefits within the proposed framework. Overall, these results suggest that jointly enhancing multi-scale precipitation representation and stable multi-step generation is a promising direction for warning-oriented short-term precipitation nowcasting. The observed improvements may also provide a more useful precipitation basis for downstream hydrological and warning-related applications.

</details>


### [318] [GraphIR: Architecture-Level Search States for LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01633)
*Zhen Liu,Wanqi Zhou,Shuanghao Bai,Yuhan Liu,Jinjun Wang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphIR，一种架构感知的中间表示，用于增强LLM引导的神经架构搜索。GraphIR通过计算骨架、突变表面和有效性包络三个互补视图组织候选架构，并构建了NAS-Dependency基准（120个问题，覆盖六个依赖推理维度）。在六个下游基准上取得最佳搜索性能，同时保持相当的模型规模和高效的端到端NAS效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM直接在可执行神经网络程序上进行NAS时，代码级灵活性无法提供有效突变所需的架构状态——LLM需要从实现细节中推断张量依赖、可编辑组件和兼容性约束，存在表示不匹配问题。

Method: 提出GraphIR架构感知中间表示，通过三个互补视图组织每个候选架构：(1) 计算骨架，描述张量流；(2) 突变表面，暴露可编辑模块和操作；(3) 有效性包络，捕获接口契约、传播形状和下游依赖。

Result: 构建NAS-Dependency基准（120问、六维依赖推理），诊断显示GraphIR在识别精确生产者出现、追踪依赖传播、诊断接口和失败风险方面特别有效。在CLRS等六个下游基准上取得最佳整体搜索性能，同时保持可比模型规模和良好的端到端NAS效率。

Conclusion: 突变导向的架构状态为可执行神经程序与LLM引导的架构演化之间提供了有效接口。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable neural architecture search (NAS) directly over executable neural network programs. However, code-level flexibility does not provide the architecture state needed for effective mutation: LLMs must infer tensor dependencies, editable components, and compatibility constraints from implementation details. To address this representation mismatch, we propose GraphIR, an architecture-aware intermediate representation that supplements executable programs with a mutation-aligned candidate state. GraphIR organizes each candidate through three complementary views: a computation skeleton describing tensor flow, a mutation surface exposing editable modules and operations, and a validity envelope capturing interface contracts, propagated shapes, and downstream dependencies. To evaluate our method, we construct NAS-Dependency, a 120-question benchmark covering six complementary dependency-reasoning dimensions. The diagnostic shows that GraphIR is particularly effective at identifying exact producer occurrences, tracing dependency propagation, and diagnosing interface and failure risks. Across six downstream benchmarks including CLRS, GraphIR achieves the best overall search performance while maintaining comparable model size and favorable end-to-end NAS efficiency when integrated into OpenEvolve. These results show that a mutation-oriented architecture state provides an effective interface between executable neural programs and LLM-guided architecture evolution.

</details>


### [319] [Evaluating Forecasting Techniques for Hardware Errors on a Large-scale HPC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01648)
*Kaiyuan Liao,Xiwei Xuan,Tanwi Mallick,Kevin Brown,Christopher D. Carothers,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用Theta超级计算机七年的生产日志，系统评估了经典统计模型和深度学习模型在HPC硬件错误时间序列预测上的有效性。结果表明，预测效果高度依赖于错误序列的时间结构：周期性且结构稳定的错误可被LSTM和Transformer等模型准确预测，而稀疏突发型错误则难以预测。文章旨在提供经验性指导而非部署就绪的预测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统中的硬件错误日志虽然能提供异常行为的早期信号，但现代预测方法在有效预测这些错误方面仍面临挑战，需要系统性地探索时间序列预测方法在HPC硬件错误动力学上的适用边界。

Method: 使用来自Theta超级计算机的七年生产日志，对比评估经典统计方法与深度学习模型（包括LSTM和Transformer架构）在硬件错误时间序列预测上的效果，同时引入时序特征以增强模型预测能力。

Result: 预测有效性强烈依赖于错误序列的时间结构：规律出现且结构稳定的错误可以被准确建模（尤以带有时间特征的LSTM和Transformer表现最佳），而稀疏且以突发为主的错误则难以预测。

Conclusion: 本文不提出部署就绪的故障预测框架，而是提供关于何时时间序列预测有效的经验性指导，并指出提升HPC硬件错误分析预测精度的潜在改进方向。

Abstract: Hardware error logs in high-performance computing (HPC) systems provide early signals of abnormal behavior, yet there remain challenges in effectively forecasting these errors using modern predictive methods. This work investigates the boundaries of applying time series forecasting to HPC hardware error dynamics. We use seven years of production logs from the Theta supercomputer to evaluate the predictive efficacy of classical statistical and deep learning models. Our results show that forecasting effectiveness depends strongly on the temporal structure of the error series: regularly occurring and structurally stable errors can be modeled accurately, particularly by LSTM and Transformer architectures with temporal features, while sparse and burst-dominated errors remain difficult to predict. Rather than proposing a deployment-ready failure prediction framework, this study provides empirical guidance on when forecasting is effective and highlights potential directions for improving forecasting accuracy in HPC hardware error analysis.

</details>


### [320] [Sharp Root Anti-Concentration via Projective Incidence and Ordered Root Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01670)
*Zijun Wang,Yuchen Miao,Yifan Hu,Huanmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Balcan、Pegden和Sharma提出的关于在线优化分段Lipschitz函数时的一维局部根反集中问题。对于齐次特征曲线和密度有界的系数分布，证明了最坏情况区间命中常数等于A乘以截面平均投影相遇速度；对于立方体支撑的系数，该速度与投影Lipschitz常数在万能常数意义下等价，给出了尖锐的、维度无关的表征并消除了此前√N的损失。对于首一d次多项式，证明了区间命中常数有限当且仅当有序实根分布具有有界密度，且因子-d比较是尖锐的。还给出了两个图学习应用，分别实现期望遗憾Õ((An²De^{BD}/ℓ+1)√T)和Õ((qn²κ+1)√T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决在线优化分段Lipschitz函数中Balcan等人提出的局部根反集中问题。已有方法存在√N的损失，且无法处理困难或奇异的系数分布，需要给出维度无关的尖锐表征，并将根结构分析转化为可验证的判定准则。

Method: 本文发展了一维局部根反集中理论：对齐次特征曲线，利用投影相遇速度的截面平均给出精确的区间命中常数；对立方体支撑系数，建立与投影Lipschitz常数在万能常数意义下的等价性。对首一d次多项式，通过条件与联合系数空间面积公式以及两图证书(fwo-chart certificate)证明了区间命中常数有限当且仅当有序实根分布有界密度，并构造了成本敏感的Gaussian-RBF调和分类器和公共偏移多项式核模型两种图学习应用。

Result: 关键结果为：(1) 区间命中常数等于A乘以截面平均投影相遇速度，且在立方体支撑情形下与投影Lipschitz常数等价（去掉√N损失，实现维度无关）；(2) 首一d次多项式的区间命中常数有限当且仅当有序实根分布有界密度，因子-d比较尖锐；(3) 两个图学习应用分别达到Õ((An²De^{BD}/ℓ+1)√T)和Õ((qn²κ+1)√T)的期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 这项工作完成了从根反集中到遗憾界的过渡链，给出了维度无关的尖锐表征，并且判定准则对依赖和奇异系数分布都是可验证的，具有理论与实践双重意义。

Abstract: This paper answers the one-dimensional local root anti-concentration questions posed by Balcan, Pegden, and Sharma in the context of online optimization of piecewise-Lipschitz functions. For a homogeneous feature curve and coefficients whose density relative to the uniform law on a symmetric convex body $K$ is bounded by $A$, we show that the worst-case interval-hitting constant equals $A$ times a section-averaged projective incidence speed. For cube-supported coefficients, this speed is equivalent, up to universal constants, to the projective Lipschitz constant. This yields a sharp, dimension-free characterization and removes the previous $\sqrt N$ loss. For monic degree-$d$ polynomials under arbitrary coefficient laws, we prove that the interval-hitting constant is finite if and only if the ordered real-root laws have bounded densities, with a factor-$d$ comparison that is sharp. Conditional and joint coefficient-space area formulas, together with a two-chart certificate, make this criterion verifiable for dependent and singular coefficient laws. We also give two graph-learning applications that complete the transition-to-regret chain. A cost-sensitive Gaussian-RBF harmonic classifier uses the projective incidence theorem and achieves expected regret $\widetilde O((An^2D e^{BD}/\ell+1)\sqrt T)$. A common-offset polynomial-kernel model uses rigid translation of the ordered roots and achieves $\widetilde O((qn^2κ+1)\sqrt T)$ regret, even when the induced coefficient law is singular in the ambient coefficient space.

</details>


### [321] [Progressive Agent Skill Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01678)
*Junhao Shen,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-α，一种基于强化学习的技能生成方法，将技能构建分解为可独立评估的顺序编辑过程，并引入新颖的回滚奖励机制，通过在锚定查询下比较原始与编辑后技能的下游执行效果来评估每一步编辑。实验表明该方法在文档到技能和经验到技能两种场景下均优于基于启发式或流水线的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能生成方法依赖启发式或流水线式整合，需要针对不同证据源专门设计。基于学习的方法虽能统一建模跨异构来源的技能生成，但面临关键挑战：技能缺乏基于相关性或正确性的自然监督信号，其价值只能通过是否改善智能体在下游任务上的表现来判断。

Method: 提出Skill-α强化学习方法：1) 将技能生成建模为顺序编辑过程，将技能构建分解为可单独评估的编辑步骤；2) 引入回滚奖励机制，通过在锚定查询上比较原始技能与编辑后技能的下游执行结果来评估每次编辑的质量；3) 通过逐步生成方式迭代优化技能质量。

Result: 在CL-Bench和tau2-bench基准上，Skill-α在文档到技能和经验到技能两种设定下均优于基于启发式或流水线的方法。在GPT-4o为主worker下，相比最强技能生成基线，平均下游成功率在CL-Bench上提升3.3个百分点，在tau2-bench上提升6.7个百分点。消融实验验证了回滚奖励和逐步生成的重要性。

Conclusion: Skill-α通过强化学习框架将技能生成形式化为可逐步评估的顺序编辑过程，利用回滚奖励有效解决了技能缺乏天然监督信号的问题，在多种异构证据源场景下生成更高质量的智能体技能，为学习驱动的技能生成提供了统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing skill generation methods largely rely on heuristics or pipeline-style consolidation, which must be specially designed for different evidence sources. In contrast, learning-based approaches offer a more unified way to model skill generation across heterogeneous sources. However, learning-based skill generation remains challenging because skills lack a natural supervision signal based on relevance or correctness; their value can largely be determined only by whether they improve the behavior of the agent on downstream tasks. To address this challenge, we propose Skill-$α$, a reinforcement learning method for progressively generating high-quality agent skills. Specifically, we formulate skill generation as a sequential editing process that decomposes skill construction into individually evaluable edits, and introduce a novel rollback reward that evaluates each edit by comparing downstream execution under the original and edited skills on an anchored query. Extensive experiments show that Skill-$α$ generates more effective skills than methods based on heuristics or pipelines in both document-to-skill and experience-to-skill settings. Under the main GPT-4o worker, Skill-$α$ improves average downstream success rates over the strongest skill-generation baseline by 3.3 points on CL-Bench and 6.7 points on tau2-bench. Further ablations validate the importance of rollback reward and progressive generation.

</details>


### [322] [Beckmann Transport Models: From Autonomous Flows to One-Step Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01692)
*Lee Cheuk-Kit,Florentin Coeurdoux,Peter Potaptchik,Yilun Du,Michael Samuel Albergo,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间无关速度场（自治流）的流匹配方法，可精确地将两个分布映射，前提是目标分布是奇异的（即支持在低维数据流形上）。同时证明该一步生成映射是某个简单守恒方程的唯一解，可直接从样本中学习。这一框架为非平衡匹配（equilibrium matching）和泊松流生成模型提供了统一视角，并在ImageNet 256x256上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法通常依赖时间相关的速度场来构建生成模型，这带来计算复杂度和训练不稳定性。作者旨在探索是否可以用时间无关的自治流来精确映射分布，从而简化生成过程并实现高效的一步生成，同时纠正现有方法中的不一致性。

Method: 提出自治流（autonomous flow）的概念，利用时间无关的常微分方程（ODE）在奇异目标分布（支持在低维流形上）上实现精确分布映射。将该映射与Beckmann输运问题中的通量约束建立动力学联系，推导出一守恒方程，其唯一解即一步生成映射，可基于样本直接学习。该方法统一了泊松流生成模型和平衡匹配（equilibrium matching）等现有方法。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256数据集上验证了自治流和一步映射方法的有效性，并展示了该理论如何修正现有方法中的不一致性。

Conclusion: 自治流为分布迁移提供了一种更简洁且理论上自洽的框架，通过时间无关的速度场实现精确的一步生成，为生成模型的设计提供了新的理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: We propose an instantiation of flow matching that relies on a time-independent velocity field (an \emph{autonomous flow}) to exactly map between two distributions, so long as the target is singular, i.e.\ supported on a lower-dimensional data manifold. We also show that the one-step generative map associated with this flow is the unique solution of a simple conservation equation, which can be used to learn the map directly from samples. These autonomous flows and maps give a dynamical meaning to the flux constraint of Beckmann's transportation problem. Their construction provides a unifying framework that recovers, for instance, the closed-form Poisson-flow generative model and equilibrium matching with a quadratic flow-matching regression loss. We illustrate how this theory corrects inconsistencies in existing methods and demonstrate the effectiveness of the autonomous flow and the one-step map on ImageNet 256x256.

</details>


### [323] [Beyond On-Policy Exploration: Integrating External Policy Rollouts for Reinforcement Learning in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01717)
*Wonseok Lee,Jimyeong Kim,Jungmin Ko,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ERILS方法，通过长度控制与来源特异性奖励处理，将外部更强策略的高奖励rollout整合进扩散大语言模型的强化学习，在Sudoku、Countdown和MATH500上显著提升多采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型的强化学习通常依赖目标模型自身的on-policy rollout，但成功的on-policy rollout稀少时，训练只能获得少量正奖励，进展有限。因此需要引入外部更强策略产生的高奖励rollout来缓解这一问题，但直接引入面临rollout长度差异和奖励联合处理不稳定两大挑战。

Method: 提出ERILS（External Rollout Integration with Length Control and Source-Specific Processing）框架，包含两个核心组件：1）外部rollout长度控制机制，统一外部rollout与on-policy rollouts的长度；2）来源特异性奖励处理，对on-policy和外部rollouts的奖励分别进行处理，避免联合处理带来的训练不稳定。

Result: 在Sudoku、Countdown和MATH500三个任务的零样本评估中，ERILS在全部任务上提升了多采样性能。在Sudoku上，ERILS达到98.4%的best-of-4完成准确率，而最强基线仅为40.3%。在Sudoku上，ERILS在128、256和512 token生成长度下均保持约90%的确定性单次生成准确率。消融分析显示，受控长度的外部rollout优于未受控版本，来源特异性奖励处理避免了联合处理导致的训练崩溃。

Conclusion: rollout构造和奖励处理是将外部rollout整合进dLLM强化学习时的重要设计维度。ERILS证明了长度控制和来源特异性处理能够有效利用外部高奖励rollout，显著提升扩散大语言模型的强化学习性能。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning methods for diffusion large language models (dLLMs) commonly rely on on-policy rollouts generated by the target dLLM itself. When successful on-policy rollouts are scarce, however, on-policy training may receive little positive reward and make only limited progress. To mitigate this problem, we explore incorporating higher-reward rollouts generated by a stronger external policy alongside on-policy rollouts from the target dLLM. However, directly incorporating these external rollouts introduces two practical challenges: differences in rollout length and instability when jointly processing rewards from on-policy and external rollouts. To address these challenges, we propose External Rollout Integration with Length Control and Source-Specific Processing (ERILS), which controls external-rollout length and processes the rewards of on-policy and external rollouts separately. Experiments on Sudoku, Countdown, and MATH500 under zero-shot evaluation show that ERILS improves multi-sample performance across all three tasks, with the largest gains on Sudoku. On Sudoku, ERILS achieves 98.4% best-of-4 completion accuracy, compared with 40.3% for the strongest baseline. ERILS also maintains approximately 90% deterministic single-completion accuracy on Sudoku across generation lengths of 128, 256, and 512 tokens. Our component analysis further shows that length-controlled external rollouts are more effective than uncontrolled external rollouts, and that source-specific reward processing avoids the training collapse observed with joint reward processing. These results show that rollout construction and reward processing are important design dimensions when integrating external rollouts into dLLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [324] [LLM-Guided Retrieval for Prediction of Molecular Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01734)
*Betty Xiong,Jan-Christian Huetter,Gabriele Scalia,Tommaso Biancalani,Sepideh Maleki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM-Guided Retrieval (LGR)框架，将分子扰动预测建模为"检索-聚合"问题：利用大语言模型在目标细胞系中已测序的化合物中检索生物相关的邻居药物，再通过固定的均值聚合器组合其表达变化来预测未测药物的响应。在Tahoe-100M单细胞扰动图谱上，LGR在未见药物、未见细胞系和开放世界三种场景下均优于ChemCPA、基于化学结构的kNN和药物均值基线，尤其在未见细胞系泛化中提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对跨细胞系的小分子扰动转录组响应进行预测对药物发现至关重要，但穷举测试所有药物-细胞组合在实验上不可行。作者认为无需构建复杂预测模型，而是可以通过检索生物学相关的已测化合物来近似未测药物的响应，因此探索将LLM作为检索模块以提供生物学先验。

Method: 将分子扰动预测形式化为检索-聚合框架：LGR首先使用大语言模型对目标细胞系中已测序的候选邻居药物进行排序，然后使用固定的均值聚合器将这些邻居药物观测到的表达增量（expression deltas）进行平均，生成对未测药物的预测。评估设置在Tahoe-100M单细胞扰动图谱上，覆盖未见药物、未见细胞系和开放世界三种泛化场景。

Result: LGR在所有设定下均优于药物均值、ChemCPA和基于化学结构的kNN基线，在未见细胞系泛化场景下提升最为显著（更高的相关性、更低的误差）。此外，LGR在基因调控的方向（符号）准确性上表现更好，表明即使在幅度指标相近时也能更好地恢复生物学上有意义的扰动效应。

Conclusion: 零样本分子扰动预测的关键驱动因素是检索质量而非预测器复杂度；LLM作为受约束的检索模块能够提供有价值的生物学先验，从而显著提升跨细胞系的扰动响应预测性能。

Abstract: Predicting transcriptomic responses to small-molecule perturbations across cell lines is central to drug discovery, but exhaustive profiling of drug-cell combinations is infeasible. We frame molecular perturbation prediction as retrieve-and-aggregate: approximate an unmeasured drug's response in a cell line by aggregating measured responses of a small set of biologically related compounds. We propose LLM-Guided Retrieval (LGR), where a large language model (LLM) ranks candidate neighbor drugs (restricted to those profiled in the target cell line); after which a fixed mean aggregator combines their observed expression deltas to form the prediction. We evaluate on the Tahoe-100M single-cell perturbation atlas under unseen-drug, unseen-cell-line, and open-world regimes. LGR consistently improves over drug mean, ChemCPA, and chemistry-based kNN baselines, with the strongest gains for unseen cell-line generalization, where it achieves higher correlation and lower error than mean baselines. Across settings, LGR improves directional (sign) accuracy of gene regulation, indicating better recovery of biologically meaningful perturbation effects even when magnitude-based metrics are similar. These results suggest that retrieval quality, rather than predictor complexity, is a key driver of zero-shot molecular perturbation prediction, and that LLMs can provide a useful biological prior when used as constrained retrieval modules.

</details>


### [325] [Disagree to Accelerate: Closing the Loop on Diffusion Feature Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01740)
*Yanchao Li,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Wanhao Liu,Yanbo Wang,T. Y. Tsui,Jinfei Liu,Yuqiang Li,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RACER是一个训练无关的闭环控制器，利用预测特征间的分歧信号来动态决定何时及如何信任跳过去噪步骤中的特征预测，从而提升扩散模型加速采样的质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练无关特征预测加速方法主要聚焦于设计更强的预测器，但忽略了不同步骤间预测误差剧烈变化的事实。开环缓存机制在每个被跳过的步骤中无条件信任预测，导致加速力度增大时质量严重下降。关键问题不仅在于如何更好地预测，还在于何时以及多大程度上信任预测。

Method: RACER从缓存自身观测可靠性：两个预测一致处特征轨迹平滑，分歧处则预测困难，该分歧可作为低成本运行时信号且无需额外的去噪器评估。基于该信号，RACER采用两种响应机制：持续将不确定预测向最近计算的特征收缩，以及在最危险的步骤刷新特征并推迟后续一个已排定的步骤以补偿额外评估。同时推导了收缩的确定性误差界。

Result: 在相同去噪评估次数下，RACER在SD3.5-Large、FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-14B和HunyuanVideo上均优于最强开环基线（测试于DrawBench、VBench和COCO）。在SD3.5上，RACER在同等质量下采样更快，且在不同预测器设计上均能泛化，例如在Taylor基线上恢复大部分质量损失。

Conclusion: 可靠的扩散加速不仅取决于预测的质量，还取决于预测的使用方式。利用缓存自身的分歧信号进行闭环的信任控制，能有效提升加速采样的质量和效率，适用于多种模型与预测器设计。

Abstract: Training-free feature forecasting accelerates diffusion sampling by predicting features at skipped denoising steps. Recent work has mainly focused on designing stronger forecasters. Yet forecast error varies sharply across steps, and open-loop caches trust the forecast in full at every skipped step. This fixed trust is what breaks as acceleration turns aggressive. The missing question is not only how to forecast better, but when and how much to trust a forecast. We show that reliability can be observed from the cache itself. Two forecasts agree where the feature trajectory is smooth, and they diverge where prediction turns hard. Their disagreement is a cheap runtime signal, and it costs no extra denoiser evaluation. Based on this signal, we introduce RACER, a training-free closed-loop controller with two responses. It continuously shrinks uncertain forecasts toward the last computed feature. At the riskiest steps, RACER refreshes the feature and repays the added evaluation by skipping a later scheduled one. We derive a deterministic error bound for the shrinkage and empirically evaluate its validity and tightness across acceleration regimes. At the same number of denoiser evaluations, RACER improves the strongest open-loop baseline across SD3.5-Large, FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-14B, and HunyuanVideo on DrawBench, VBench, and COCO. On SD3.5, we further show that RACER samples faster at equal quality. RACER generalizes across forecasting designs as well. For example, it recovers much of the quality lost on a Taylor base. These results show that reliable diffusion acceleration also depends on how forecasts are used. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/RACER

</details>


### [326] [Toward Plasticity-Preserving KL Regularization for Capability Retention in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01743)
*Li Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Xiaohan Wang,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Tianhao Peng,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoKL（Correctness-Conditioned KL Regularization），一种条件正则化框架，将KL约束从完整输出分布收窄到正确性条件化的响应分布，以在保留基座模型已有能力的同时，最小化对新任务学习的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型后训练中的RL优化新目标会导致已有能力退化，标准全策略KL正则化虽然能缓解遗忘，但约束了整个响应分布，过度限制了探索和目标任务学习。作者希望设计一种更精确的约束，在保留已有能力的同时尽可能减少对新任务学习的干扰。

Method: CoKL将保留约束从全输出分布收窄至正确性条件化的响应分布。使用前向KL散度实例化CoKL，并推导出适用于RL训练的实际有限组（finite-group）训练目标。在总体层面，CoKL将正确响应的总概率与正确性条件化分布解耦，只正则化参考策略支持的正确答案之间的相对概率分配，而不直接锚定错误输出或总正确性质量。

Result: 理论证明全策略前向和反向KL正则化在参考策略不完美时会导致严格的最优正确性差距（optimal correctness gap），而CoKL能避免此局限。在受控多解环境和多模型规模的持续后训练实验中，CoKL在目标任务提升与已有能力保留之间取得了比现有正则化方法更优的平衡。

Conclusion: CoKL作为一种正确的条件化KL正则化框架，能够更精准地约束模型输出，在防止能力遗忘的同时不干扰新任务学习，为LLM后训练中的能力保留提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but optimization toward new objectives can degrade capabilities already present in the base model. KL regularization is widely used to mitigate such forgetting by constraining policy drift toward a reference model. However, standard full-policy KL regularization constrains the entire response distribution and may unnecessarily restrict exploration and target-task learning. This raises a natural question: can a more precise constraint preserve existing capabilities while minimizing interference with learning new tasks? To this end, we propose \underline{Co}rrectness-Conditioned \underline{KL} Regularization (CoKL), a conditional regularization framework that narrows the preservation constraint from the full output distribution to correctness-conditioned response distributions. We instantiate CoKL with forward KL divergence and derive a practical finite-group training objective for RL-based LLM post-training. At the population level, CoKL decouples the total probability assigned to correct responses from their correctness-conditioned distribution, thereby regularizing the relative probability allocation among reference-supported correct responses without directly anchoring incorrect outputs or total correctness mass. We further show that full-policy forward and reverse KL regularization induce a strict optimal correctness gap when the reference policy is imperfect, whereas CoKL avoids this limitation. Experiments in controlled multi-solution environments and continual post-training settings across multiple model scales demonstrate that CoKL achieves a more favorable balance between target-task improvement and prior-capability retention than existing regularization methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/CoKL.

</details>


### [327] [Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Radio Resource Management under Coupled Finite-Horizon Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01745)
*Yeonseo Jeong,Wonhyeok Ko,Sungweon Hong,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HeLyMARL，一种基于Lyapunov嵌入的异构多智能体强化学习框架，通过虚拟队列的漂移加惩罚分解，将有限时域的能量和切换预算约束转化为每时隙的统一奖励，解决密集无线网络中的用户关联、调度、基站激活与切换控制的联合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集无线网络中，最大化比例公平下的吞吐量需要联合管理用户关联、调度、基站激活和切换控制，同时受限于有限时域的能量与切换预算。这带来了基站侧能量管理与用户侧切换调节之间的根本矛盾。虽然MARL是解决这类分布式序贯控制的自然框架，但面临两大困难：有限时域预算约束无法在每个时隙评估，且非线性比例公平效用缺乏原理性的每时隙分解。

Method: HeLyMARL通过漂移加惩罚（drift-plus-penalty）分解与虚拟队列（virtual queues）机制，将能量和切换约束压力直接内化到统一的每时隙奖励中，从而将受约束的有限时域问题转化为无约束的MARL问题。该框架是异构的，能够建模不同类型的智能体（如基站与用户侧实体）之间的分布式决策。

Result: 与两种基于拉格朗日的方法对比揭示了一个时间尺度分离：拉格朗日松弛仅在训练轮次之间调节约束，而HeLyMARL的虚拟队列在情节内每个部分时域上限制累积预算消耗，提供了贪心Lyapunov控制无法实现的步调（pacing）保证。仿真结果表明HeLyMARL是唯一能够在整个时域内同时维持吞吐量-公平性平衡和连续服务的方案，优于传统MARL、基于Lyapunov的方法和有约束MARL基线，且不会出现预算过早耗尽。

Conclusion: HeLyMARL通过虚拟队列的Lyapunov嵌入方法有效解决了有限时域预算约束下密集无线网络分布式控制中的两个核心困难，在预算管理（pacing）上优于拉格朗日松弛和贪心Lyapunov方法，是唯一能同时保证吞吐量-公平性平衡与整个时域内不间断服务的方案。

Abstract: Maximizing throughput under proportional fairness in dense wireless networks requires jointly managing user association, scheduling, base station (BS) activation, and handover control under hard finite-horizon energy and handover budgets, which induces a fundamental tension between BS-side energy management and user-side handover regulation. While multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is a natural framework for such distributed sequential control, its application here faces two difficulties: finite-horizon budget constraints cannot be evaluated at each time slot, and the nonlinear proportional fairness utility admits no principled per-slot decomposition. We propose HeLyMARL, a Lyapunov-embedded heterogeneous MARL framework that resolves both via drift-plus-penalty decomposition with virtual queues. The energy and handover constraint pressures are internalized directly into a unified per-slot reward, converting the constrained finite-horizon problem into an unconstrained MARL problem. Comparison against two Lagrangian-based alternatives reveals a timescale separation: Lagrangian relaxation regulates constraints only across training episodes, whereas the virtual queues of HeLyMARL bound cumulative budget consumption at every partial horizon within an episode, a pacing guarantee beyond the reach of greedy Lyapunov-based control. Simulations show that HeLyMARL is the only method that sustains the throughput-fairness balance together with uninterrupted service throughout the horizon, outperforming conventional MARL, Lyapunov-based, and constrained MARL benchmarks without premature budget exhaustion.

</details>


### [328] [Multi-Source Dynamic Graph Learning for Compound-Flood Forecasting in Managed Coastal Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01775)
*Liangjun You,Min Wu,Orlando Woods,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种锚定式洪水预报框架，通过状态和前置时间依赖的有界残差校正，在保持本地时序预测稳定性的同时融合多站点信号，改善持续高水位平台期的预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复合洪水预报模型虽在平均误差指标上表现良好，但全局误差指标可能掩盖对长时间高水位平台期的较差复现能力，而这恰恰对洪水预警至关重要。由于水文气象和调度信号分布在异质监测站点上，单站点记录无法完整表征高水位动态，但跨站点信号的过度自由融合又会破坏本地时序预测的稳定性。

Method: 提出一种锚定式预报框架，通过状态依赖和前置时间依赖的有界残差校正来融合跨站点信息。利用水文气象与运行观测构建多源态势表征，自适应校准站点间关系与校正尺度，在保持本地时序预测作为稳定锚点的同时实现针对性的跨站点调整。

Result: 实验表明，选择性整合多站动态条件能够提高持续高水位平台期的预报可靠性，同时保持常规水文条件下的高精度。

Conclusion: 该框架超越了传统全局误差统计的评估方式，通过事件尺度高水位过程的时序对齐进行评价，证明所提方法可有效支撑洪水早期预警和水资源管理决策支持。

Abstract: Compound flooding in managed coastal systems is influenced by hydrological conditions and water-management activity observed across multiple monitoring stations. Current forecasting models can capture temporal dependencies with low average errors, but global error metrics may conceal poor reproduction of prolonged high-water plateaus that are relevant to flood early warning. Because hydrometeorological and operational signals are distributed across heterogeneous gages, single-site records do not fully represent high-water dynamics. Nevertheless, unconstrained fusion of cross-site signals can degrade the stability of local temporal forecasts. This work proposes an anchored forecasting framework that incorporates cross-site information through state- and lead-dependent bounded residual corrections. A multi-source regime representation constructed from hydrometeorological and operational observations adaptively calibrates inter-site relationships and correction scales, enabling targeted cross-site adjustment while preserving the local temporal forecast as a stable anchor. Beyond conventional global error statistics, we evaluate event-scale high-water characteristics through the temporal alignment of forecasted and observed high-water processes. Experiments demonstrate that selectively integrating multi-station dynamic conditions improves the prediction reliability of sustained high-water plateaus while maintaining high accuracy during routine hydrological conditions, supporting flood early warning and water-management decision support.

</details>


### [329] [ReFP-AD: Rectified Flow Preconditioning for Energy-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01793)
*Camile Lendering,Erkut Akdag,Joaquín Figueira,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReFP-AD通过最优传输耦合的整流流学习几何重参数化，将高维DINOv2 token嵌入映射到良态潜在空间，从而稳定EBM的有限步MCMC采样，实现统一的异常检测，在MVTec-AD和VisA上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一异常检测需要对高度异质的正常数据进行建模，而无需异常样本。虽然DINOv2等基础模型提供丰富的token表示，但在这些高维空间中利用这些表示进行显式密度估计仍然困难。EBM虽然提供了原则性框架，但在高维token空间中的训练因各向异性和强跨维相关性而不稳定，导致有限步MCMC采样退化。作者识别出这一不稳定性的根本原因是几何性的。

Method: 提出ReFP-AD框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过最优传输耦合的整流流学习几何重参数化，将高维token嵌入映射到良态潜在空间；(2) 在该预条件化空间中采用预条件SGLD进行稳定的持久对比散度训练，在完整维度的token空间中实现EBM训练。异常分数通过学习的能量景观的梯度范数推导。

Result: 在MVTec-AD上达到98.6%/97.9%的图像/像素AUROC，在VisA上达到97.3%/99.0%，相比先前的统一EBM基线在图像AUROC上最高提升+10.8%。消融实验证明几何重参数化对有限步MCMC和准确异常定位至关重要。

Conclusion: 几何重参数化是解决高维token空间中EBM训练不稳定性的关键，能够实现稳定的有限步MCMC采样，从而在统一异常检测中取得显著性能提升。该方法代码已开源。

Abstract: Unified anomaly detection requires modeling highly heterogeneous normal data without access to anomalous samples. While foundation models like DINOv2 provide rich token representations, leveraging these spaces for explicit density estimation remains challenging. Energy-Based Models (EBMs) offer a principled formulation, but their training in high-dimensional token spaces is unstable due to anisotropy and strong cross-dimensional correlations, which degrades finite-step Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. We identify this instability as fundamentally geometric and introduce ReFP-AD (Rectified Flow Preconditioning for Anomaly Detection), which learns a geometric reparameterization that maps high-dimensional embeddings into a well-conditioned latent space via an optimal transport (OT)-coupled rectified flow. This preconditioning enables stable persistent contrastive divergence with preconditioned Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics (SGLD) in full-dimensional token spaces. Anomaly scores are then derived from the learned energy landscape using gradient norms. Under a strict unified protocol on the MVTec-AD and VisA datasets, ReFP-AD achieves 98.6%/97.9% Image/Pixel AUROC on MVTec-AD and 97.3%/99.0% on VisA, outperforming prior unified EBM baselines by up to +10.8% in Image AUROC. Ablation experiments demonstrate that geometric reparameterization is critical for finite-step MCMC and accurate anomaly localization in high-dimensional token spaces. Code is available at https://github.com/CLendering/ReFP-AD

</details>


### [330] [LEAP: Lean Environment-Feedback via Adaptive Pruning for Code RL in GPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01804)
*Tankun Li,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAP框架，一个面向低层硬件加速器对齐的可扩展、计算高效的多轮强化学习框架，通过难度条件剪枝（DCP）和基于排名的奖励公式来解决CUDA内核生成中稀疏奖励和高编译延迟的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GRPO的无评论家RL框架虽在高级代码生成上表现优异，但应用于低层系统编程（如CUDA内核生成）时面临严峻挑战：二元通过/失败奖励导致信号严重稀疏，多轮环境反馈循环存在高编译延迟和跨轨迹奖励稀释问题。需要一种新的范式来应对这些低层硬件编程特有的困难。

Method: LEAP包含两个核心组件：(1) 难度条件剪枝（DCP），一种动态门控机制，自适应地剪除简单和过于灾难性的任务，将资源密集的编译和硬件探索集中到高价值复杂任务上；(2) 基于排名的奖励（Rank-Based Reward），从GRPO rollout组内的成对锦标赛结果中推导无尺度的相对优势，内在惩罚简单提示上的token低效性，同时最大化挑战性分布的梯度信号。

Result: 经验评估表明，LEAP在首轮熟练度和多轮调试韧性上都优于基线，且比无剪枝的多轮基线收敛更快，证明了其在低层代码RL任务中的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: LEAP建立了低层代码RL的实用范式，通过自适应资源分配和排名驱动的奖励机制，在保持多轮调试能力的同时显著提升训练效率和收敛速度，为硬件加速器感知的低层系统编程RL提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) via reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced code generation capabilities. To bypass the heavy memory footprint of critic networks, current state-of-the-art frameworks leverage critic-free paradigms like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) tied to rule-based verification sandboxes. However, applying these frameworks to low-level systems programming, such as CUDA kernel generation-presents severe challenges: binary pass/fail rewards introduce severe signal sparsity, while multi-turn environmental feedback loops suffer from prohibitive compilation latencies and reward dilution across trajectories. In this work, we introduce LEAP (Lean Environment-Feedback via Adaptive Pruning), a scalable and computationally efficient multi-turn RL framework optimized for low-level hardware accelerator alignment. LEAP features Difficulty-Conditioned Pruning (DCP), a dynamic gating mechanism that adaptively cuts off simple and overly catastrophic tasks from multi-turn expansion, focusing resource-heavy compilation and hardware exploration exclusively on high-value, complex tasks. To fully operationalize these paths without manual hyperparameter engineering, we propose a Rank-Based Reward formulation. By deriving scale-free relative advantages from pairwise tournament outcomes within the GRPO rollout group, our method inherently penalizes token inefficiency on simple prompts while maximizing learning gradients on challenging distributions. Empirical evaluations show that LEAP achieves superior first-turn proficiency and robust multi-turn debugging resilience while converging faster than unpruned multi-turn baselines, establishing a practical paradigm for low-level code RL.

</details>


### [331] [Predictive Maintenance: Deep Learning-Based Remaining Useful Life Prediction for Combat Aircraft Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01819)
*Fatih Ürgen,Doğay Altınel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的预测性维护模型，利用NASA C-MAPSS FD001/FD004数据集预测战斗机发动机剩余使用寿命。模型通过滑动窗口将多变量传感器数据转化为序列块，在FD001上取得R²=0.8901、RMSE=13.28、NASA风险评分320.34，在FD004上RMSE=15.71，并在30周期关键阈值下达到0.9973的AUC，同时开发了决策支持模拟器验证战斗飞行剖面下的维护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升战斗机发动机作战就绪率并降低非计划维护成本，传统维护方式在动态任务剖面下往往不够充分，需要更精确的剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计方法。

Method: 采用深度学习方法从多变量传感器数据中自主提取特征；分别使用50步和30步滑动窗口将FD001和FD004数据转换为序列块；模型与RF、CNN-LSTM和BiLSTM基线进行对比验证；并开发了决策支持模拟器用于验证战斗飞行剖面下的维护方案。

Result: FD001数据集上：R²=0.8901，RMSE=13.28，NASA风险评分320.34；FD004多工况数据集上RMSE=15.71，表明模型具备泛化能力；在30周期关键阈值下AUC达0.9973，可靠性高。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度学习预测性维护模型在时域退化特征自主提取方面优于对比基线，在单工况和多工况数据集上均表现出良好的准确性和泛化能力，配合决策支持模拟器可有效保障战斗机发动机在激进战斗飞行剖面下的维护可靠性与作战就绪率。

Abstract: To improve the operational readiness of combat aircraft engines and reduce unplanned maintenance costs, accurately estimating the remaining useful life (RUL) is critical. Traditional maintenance often proves insufficient under dynamic mission profiles. In this study, a deep learning-based predictive maintenance model capable of autonomously extracting features from multivariate sensor data was developed. Using the NASA C-MAPSS FD001 and FD004 datasets, data were converted into sequential blocks via 50- and 30-step sliding windows, respectively. The model's architectural superiority in autonomously extracting temporal degradation features was validated against RF, CNN-LSTM, and BiLSTM baselines. On FD001, it achieved an R-squared (R2) of 0.8901, a 13.28 RMSE, and a 320.34 NASA risk score, demonstrating generalizability on the multi-regime FD004 dataset with a 15.71 RMSE. The proposed maintenance protocol achieved a 0.9973 AUC at the critical 30-cycle threshold, ensuring high reliability. Additionally, a decision-support simulator has been developed to validate this protocol under aggressive combat flight profiles.

</details>


### [332] [WorldDynCache: Risk-Controlled Latent Dynamics Approximation for Diffusion World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01845)
*Leyang Chen,Junyi Wu,Shaoqiu Zhang,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldDynCache，一种风险可控的潜变量动力学近似框架，用于加速扩散世界模型的推理。它包含一个轻量级潜变量转移风险估计器来跟踪并校准近似缺陷的累积影响，以及一个条件与阶段感知的提升潜变量代理模型来近似潜变量演化。在HunyuanVoyager-13B和Aether-5B上分别实现4.92倍和2.15倍加速，并在WorldScore、PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标上取得最优生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型能生成高质量的未来帧，但重复的transformer评估使推理速度过慢。现有缓存方法（复用中间特征、选择性更新token、根据局部漂移或短原生空间历史重用并外推去噪输出）存在缺陷：它们无法识别跨跳步累积的近似诱导潜变量转移缺陷，也无法捕捉潜变量演化方向的阶段相关或条件相关变化。

Method: WorldDynCache由两个核心组件构成：(1) 轻量级潜变量转移风险估计器——跟踪近似缺陷对未来影响的累积，并通过精确锚点处观察到的反事实缺陷进行校准；(2) 条件与阶段感知的提升潜变量代理——在无需额外transformer评估的情况下近似潜变量演化过程。

Result: 在HunyuanVoyager-13B和Aether-5B上，WorldDynCache分别实现4.92倍和2.15倍加速，并在WorldScore、PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS等生成质量指标上优于所有对比的缓存方法。

Conclusion: WorldDynCache通过风险控制的潜变量动力学近似，在显著加速扩散世界模型推理的同时保持了最优的生成质量，有效解决了现有缓存方法在跨跳步累积误差和潜变量演化方向变化方面的不足。

Abstract: Diffusion world models generate high-quality futures, but re- peated transformer evaluations make inference prohibitively slow. Existing caches reuse intermediate features, selectively update tokens, or reuse and extrapolate denoising outputs ac- cording to local drift or short native-space histories. These criteria can miss both approximation-induced latent transition defects that accumulate across skipped steps and phase- or condition-dependent changes in the direction of latent evo- lution. We propose WorldDynCache, a risk-controlled latent dynamics approximation framework with two core compo- nents. First, a lightweight latent-transition risk estimator tracks the accumulated future impact of approximation defects and calibrates its predictions against counterfactual defects ob- served at exact anchors. Second, a condition- and phase- aware lifted latent surrogate approximates latent evolution without extra transformer evaluations. On HunyuanVoyager- 13B and Aether-5B, WorldDynCache achieves 4.92 times and 2.15 times speedups, respectively, while attaining the best gen- eration quality among the compared caching methods across WorldScore, PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS.

</details>


### [333] [Beyond Magnitude and Shape: A Direction-Aware Loss for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01857)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CosDir，一种基于余弦相似度的方向感知损失函数，用于时序预测中提升方向准确率，同时保持幅度精度，并进一步提出自适应权重扩展CosDir-UW以免超参数调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风险管理和金融预测等决策驱动应用中，序列的变动方向往往与精确值同等重要，但现有预测损失（如MSE）仅优化点幅度或形状/频率结构，均未显式针对方向。研究发现MSE训练的预测器在小幅度变动的方向上表现不佳，存在学习信号缺失问题。

Method: 提出CosDir损失，通过对预测与目标的差分向量计算余弦相似度来对齐方向；由于余弦相似度具有尺度不变性，在小幅变动上仍能保持方向梯度，从而弥补MSE的不足。CosDir为轻量即插即用项，无需修改网络结构。进一步提出CosDir-UW，在训练过程中自适应学习方向项与幅度项的混合比例，免去逐数据集调超参数的麻烦。

Result: 在超过10万次实验中，CosDir在显著提升方向准确率的同时保持幅度精度不变，且优于多种现有损失函数；CosDir-UW无需超参数即可达到与逐数据集调优权重相当的性能。

Conclusion: CosDir/CosDir-UW是一种简单有效且通用的方向感知损失解决方案，可附加到任意预测主干上，有效解决了MSE在小幅变动方向上失效的问题。

Abstract: The direction of change --- whether a series will move up or down --- is often as important as its exact value in decisiondriven applications such as risk management and financial forecasting. However, most forecasting losses optimize either point magnitude or shape and frequency structure, and none explicitly targets the direction of change. In this paper, we find that MSE-trained forecasters fail on the direction of small moves. To address this, we propose CosDir, a simple yet effective direction-aware loss that aligns the difference vectors of the prediction and the target via cosine similarity. Being scale-invariant, CosDir keeps a directional gradient on small moves, re-injecting learning signal exactly where MSE neglects it. CosDir is a lightweight, plug-in term that attaches to any backbone without architectural modification. Since the best ratio for mixing the directional and magnitude terms differs across datasets, we further propose CosDir-UW, an extension that makes this ratio adaptive by learning it during training, matching a per-dataset tuned weight with no hyperparameter. We conduct over 100K experiments, demonstrating that our method consistently and significantly improves directional accuracy while preserving magnitude accuracy, and that it outperforms various loss functions. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/cosdir.

</details>


### [334] [LAB-Tab: LLM-Augmented Bayesian Network Adaptation for Few-Shot Tabular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01879)
*Zijian Shen,Taijie Chen,Bin Zhou,Ziyang Jiang,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAB-Tab，一种基于LLM增强的贝叶斯网络（BN）自适应框架，用于源域感知的小样本表格数据生成。它先从源数据拟合BN，再用LLM提出目标域可能但源图缺失的BN边以扩展可编辑空间，并通过PPO策略以边级操作（保留、减弱、增强、翻转、停用）校准这些边。在六个跨域分布偏移场景中，LAB-Tab在10%目标数据预算下取得最佳性能，宏观Overall分数相对最强基线降低33.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据生成在目标域数据稀缺时很困难。现有方法要么直接拟合稀疏目标统计导致过拟合偶然模式，要么直接复用源域生成器导致保留目标域不再成立的依赖关系。需要一个能够结合源数据信息同时适应目标域分布偏移的框架。

Method: LAB-Tab分三阶段：首先从源数据拟合贝叶斯网络；然后利用LLM基于语义和弱统计证据提出目标域可能存在的、但源BN图中缺失的边，将其转化为显式结构假设以扩展可编辑边空间；最后通过PPO强化学习策略对增强BN中的边执行边级动作（keep/weaken/strengthen/flip/deactivate），奖励函数结合分布对齐、下游效用和目标相关依赖的保留，对校准后的BN采样生成目标域表格。

Result: 在基于三个US Census (ACS)预测任务构建的六个源-目标分布偏移场景中，LAB-Tab在10%目标数据预算下取得最佳整体性能，在六个单独场景中领先四个，相对最强基线将宏观Overall分数降低33.8%；同时获得最佳宏观JSD、WAPE和UtilityGap指标，并保持有竞争力的特征-标签保真度。

Conclusion: LLM增强的贝叶斯网络自适应框架能有效结合源域结构知识与目标域稀疏信号，通过显式结构假设扩展和PPO边级校准，在小样本跨域表格生成场景中优于现有基线。

Abstract: Tabular data generation supports analysis and decision-making when target-domain data are scarce, yet collecting complete target samples is often costly. A practical but underexplored setting provides only a few target records together with richer source data from a related domain. Existing few-shot tabular generators often either fit sparse target statistics directly, which can overfit incidental patterns, or reuse source-domain generators, which may preserve dependencies that no longer hold in the target domain. To address this problem, we propose LAB-Tab, an LLM-augmented Bayesian network (BN) adaptation framework for source-aware few-shot tabular generation. LAB-Tab first fits a BN from source data and then uses an LLM to propose plausible target-domain BN edges that are absent from the source BN graph. This step converts semantic and weak statistical evidence into explicit structural hypotheses, thereby expanding the editable edge space beyond the source-fitted graph. Because the proposed edges may be noisy and interact with existing dependencies, a PPO policy calibrates edges in the augmented BN through edge-level actions, including keep, weaken, strengthen, flip, and deactivate. The PPO policy is trained with a reward that combines distributional alignment, downstream utility, and preservation of target-relevant dependencies. The adapted BN is then sampled to synthesize target-domain tables. Across six source--target distribution-shift scenarios built from three US Census (ACS) prediction tasks, LAB-Tab achieves the best performance at the 10% target-data budget, leads four of the six individual scenarios, and reduces the macro Overall score by 33.8% relative to the strongest baseline. It also obtains the best macro JSD, WAPE, and UtilityGap while maintaining competitive feature--label preservation.

</details>


### [335] [CARE: A Cascaded Framework for Efficient and Reliable Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01885)
*Zemin Chao,Qianhui Xu,Jianhe Cen,Guangzhi Ge,Xiao Chen,Hoangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE，一种模型无关的级联推理框架，用于时间序列异常检测，通过轻量级预过滤器模型（LPM）快速筛选高置信度正常样本，仅将不确定样本路由至高容量复杂检测模型（CDM），实现2.7倍至4.8倍的推理加速，同时保持检测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列异常检测中虽性能优异，但复杂架构带来显著推理开销。现有方法对所有数据点采用统一推理策略，然而异常本质上是稀缺的，绝大多数时序数据是可预测的正常模式，导致大量不必要的计算浪费。

Method: CARE框架包含两个核心组件：轻量级预过滤器模型（LPM）和高容量复杂检测模型（CDM）。LPM由残差MLP自编码器和正态条件门控机制构成，用于快速过滤高置信度正常样本。框架引入结构注意力模块（Structure Attention）显式捕获通道级异常贡献，并通过置信度引导的选择性路由目标函数优化门控网络，学习可靠的路由决策以减少不必要的CDM调用。

Result: 在八个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，CARE能有效隔离高置信度正常样本。通过仅将不确定样本路由至CDM，与最精确的SOTA方法相比，推理速度提升2.7倍至4.8倍，同时保持具有竞争力的检测质量。

Conclusion: CARE通过级联推理策略和置信度引导的路由机制，在保持检测精度的前提下显著降低推理开销，为时间序列异常检测提供了一种高效且模型无关的加速方案。

Abstract: While deep learning models have achieved state-of-the-art performance in time series anomaly detection, their complex architectures incur substantial inference overhead. Existing methods typically apply a uniform inference strategy across all data points, which is inefficient given that anomalies are inherently scarce and the vast majority of temporal data consists of predictable normal patterns. To mitigate this bottleneck, we propose CARE, a model-agnostic cascaded inference framework that integrates a Lightweight Pre-filter Model (LPM) with an existing high-capacity Complex Detection Model (CDM). The LPM rapidly filters high-confidence normal samples using a Residual MLP AutoEncoder and a Normality-Conditioned Gating mechanism. Crucially, we introduce a Structure Attention module to explicitly capture channel-wise anomaly contributions, and optimize the gating network via a confidence-guided selective routing objective that learns reliable routing decisions to reduce unnecessary CDM invocations. Extensive experiments across eight real-world benchmarks demonstrate that CARE effectively isolates high-confidence normal samples. By routing only uncertain samples to the CDM, our framework achieves $2.7\times$ to $4.8\times$ inference speedup compared to the most accurate SOTA approaches, while still maintaining competitive detection quality.

</details>


### [336] [Understanding and Correcting Low-Frequency Bias in EEG Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01898)
*Junjie Yu,Zihan Deng,Jianyu Zhang,Junrong Mu,Jiahui An,Wenxiao Ma,Ziling Lu,Yue Wang,Yan Zhu,Kexin Lou,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现EEG基础模型普遍存在低频偏置，即扩大预训练数据或模型容量并不能持续提升下游性能。该偏置源于EEG的1/f^α谱结构与神经网络优先学习低频成分的倾向之间的交互，且在掩码自编码器中ℓ2重建目标进一步放大此失衡。作者提出FAME框架，通过在预定义频带内独立标准化重建目标并均衡各频带损失，实现频谱均衡的掩码自编码，在OmniEEG-Bench的41个下游任务中24个达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型的预训练扩展（数据规模、模型容量、预训练目标）无法一致地提升下游性能，作者揭示其根本原因在于表示中的持续低频偏置，并需设计机制来缓解该偏置以学习更具迁移性的EEG表示。

Method: FAME（Frequency-balanced Masked Autoencoding）框架：从掩码EEG输入重建预定义EEG频带中的时频活动，在每个频带内独立标准化重建目标，并为所有频带损失赋予相等权重，从而在EEG频谱上实现均衡的监督信号。

Result: 在OmniEEG-Bench的41个下游任务上，FAME学习到更频谱均衡的表示，并在其中24个任务上达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 均衡的频谱监督对学习可迁移的EEG表示至关重要，FAME通过频率均衡的掩码自编码有效缓解低频偏置，验证了频谱均衡监督的重要性。

Abstract: Increasing EEG pretraining data scale or model capacity does not consistently improve downstream performance. We identify a persistent low-frequency bias in representations learned by diverse EEG foundation models, which remains across dataset scales, model capacities, and pretraining objectives. Our analysis links this bias to the interaction between EEG's $1/f^α$-like spectral structure and neural networks' tendency to preferentially learn low-frequency components. In masked autoencoders, the $\ell_2$ reconstruction objective further amplifies this imbalance: under comparable relative reconstruction errors, high-power low-frequency components contribute disproportionately to the loss. To address this issue, we introduce FAME, a frequency-balanced masked autoencoding framework that reconstructs time--frequency activity in predefined EEG bands from masked EEG inputs. FAME independently standardizes the reconstruction targets within each band and assigns equal weight to all band-specific losses, thereby balancing supervision across the EEG spectrum. Evaluated on 41 downstream tasks in OmniEEG-Bench, FAME learns more spectrally balanced representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance on 24 of them. These results underscore the importance of balanced spectral supervision for learning transferable EEG representations.

</details>


### [337] [Finite-Time Analysis of Discounted Exponential-Utility Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01917)
*Ankur Naskar,Vivek T A,Aditya Kumar,Gugan Thoppe,Prashanth L. A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为折扣指数效用强化学习中的两种无模型不动点算法建立了~O(1/√n)的有限时间收敛率（在异步马尔可夫采样下），并使用无参数步长。这是该领域首个有限时间保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 折扣指数效用提供了风险敏感序列决策的原理性准则，但其非线性结构使强化学习变得复杂。先前工作引入了Bellman兼容替代函数和两种无模型不动点算法，但其主要收敛结果是渐近的，缺乏有限时间分析。

Method: 对于单时间尺度算法：利用算子的有界性、单调性和齐次性获得相对误差动力学的局部伪收缩性质，结合基于Moreau包络的Lyapunov函数和Polyak-Ruppert平均来推导收敛率。对于双时间尺度算法：主要挑战是控制快时间尺度上的跟踪误差。

Result: 在异步马尔可夫采样下，为两种算法建立了~O(1/√n)的有限时间收敛率，且步长参数无需人工调节（参数自由的）。这是折扣指数效用无模型强化学习的首个有限时间保证。

Conclusion: 本文填补了折扣指数效用强化学习中渐近结果与有限时间分析之间的空白，为风险敏感的序贯决策任务提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Discounted exponential utility provides a principled criterion for risk-sensitive sequential decision-making, but its nonlinear structure complicates reinforcement learning. A recent work \citep{thoppe2026reinforcement} addressed this difficulty by introducing a Bellman-compatible surrogate and two model-free fixed-point algorithms for optimizing it over stationary policies. However, their main convergence results are asymptotic. In this work, we establish finite-time rates of $\tilde{O} (1/\sqrt{n})$ for the aforementioned two algorithms under asynchronous Markovian sampling, where $n$ is the iteration index and $\tilde{O}$ hides logarithmic expressions. Importantly, we employ parameter-free choices for the stepsize parameter to derive these rate results. For the algorithmically simpler one-timescale method, the main challenge is that its update equation is not directly aligned with the contraction geometry of its underlying power-law operator. We overcome this mismatch by exploiting the boundedness, monotonicity, and homogeneity of the operator to obtain a local pseudo-contraction property for the relative-error dynamics. We then use a Moreau-envelope-based Lyapunov function and Polyak--Ruppert averaging to obtain the stated convergence rate with parameter-free stepsizes. For the two-timescale method, the main challenge is to control a tracking error on the faster timescale. These results provide the first finite-time guarantees for model-free discounted exponential-utility reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [338] [HarnessCompass: Guiding Automatic Harness Evolution toward Generalizable and Effective Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01918)
*Luan Zhang,Ruochen Zhou,Dandan Song,Zhengyu Chen,Yuhang Tian,Jun Yang,Huipeng Ma,Chenhao Li,Guangyuan Feng,Xudong Li,Yizhou Jin,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessCompass，一个自动harness进化框架，通过约束进化、主动反馈和组件级优化三大核心机制，解决了现有自动harness进化方法过度拟合进化任务、信号单一和组件干扰的问题，在SWE-bench Verified上仅用5轮迭代将Pass@1从54%提升至66%，并展现出优异的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动harness进化方法存在三个关键缺陷：(1)过度拟合进化任务，缺乏对任务无关、可泛化harness变化的约束；(2)仅依赖轨迹衍生的信号，缺乏更丰富的反馈来源；(3)对不同harness组件进行联合优化，导致组件间相互干扰。这些缺陷限制了harness进化的有效性和泛化能力。

Method: HarnessCompass包含三大核心设计：(1)约束进化——在进化过程中施加全局约束，仅允许任务无关的harness修改，确保变化可以泛化到进化任务之外；(2)主动反馈——除轨迹衍生证据外，额外引入agent关于harness使用的第一人称主动反馈，提供更丰富的进化信号；(3)组件级优化——将不同harness组件解耦分别优化后再整合为统一harness，减少跨组件干扰同时保留组件协同效应。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified基准上与GPT-5.4结合，HarnessCompass仅用5次进化迭代就将Pass@1从54%提升到66%，在有效性和进化效率上均优于AHE。此外，进化得到的harness可以有效地迁移到留出任务和其他模型上，展现出比先前自动harness进化方法更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: HarnessCompass通过约束进化、主动反馈和组件级优化三个方面的改进，显著提升了自动harness进化的有效性、效率和泛化性，为LLM agent在可执行环境中的表现优化提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Harness design plays a critical role in agent performance by shaping how large language models (LLMs) perceive, reason over, and act within executable environments. Recent work has proposed automatic harness evolution, which iteratively improves the harness from agent--environment interactions. However, existing methods often overfit to the evolution tasks, rely exclusively on trajectory-derived signals, and optimize harness components jointly, causing interference across components. We propose HarnessCompass, a novel automatic harness evolution framework built around constrained evolution, proactive feedback, and component-wise optimization. HarnessCompass first enforces global constraints on evolution, restricting modifications to task-agnostic harness changes that generalize beyond the evolution tasks. It then augments trajectory-derived evidence with proactive first-person feedback from the agent about harness usage, yielding richer signals for evolution. Finally, it decouples the optimization of different harness components before consolidating them into a unified harness, reducing cross-component interference while preserving component synergy. On SWE-bench Verified with GPT-5.4, HarnessCompass improves Pass@1 from 54\% to 66\% in only 5 evolution iterations, outperforming AHE in both effectiveness and evolution efficiency. In addition, the evolved harness transfers effectively to held-out tasks and other models, demonstrating substantially stronger generalization than prior automatic harness evolution methods.

</details>


### [339] [ChaosProbe: A Neurochaotic Lens on Frozen Transformer Input-Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01968)
*Kunal Kumar Pant,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChaosProbe，一种受神经混沌启发的确定性方法，通过构建冻结Transformer输入嵌入空间的响应式指纹来揭示模型间的结构关系。在80个中性提示和四个预训练模型（GPT-2、DistilGPT2、BERT、RoBERTa）的概念验证研究中，该方法通过皮尔逊相关、斯皮尔曼相关和余弦相似度成功恢复了全部同族最近邻分配和期望的互族对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型通常通过其下游任务表现或生成质量来理解，但冻结的输入嵌入空间本身的内部结构很少被直接探测。作者希望通过受控的确定性探针，在上下文计算和任务适应之前，从响应视角揭示不同模型输入嵌入空间的潜在结构与族间关系。

Method: ChaosProbe对每个提示级嵌入矩阵施加混沌轨迹变换，并汇总其Firing Rate（发射率）和Entropy（熵）通道响应，辅以表示级互补度量，为每个模型生成固定长度的签名。该方法是确定性的神经混沌启发方法，无需训练或任务特定调整。

Result: 在80个中性提示和四个预训练模型的概念验证中，皮尔逊相关、斯皮尔曼相关和余弦相似度均完全恢复了四个同族最近邻分配及两个期望互族对；欧氏距离恢复了四个中的三个分配和两个互族对中的一个。配对自助重采样支持皮尔逊和斯皮尔曼配对的稳定性，签名有效性检查确认结果不受常数或塌缩响应支配。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了依赖于队列的概念验证，表明确定性神经混沌响应签名可以揭示冻结Transformer输入嵌入空间之间的大尺度结构关系，为理解模型内部表示提供了不依赖下游任务的补充视角。

Abstract: Transformer models are most often understood through what they do: their benchmark performance, generation quality, or behavior on downstream tasks. Yet frozen transformer input-embedding spaces may also be examined through their responses to a controlled deterministic probe before contextual computation or task-specific adaptation. Guided by this response-based view, we introduce \emph{ChaosProbe}, a deterministic neurochaos-inspired method for constructing response-based fingerprints of frozen transformer input-embedding spaces. For each prompt-level embedding matrix, ChaosProbe applies a chaotic trajectory-based transformation and summarizes its Firing Rate and Entropy channel responses with complementary representation-level measures, producing a fixed-length signature for each model. In a bounded proof-of-concept study of $80$ neutral prompts and four pretrained models---GPT-2, DistilGPT2, BERT-base-uncased, and RoBERTa-base---Pearson correlation, Spearman correlation, and cosine similarity each recover all four same-family nearest-neighbor assignments and both expected mutual family pairs. Euclidean distance recovers three of the four assignments and one of the two mutual family pairs. Paired bootstrap resampling supports the stability of the Pearson and Spearman pairings over the observed prompt set, and signature-validity checks show that constant or collapsed responses do not dominate the reported fingerprints. These results provide a cohort-dependent proof of concept that deterministic neurochaotic response signatures can expose broad structure among frozen transformer input-embedding spaces.

</details>


### [340] [AOS: Adaptive Optimizer Switching via Training-State Signals for Faster Convergence and Better Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01997)
*Alok Kumar Pandey,Umang Chaturvedi,Aatish Rana,Gopi Krishna Nedanuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AOS-R（基于规则的适应性优化器切换）轻量级控制器，通过监控六种在线梯度空间信号，在训练过程中自动在AdamW、SGD-M和Lion三种优化器之间切换，以适配深度网络优化的不同阶段需求。在CIFAR-100/WRN-28x10上以81 epochs达到78% top-1精度，比AdamW快26%，并在8个模型-数据集基准上实现6/8的最佳精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一优化器无法同时满足深度网络优化不同阶段的需求：自适应方法（如Adam系列）善于处理早期噪声梯度，但在平坦最优点附近会过冲；而带动量的SGD在后期泛化更好，但早期收敛缓慢。需要一个能够随优化景观动态切换优化器的机制。

Method: AOS-R是一个轻量级控制器，监控六个在线梯度空间信号：梯度噪声尺度（GNS）、Hutchinson曲率迹、损失停滞、更新稳定性比率、梯度稳定性指数（GSI）和损失改进比率（LIR）。根据这些信号动态在AdamW、SGD-M和Lion之间切换。切换时通过状态保持的动量转移和400步学习率桥接机制防止精度下降。

Result: 在CIFAR-100/WRN-28x10上，AOS-R以81 epochs达到78% top-1精度，比AdamW（109 epochs）少26%，比SGD-M（143 epochs）少43%，比Lion（96 epochs）少16%。在8个模型-数据集基准上，使用单一共享超参数配置，AOS-R在6/8组合上取得最佳精度，平均+0.4个百分点提升，相对AdamW取得0.80x的收敛加速。

Conclusion: AOS-R表明基于规则的优化器切换策略能够有效结合不同优化器的优势，在保持甚至提升最终精度的同时显著加快收敛速度，且无需针对每个任务调整超参数，是一种实用且高效的优化策略。

Abstract: Single-optimizer training is a poor fit for the distinct phases of deep network optimization: adaptive methods handle noisy early gradients well but overshoot flat minima, while SGD with momentum generalizes better in the late phase but converges slowly early on.
  We introduce AOS-R (Adaptive Optimizer Switching, Rule-Based), a lightweight controller that monitors six online gradient-space signals -- gradient noise scale (GNS), Hutchinson curvature trace, loss stagnation, update stability ratio, gradient stability index (GSI), and loss improvement ratio (LIR) -- and switches among AdamW, SGD-M, and Lion as the optimization landscape evolves. State-preserving momentum transfer and a 400-step learning-rate bridge prevent accuracy degradation at every transition point.
  On CIFAR-100/WRN-28x10, AOS-R reaches 78% top-1 in 81 epochs -- 26% fewer than AdamW (109), 43% fewer than SGD-M (143), and 16% fewer than Lion (96). Across eight model-dataset benchmarks, AOS-R achieves best accuracy on 6 of 8 combinations with a mean +0.4 pp gain and 0.80x convergence speedup over AdamW under a single shared hyperparameter configuration.

</details>


### [341] [DART: Decoded Attention over Recurrent States for Efficient Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02032)
*Yixiao Qian,Song Chen,Pengkai Wang,Jiaxu Liu,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART是一种基于Mamba-2状态空间对偶（SSD）视角的新型混合架构方法，通过从Mamba-2的块扫描中保留块状态贡献作为记忆，解码出token条件的键和值，并执行状态记忆注意力（SMA），同时通过门控残差连接与原生Mamba-2输出融合。DART在保持语言建模质量的同时显著减少了与序列长度相关的推理缓存，并大幅提升了联想记忆和信息检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型架构要么依赖token级注意力记忆（Transformer），要么依赖紧凑的循环状态（SSM/线性注意力），它们通常被分开实例化或仅在层级别交错。作者希望探索是否可以用一个共享的记忆表示同时支持循环压缩和注意力式检索，弥合这两类架构在记忆机制上的鸿沟。

Method: （1）从Mamba-2的SSD视角出发，观察到Mamba-2从状态中解码token条件值但不解码token条件键；（2）DART保留Mamba-2块扫描产生的块状态贡献作为块状态记忆；（3）从这些记忆中解码token条件的键和值，执行状态记忆注意力（SMA）；（4）通过门控残差连接将检索输出与原生Mamba-2输出相结合；（5）复用Mamba-2块扫描并将SMA实现为FlashAttention风格的计算以支持高效训练。

Result: 与匹配的注意力基线相比，DART大幅减少了与序列长度相关的推理缓存（当块大小S=256、状态大小N=128时节省75%）。与Mamba-2相比，DART在保持一般语言建模质量的同时，显著提升了联想记忆和信息检索能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过共享的记忆表示可以同时实现循环压缩与注意力式检索，DART提供了一种在实际的训练/推理效率和能力之间取得更好权衡的混合架构方案，为未来语言模型记忆机制的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Modern language models are built primarily from Transformers, recurrent models, and their hybrid architectures. Transformers rely on token-level attention memories, while recurrent models such as state space models (SSMs) and linear attention maintain compact recurrent states. These architectures are typically instantiated separately or interleaved at the layer level, leaving open whether a shared memory representation can support both recurrent compression and attention-style retrieval. We study this question through the state space duality (SSD) view of Mamba-2, where the SSM state can be interpreted as a compressed associative key--value (KV) cache. We observe that Mamba-2 decodes token-conditioned values from this state but does not decode token-conditioned keys. Based on this observation, we propose DART (Decoded Attention over Recurrent sTates), which retains the chunk state contributions produced by the Mamba-2 chunked scan as chunk state memories, decodes token-conditioned keys and values from these memories, and performs state-memory attention (SMA) over the resulting KV pairs. The retrieved output is then combined with the native Mamba-2 output through a gated residual connection. DART supports practical training by reusing the Mamba-2 chunked scan and implementing SMA as a FlashAttention-style computation. Our analysis and experiments show that DART substantially reduces the length-dependent inference cache compared with a matched attention baseline (e.g., $75\%$ savings when the chunk size is $S=256$ and the state size is $N=128$). Compared with Mamba-2, DART substantially improves associative recall and retrieval while preserving general language-modeling quality.

</details>


### [342] [Upper-Expectile Multi-Step Q-Learning for Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02034)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出期望线n步Q学习（ENQ），用非对称期望线损失替代对称的n步时序差分损失来加速奖励传播，并避免多步回报带来的悲观偏差。理论上证明ENQ算子为γ^n压缩映射，在确定性动态下τ=1时偏差可在最优动作价值函数上消失；在27个操纵与导航任务上仅用单一期望线水平τ=0.8即与LQL竞争，且训练吞吐量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步回报虽能加速离线策略强化学习中的奖励传播，但会将每个决策的评估与其后的次优记录动作耦合，导致随视界增长的悲观偏差。作者旨在设计一种既能利用多步回报的加速效果、又能缓解或消除这种悲观偏差的方法。

Method: 将对称的n步TD损失替换为作用于动作价值误差的非对称期望线损失，引入期望线水平τ作为唯一新增超参数。作者证明了ENQ算子是γ^n压缩映射；在确定性动力学下，τ=1时偏差在最优动作价值函数Q*上的支持集内配对处消失，且对应不动点满足分离-n实例及其下界不等式倍数（用于长视界Q学习LQL）。在随机动力学下，算子偏差具有与视界无关噪声常数的双边界。

Result: 使用单一期望线水平τ=0.8和固定备份视界，在27个操纵与导航任务实例上，ENQ在整体上与LQL竞争，在性能剖析研究中获得更高的训练步吞吐量，并在受控缩放实验中从十评论员集成中获益更多。

Conclusion: ENQ通过非对称期望线损失有效解决了多步回报中悲观偏差的问题，以最小的超参数开销（仅τ）实现了与LQL相当甚至更优的性能，同时具有更高的训练效率和更好的集成扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-step returns accelerate reward propagation in off-policy reinforcement learning, but couple the evaluation of each decision to the suboptimal logged actions that follow it, inducing a pessimistic bias that grows with the horizon. We propose Expectile $n$-step Q-learning (ENQ), which replaces the symmetric $n$-step temporal-difference (TD) loss with an asymmetric expectile loss on the action-value error, with expectile level $τ$ as the only method-specific hyperparameter added beyond $n$-step TD. We prove that the ENQ operator is a $γ^{n}$-contraction. Under deterministic dynamics, at $τ=1$, its bias vanishes at the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ on covered in-support pairs, and the corresponding fixed point satisfies the separation-$n$ instance and its multiples of the lower-bound inequality used by Long-Horizon Q-learning (LQL). Under stochastic dynamics, the operator bias admits two-sided bounds with horizon-independent noise constants. Using a single expectile level $τ=0.8$ and a fixed backup horizon across 27 manipulation and navigation task instances, ENQ is competitive with LQL on aggregate, achieves higher measured training-step throughput in our profiling study, and benefits more from a ten-critic ensemble in a controlled scaling experiment.

</details>


### [343] [Convex Neural Energy Elements: Monolithic Finite-Element Assembly of Geometry-Parameterized Neural Operators with Stability and Error Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02036)
*Hongyue Jiang,Jianjiang Zhan,Chenzhuo Zhang,Fan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"凸神经能量单元"（convex neural energy elements），将神经算子从逐个训练的固定几何单元扩展为可复用、几何参数化的单元库。每个单元导出一个在其边界自由度上架构凸且由几何平滑参数化的标量能量，通过hypernetwork生成半正定二次型实现。该框架保证了组装后的全局系统正定性，并通过正则化零空间原理消除不可约偏差，使神经算子从一次性替代品转变为继承原方法组装保证的可复用单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将神经算子单元方法从固定几何单元扩展到可复用的几何参数化单元库会在结构上失败：通过值回归训练出的场预测算子诱导出的能量其组装Hessian矩阵不定，即使场预测精度达到1%，牛顿法仍收敛到虚假极小值（247%误差）。这暴露了传统神经算子作为"一次性替代品"的结构性局限，缺乏跨几何复用和系统组装的数学保证。

Method: (1) 提出凸神经能量单元：每个单元导出标量能量E(g,U)，在边界自由度U上架构凸、由几何g平滑参数化；(2) 通过hypernetwork生成半正定二次型实现该能量，并预留输入凸修正用于非二次物理；(3) 确立正则化零空间原理——正则化器的零空间必须包含物理零空间，以消除不可约偏差；(4) 证明组装后的单元继承经典保证：奇异单元刚度矩阵可产生正定全局系统；提供条件误差界（能量到解的精度、单元数标度、几何泛化）并逐一实验验证。

Result: 在含椭圆孔的热传导问题上，单个训练单元可组装成2x2至8x8网格及L形布局的未见几何，相对L2误差0.6-1.0%，边界量工作负载下每个几何设置速度快175倍。第二种训练单元类型可与第一种在单一整体组装中自由混合；三维实例在八单元组装上达到0.23%误差。平面应变弹性单元（物理零空间三维）落在解析预测的正则化底线上。

Conclusion: 将能量作为学习对象这一转变，使神经算子从单一用途的替代品变为可复用单元，并继承了其所扩展方法的组装保证。该保证是类型无关和维度无关的，为神经算子方法在有限元框架下的可复用、可组装应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Extending the neural-operator element method from individually trained, fixed-geometry neural elements to a library of reusable, geometry-parameterized element types fails structurally: a field-predicting operator trained by value regression induces an energy whose assembled Hessian is indefinite, and Newton converges to spurious minima (247% error) even with 1%-accurate field predictions. We introduce convex neural energy elements: each element exports a scalar energy E(g,U), architecturally convex in its boundary degrees of freedom U and smoothly parameterized by its geometry g, realized as a hypernetwork-generated positive-semidefinite quadratic form (an input-convex correction is reserved for non-quadratic physics). A regularization-nullspace principle--the regularizer's nullspace must contain the physics nullspace--removes an otherwise irreducible bias, and assembled elements inherit the classical guarantee that singular element stiffnesses yield a positive-definite global system. We prove conditional error bounds (energy-to-solution accuracy, element-count scaling, geometry generalization) and verify each experimentally. On heat conduction with elliptic holes, one trained element assembles into 2x2 to 8x8 grids and an L-shaped layout of unseen geometries at 0.6-1.0% relative L2 error, with 175x faster per-geometry setup for boundary-quantity workloads. A second trained element type mixes freely with the first in one monolithic assembly, and a three-dimensional instantiation reaches 0.23% on eight-element assemblies--the guarantees are type- and dimension-agnostic. A plane-strain elasticity element, whose physics nullspace is three-dimensional, lands on the analytically predicted regularization floors. Making the energy the learned object turns neural operators from single-use surrogates into reusable elements that inherit the assembly guarantees of the method they extend.

</details>


### [344] [Secrets Everywhere: Auditing Memorization in Mobility Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02052)
*Anne Josiane Kouam,Hristo Boyadzhiev,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统审计了移动预测模型中的记忆化（memorization）问题，提出了一个多粒度框架来量化个体位置、锚点对和子轨迹段三个层面的记忆化风险，并通过用户基准参考集评估模型泄露训练数据隐私的程度。实验发现记忆化普遍存在，与用户行为规律性相关，并在推理时增加数据提取风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动预测模型被广泛应用于城市分析、导航和个性化服务中，但人们对它们从训练数据中记忆并暴露敏感用户轨迹的潜力知之甚少。虽然记忆化在语言模型中已有大量研究，但移动预测面临独特挑战：训练序列在不同空间和时间尺度上编码人类行为，隐私风险在不同粒度上表现不同，而目前缺乏对移动预测模型记忆化风险的系统性量化评估。

Method: 本文首次对移动预测模型进行系统性记忆化审计。具体方法包括：(1) 针对缺乏随机空间（randomness space）、轨迹多尺度结构、用户行为多样性等挑战，提出一个在不同粒度（个体位置、锚点对、子轨迹段）量化移动记忆化的框架；(2) 开发用户锚定的参考集（user-grounded reference sets）来评估模型倾向于输出训练数据而非现实替代方案的程度；(3) 在多个模型和数据集上进行大规模评估。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的评估揭示了普遍存在的记忆化模式。这些模式与用户的行为规律性相关，并增加了在推理时提取数据的风险，说明了移动预测模型存在系统性的隐私泄露隐患。

Conclusion: 研究结果呼吁对移动预测模型进行强制性的隐私审计（mandatory privacy auditing），以保障用户在移动轨迹数据中的隐私安全。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction models, which forecast the next location in a user's trajectory, are increasingly deployed in urban analytics, navigation, and personalized services. Yet, little is known about their potential to memorize and expose sensitive user trajectories from training data. While memorization has been extensively studied in language models, mobility prediction poses unique challenges: training sequences encode human behavior at various spatial and temporal scales, creating privacy risks at different granularities.
  In this paper, we conduct the first systematic audit of memorization in mobility prediction models.
